
## 1. Motivations

*This section is non-normative.*

Web Applications traditionally assume that the network is reachable.
This assumption pervades the platform. HTML documents are loaded over
HTTP and traditionally fetch all of their sub-resources via subsequent
HTTP requests. This places web content at a disadvantage versus other
technology stacks.

The [service worker](#dfn-service-worker) is designed first to redress this balance by providing
a Web Worker context, which can be started by a runtime when navigations
are about to occur. This event-driven worker is registered against an
origin and a path (or pattern), meaning it can be consulted when
navigations occur to that location. Events that correspond to network
requests are dispatched to the worker and the responses generated by the
worker may override default network stack behavior. This puts the
[service worker](#dfn-service-worker), conceptually, between the network and a document
renderer, allowing the [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker) to provide content for documents, even while offline.

Web developers familiar with previous attempts to solve the offline
problem have reported a deficit of flexibility in those solutions. As a
result, the [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker) is highly procedural, providing a maximum of
flexibility at the price of additional complexity for developers. Part
of this complexity arises from the need to keep [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker) responsive in the face of a single-threaded execution
model. As a result, APIs exposed by [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker) are almost entirely asynchronous, a pattern familiar in
other JavaScript contexts but accentuated here by the need to avoid
blocking document and resource loading.

Developers using the [HTML5 Application
Cache](https://www.w3.org/TR/2014/REC-html5-20141028/browsers.html#appcache)
have also [reported that several
attributes](https://alistapart.com/article/application-cache-is-a-douchebag/)
of the design contribute to [unrecoverable
errors](https://alistapart.com/article/application-cache-is-a-douchebag/#section6).
A key design principle of the [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker) is that errors should *always* be recoverable. Many
details of the update process of [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker) are designed to avoid these hazards.

[Service workers](#dfn-service-worker) are started and kept alive by their relationship to
events, not documents. This design borrows heavily from developer and
vendor experience with [shared
workers](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#shared-workers-and-the-sharedworker-interface)
and [Chrome Background
Pages](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/extensions/mv2/background-pages).
A key lesson from these systems is the necessity to time-limit the
execution of background processing contexts, both to conserve resources
and to ensure that background context loss and restart is top-of-mind
for developers. As a result, [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker) bear more than a passing resemblance to [Chrome Event
Pages](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/apps/event_pages), the
successor to Background Pages. [Service
workers](#dfn-service-worker) may be started by user agents *without an attached
document* and may be killed by the user agent at nearly any time.
Conceptually, [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker) can be thought of as Shared Workers that can start,
process events, and die without ever handling messages from documents.
Developers are advised to keep in mind that [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker) may be started and killed many times a second.

[Service workers](#dfn-service-worker) are generic, event-driven, time-limited script contexts
that run at an origin. These properties make them natural endpoints for
a range of runtime services that may outlive the context of a particular
document, e.g. handling push notifications, background data
synchronization, responding to resource requests from other origins, or
receiving centralized updates to expensive-to-calculate data (e.g.,
geolocation or gyroscope).

## 2. Model

### 2.1. Service Worker

A [service worker] is a type of [web
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#workers). A [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker) executes in the registering [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client)'s
[origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin).

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker) has an associated [state], which is one of
\"`parsed`\", \"`installing`\", \"`installed`\", \"`activating`\",
\"`activated`\", and \"`redundant`\". It is initially \"`parsed`\".

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker) has an associated [script url] (a
[URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url)).

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker) has an associated [type] which is either
\"`classic`\" or \"`module`\". Unless stated otherwise, it is
\"`classic`\".

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker) has an associated [containing service worker
registration] (a [service
worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration)), which contains itself.

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker) has an associated [global
object] (a
[`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`](#serviceworkerglobalscope) object or null).

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker) has an associated [script
resource] (a
[script](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-script)), which represents its own script resource. It is
initially set to null.

A [script resource](#dfn-script-resource) has an associated [has ever been evaluated
flag]. It is initially
unset.

A [script resource](#dfn-script-resource) has an associated [policy
container] (a [policy
container](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#policy-container)). It is initially a new policy container.

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker) has an associated [script resource
map] which is an [ordered
map](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-map) where the keys are
[URLs](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url) and the values are
[responses](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response).

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker) has an associated [set of used
scripts] (a
[set](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-set)) whose
[item](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-item) is a
[URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url). It is initially a new
[set](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-set).

 The [set of used
scripts](#dfn-set-of-used-scripts) is only used to prune unused resources from a new
worker's map after installation, that were populated based on the old
worker's map during the update check.

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker) has an associated [skip waiting
flag]. Unless stated otherwise it is unset.

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker) has an associated [classic scripts imported
flag]. It is initially
unset.

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker) has an associated [set of event types to
handle] (a
[set](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-set)) whose
[item](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-item) is an [event
listener](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-listener)'s event type. It is initially a new
[set](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-set).

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker) has an associated [set of extended
events] (a
[set](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-set)) whose
[item](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-item) is an
[`ExtendableEvent`](#extendableevent). It is initially a new
[set](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-set).

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker) has an associated [start
status] which can be null
or a
[Completion](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-completion-record-specification-type). It is initially null.

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker) has an associated [all fetch listeners are empty
flag]. It is
initially unset.

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker) has an associated [list of router
rules] (a
[list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list) of
[`RouterRule`](#dictdef-routerrule)s). It is initially an empty
[list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list).

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker) is said to be [running] if its
[event
loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop) is running.

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker) has an associated [\[\[service worker
queue\]\]] (a [parallel
queue](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#parallel-queue)).

#### 2.1.1. Lifetime

The lifetime of a [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker) is tied to the execution lifetime of events and not
references held by [service worker
clients](#dfn-service-worker-client) to the
[`ServiceWorker`](#serviceworker) object.

A user agent *may*
[terminate](#terminate-service-worker) [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker) at any time it:

- Has no event to handle.

- Detects abnormal operation: such as infinite loops and tasks exceeding
 imposed time limits (if any) while handling the events.

#### 2.1.2. Events

The Service Workers specification defines [service worker
events] (each of which is an
[event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event)) that include (see the
[list](#execution-context-events)):

- [Lifecycle events]:
 [`install`](#service-worker-global-scope-install-event) and
 [`activate`](#service-worker-global-scope-activate-event).

- [Functional events]:
 [`fetch`](#service-worker-global-scope-fetch-event) and the
 [events](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event) defined by other specifications that
 [extend](#extensibility) the Service Workers specification. (See the
 [list](#execution-context-events).)

- [`message`](#eventdef-serviceworkerglobalscope-message) and
 [`messageerror`](#eventdef-serviceworkerglobalscope-messageerror).

### 2.2. Service Worker Timing

Service workers mark certain points in time that are later exposed by
the [navigation
timing](https://w3c.github.io/navigation-timing/#dom-performancenavigationtiming) API and [resource
timing](https://w3c.github.io/resource-timing/#dom-performanceresourcetiming) API.

A [service worker timing info] is a
[struct](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct). It has the following
[items](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct-item):

[start time]

: A
 [`DOMHighResTimeStamp`](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp), initially 0.

[fetch event dispatch time]

: A
 [`DOMHighResTimeStamp`](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp), initially 0.

[worker router evaluation start]

: A
 [`DOMHighResTimeStamp`](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp), initially 0.

[worker cache lookup start]

: A
 [`DOMHighResTimeStamp`](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp), initially 0.

[worker matched router source]

: A
 [`DOMString`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMString), initially an empty string.

[worker final router source]

: A
 [`DOMString`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMString), initially an empty string.

### 2.3. Service Worker Registration

A [service worker registration] is a tuple of a [scope
url](#dfn-scope-url), a [storage
key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#storage-key), and a set of [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker), an [installing
worker](#dfn-installing-worker), a [waiting
worker](#dfn-waiting-worker), and an [active
worker](#dfn-active-worker).
A user agent *may* enable many [service worker
registrations](#dfn-service-worker-registration) at a single origin so long as the [scope
url](#dfn-scope-url) of the
[service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration) differs. A [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration) of an identical [scope
url](#dfn-scope-url) when one
already exists in the user agent causes the existing [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration) to be replaced.

A [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration) has an associated [storage
key] (a
[storage
key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#storage-key)).

A [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration) has an associated [scope url] (a
[URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url)).

A [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration) has an associated [installing
worker] (a
[service worker](#dfn-service-worker) or null) whose [state](#dfn-state) is \"`installing`\". It is initially set to null.

A [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration) has an associated [waiting worker] (a [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker) or null) whose [state](#dfn-state) is \"`installed`\". It is initially set to null.

A [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration) has an associated [active worker] (a [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker) or null) whose [state](#dfn-state) is either \"`activating`\" or \"`activated`\". It is
initially set to null.

A [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration) has an associated [last update check
time]. It is
initially set to null.

A [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration) is said to be
[stale] if the
registration's [last update check
time](#dfn-last-update-check-time) is non-null and the time difference in seconds
calculated by the current time minus the registration's [last update
check
time](#dfn-last-update-check-time) is greater than 86400.

A [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration) has an associated [update via cache
mode], which
is \"`imports`\", \"`all`\", or \"`none`\". It is initially set to
\"`imports`\".

A [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration) has one or more [task
queues] that
back up the
[tasks](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-task) from its [active
worker](#dfn-active-worker)'s [event
loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop)'s corresponding [task
queues](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#task-queue). (The target task sources for this back up operation
are the [handle fetch task
source](#dfn-handle-fetch-task-source) and the [handle functional event task
source](#dfn-handle-functional-event-task-source).) The user agent dumps the [active
worker](#dfn-active-worker)'s
[tasks](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-task) to the [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration)'s [task
queues](#dfn-service-worker-registration-task-queue) when the [active
worker](#dfn-active-worker)
is
[terminated](#terminate-service-worker) and [re-queues those
tasks](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) to the [active
worker](#dfn-active-worker)'s [event
loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop)'s corresponding [task
queues](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#task-queue) when the [active
worker](#dfn-active-worker)
spins off. Unlike the [task
queues](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#task-queue) owned by [event
loops](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop), the [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration)'s [task
queues](#dfn-service-worker-registration-task-queue) are not processed by any [event
loops](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop) in and of itself.

A [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration) has an associated
[[`NavigationPreloadManager`](#navigationpreloadmanager)] object.

A [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration) has an associated [navigation preload enabled
flag]. It is initially unset.

A [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration) has an associated [navigation preload header
value], which is a [byte
sequence](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-sequence). It is initially set to \``true`\`.

A [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration) is said to be
[unregistered] if [registration
map](#dfn-scope-to-registration-map)\[this [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration)'s ([storage
key](#service-worker-registration-storage-key),
[serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer) [scope url](#dfn-scope-url))\] is not this [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration).

#### 2.3.1. Lifetime

A user agent *must* persistently keep a list of
[registered](#register) [service
worker
registrations](#dfn-service-worker-registration) unless otherwise they are explicitly
[unregistered](#dfn-service-worker-registration-unregistered). A user agent has a [registration
map](#dfn-scope-to-registration-map) that stores the entries of the tuple of [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration)'s ([storage
key](#service-worker-registration-storage-key),
[serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer) [scope url](#dfn-scope-url)) and the corresponding [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration). The lifetime of [service worker
registrations](#dfn-service-worker-registration) is beyond that of the
[`ServiceWorkerRegistration`](#serviceworkerregistration) objects which represent them within the lifetime of
their corresponding [service worker
clients](#dfn-service-worker-client).

### 2.4. Service Worker Client

A [service worker client] is an
[environment](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment).

A [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client) has an associated [discarded
flag]. It is
initially unset.

Each [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client) has the following [environment discarding
steps](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-discarding-steps):

1. Set `client`'s [discarded
 flag](#service-worker-client-discarded-flag).

 Implementations can discard clients whose [discarded
flag](#service-worker-client-discarded-flag) is set.

A [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client) has an algorithm defined as the
[origin] that returns
the [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client)'s
[origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin) if the [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client) is an [environment settings
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object), and the [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client)'s [creation
URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-creation-url)'s
[origin](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-origin) otherwise.

A [window client] is a [service
worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client) whose [global
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-global) is a
[`Window`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#window) object.

A [dedicated worker client]
is a [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client) whose [global
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-global) is a
[`DedicatedWorkerGlobalScope`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#dedicatedworkerglobalscope) object.

A [shared worker client] is a [service
worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client) whose [global
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-global) is a
[`SharedWorkerGlobalScope`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#sharedworkerglobalscope) object.

A [worker client] is either a
[dedicated worker
client](#dfn-dedicatedworker-client) or a [shared worker
client](#dfn-sharedworker-client).

### 2.5. Control and Use

A [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client) has an [active service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker) that serves its own loading and its subresources. When
a [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client) has a non-null [active service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker), it is said to be [controlled] by that [active service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker). When a [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client) is [controlled](#dfn-control) by a [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker), it is said that the [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client) is [using] the [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker)'s [containing service worker
registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration). A [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client)'s [active service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker) is determined as explained in the following
subsections.

*The rest of the section is non-normative.*

The behavior in this section is not
fully specified yet and will be specified in [HTML
Standard](https://html.spec.whatwg.org). The work is tracked by the
[issue](https://github.com/w3c/ServiceWorker/issues/765) and the [pull
request](https://github.com/whatwg/html/pull/2809).

#### 2.5.1. The window client case

A [window client](#dfn-window-client) is
[created](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/#set-up-a-window-environment-settings-object)
when a [browsing
context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#browsing-context) is
[created](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/#creating-a-new-browsing-context)
and when it
[navigates](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsing-the-web.html#navigate).

When a [window client](#dfn-window-client) is
[created](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/#set-up-a-window-environment-settings-object)
in the process of a [browsing
context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#browsing-context)
[creation](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/#creating-a-new-browsing-context):

If the [browsing
context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#browsing-context)'s initial [active
document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document)'s
[origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin) is an [opaque
origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin-opaque), the [window
client](#dfn-window-client)'s [active service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker) is set to null. Otherwise, it is set to the creator
[document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-document)'s [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client)'s [active service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker).

When a [window client](#dfn-window-client) is
[created](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/#set-up-a-window-environment-settings-object)
in the process of the [browsing
context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#browsing-context)'s
[navigation](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsing-the-web.html#navigate):

If the
[fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch) is routed through [HTTP
fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-http-fetch), the [window
client](#dfn-window-client)'s [active service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker) is set to the result of the [service worker
registration
matching](#match-service-worker-registration). Otherwise, if the created
[document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-document)'s
[origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin) is an [opaque
origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin-opaque) or not the
[same](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#same-origin) as its creator
[document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-document)'s
[origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin), the [window
client](#dfn-window-client)'s [active service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker) is set to null. Otherwise, it is set to the creator
[document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-document)'s [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client)'s [active service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker).

 For an initial replacement
[navigation](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsing-the-web.html#navigate), the initial [window
client](#dfn-window-client)
that was
[created](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/#set-up-a-window-environment-settings-object)
when the [browsing
context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#browsing-context) was
[created](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/#creating-a-new-browsing-context)
is reused, but the [active service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker) is determined by the same behavior as above.

[Sandboxed](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/iframe-embed-object.html#attr-iframe-sandbox)
[`iframe`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/iframe-embed-object.html#the-iframe-element)s without the sandboxing directives,
`allow-same-origin` and `allow-scripts`, result in having the [active
service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker) value of null as their
[origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin) is an [opaque
origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin-opaque).

#### 2.5.2. The worker client case

A [worker client](#dfn-worker-client) is
[created](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/#set-up-a-worker-environment-settings-object)
when the user agent [runs a
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#run-a-worker).

When the [worker client](#dfn-worker-client) is created:

When the
[fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch) is routed through [HTTP
fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-http-fetch), the [worker
client](#dfn-worker-client)'s [active service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker) is set to the result of the [service worker
registration
matching](#match-service-worker-registration). Otherwise, if the [worker
client](#dfn-worker-client)'s
[origin](#service-worker-client-origin) is an [opaque
origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin-opaque), or the
[request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request)'s
[URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url) is a [blob
URL](https://w3c.github.io/FileAPI/#blob-url) and the [worker
client](#dfn-worker-client)'s
[origin](#service-worker-client-origin) is not the
[same](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#same-origin) as the
[origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin) of the last
[item](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-item) in the [worker
client](#dfn-worker-client)'s [global
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object)'s [owner
set](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#concept-WorkerGlobalScope-owner-set), the [worker
client](#dfn-worker-client)'s [active service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker) is set to null. Otherwise, it is set to the [active
service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker) of the [environment settings
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object) of the last
[item](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-item) in the [worker
client](#dfn-worker-client)'s [global
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object)'s [owner
set](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#concept-WorkerGlobalScope-owner-set).

 [Window
clients](#dfn-window-client) and [worker
clients](#dfn-worker-client) with a [data:
URL](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc2397#section-2) result in
having the [active service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker) value of null as their
[origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin) is an [opaque
origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin-opaque). [Window
clients](#dfn-window-client) and [worker
clients](#dfn-worker-client) with a [blob
URL](https://w3c.github.io/FileAPI/#blob-url) can inherit the [active service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker) of their creator
[document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-document) or owner, but if the
[request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request)'s
[origin](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-origin) is not the
[same](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#same-origin) as the
[origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin) of their creator
[document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-document) or owner, the [active service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker) is set to null.

### 2.6. Task Sources

The following additional [task
sources](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#task-source) are used by [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker).

The [handle fetch task source]

: This [task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#task-source) is used for
 [dispatching](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-dispatch)
 [`fetch`](#service-worker-global-scope-fetch-event) events to [service
 workers](#dfn-service-worker).

The [handle functional event task source]

: This [task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#task-source) is used for features that
 [dispatch](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-dispatch) other [functional
 events](#dfn-functional-events), e.g.
 [`push`](https://w3c.github.io/push-api/#pushevent-interface) events, to [service
 workers](#dfn-service-worker).

 A user agent may use a separate task source for
 each functional event type in order to avoid a head-of-line blocking
 phenomenon for certain functional events.

### 2.7. User Agent Shutdown

A user agent *must* maintain the state of its stored [service worker
registrations](#dfn-service-worker-registration) across restarts with the following rules:

- An [installing
 worker](#dfn-installing-worker) does not persist but is discarded. If the [installing
 worker](#dfn-installing-worker) was the only [service
 worker](#dfn-service-worker) for the [service worker
 registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration), the [service worker
 registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration) is discarded.

- A [waiting worker](#dfn-waiting-worker) promotes to an [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker).

To attain this, the user agent *must* invoke [Handle User Agent
Shutdown](#handle-user-agent-shutdown) when it terminates.

## 3. Client Context

Bootstrapping with a service worker:

``` highlight
// scope defaults to the path the script sits in
// "/" in this example
navigator.serviceWorker.register("/serviceworker.js").then(registration => {
 console.log("success!");
 if (registration.installing) {
 registration.installing.postMessage("Howdy from your installing page.");
 }
}, err => {
 console.error("Installing the worker failed!", err);
});
```

### 3.1. [`ServiceWorker`]
```
[SecureContext, Exposed=(Window,Worker)]
interface ServiceWorker : EventTarget {
 readonly attribute USVString scriptURL;
 readonly attribute ServiceWorkerState state;
 undefined postMessage(any message, sequence<object> transfer);
 undefined postMessage(any message, optional StructuredSerializeOptions options = );

 // event
 attribute EventHandler onstatechange;
};
ServiceWorker includes AbstractWorker;

enum ServiceWorkerState {
 "parsed",
 "installing",
 "installed",
 "activating",
 "activated",
 "redundant"
};
```

A [`ServiceWorker`](#serviceworker) object represents a [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker). Each
[`ServiceWorker`](#serviceworker) object is associated with a [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker). Multiple separate objects implementing the
[`ServiceWorker`](#serviceworker) interface across documents and workers can all be
associated with the same [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker) simultaneously.

A [`ServiceWorker`](#serviceworker) object has an associated
[`ServiceWorkerState`](#enumdef-serviceworkerstate) object which is itself associated with [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker)'s [state](#dfn-state).

#### 3.1.1. Getting [`ServiceWorker` instances]
An [environment settings
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object) has a [service worker object
map], a
[map](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-map) where the
[keys](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-getting-the-keys) are [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker) and the
[values](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-getting-the-values) are
[`ServiceWorker`](#serviceworker) objects.

To [get the service worker object] representing `serviceWorker` (a [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker)) in `environment` (an [environment settings
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object)), run these steps:

1. Let `objectMap` be `environment`'s [service
 worker object
 map](#environment-settings-object-service-worker-object-map).

2. If `objectMap`\[`serviceWorker`\] does not
 [exist](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), then:

 1. Let `serviceWorkerObj` be a new
 [`ServiceWorker`](#serviceworker) in `environment`'s
 [Realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object's-realm), and associate it with
 `serviceWorker`.

 2. Set `serviceWorkerObj`'s
 [`state`](#dom-serviceworker-state) to `serviceWorker`'s
 [state](#dfn-state).

 3. Set `objectMap`\[`serviceWorker`\] to
 `serviceWorkerObj`.

3. Return `objectMap`\[`serviceWorker`\].

#### 3.1.2. [`scriptURL`]
The [`scriptURL`] getter
steps are to return the [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker)'s
[serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer) [script url](#dfn-script-url).

For example, consider a document
created by a navigation to `https://example.com/app.html` which
[matches](#match-service-worker-registration) via the following registration call which has been
previously executed:

``` highlight
// Script on the page https://example.com/app.html
navigator.serviceWorker.register("/service_worker.js");
```

The value of `navigator.serviceWorker.controller.scriptURL` will be
\"`https://example.com/service_worker.js`\".

#### 3.1.3. [`state`]
The [`state`] attribute *must*
return the value (in
[`ServiceWorkerState`](#enumdef-serviceworkerstate) enumeration) to which it was last set.

#### 3.1.4. [`postMessage(message, transfer)`]
The
[`postMessage(``message``, ``transfer``)`] method steps are:

1. Let `options` be «\[ \"transfer\" → `transfer`
 \]».

2. Invoke
 [`postMessage(message, options)`](#dom-serviceworker-postmessage-message-options) with `message` and `options`
 as the arguments.

#### 3.1.5. [`postMessage(message, options)`]
The
[`postMessage(``message``, ``options``)`]
method steps are:

1. Let `serviceWorker` be the [service
 worker](#dfn-service-worker) represented by
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this).

2. Let `incumbentSettings` be the [incumbent settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#incumbent-settings-object).

3. Let `incumbentGlobal` be `incumbentSettings`'s
 [global
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-global).

4. Let `serializeWithTransferResult` be
 [StructuredSerializeWithTransfer](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/structured-data.html#structuredserializewithtransfer)(`message`,
 `options`\[\"[`transfer`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/web-messaging.html#dom-structuredserializeoptions-transfer)\"\]). Rethrow any exceptions.

5. If the result of running the [Should Skip
 Event](#should-skip-event) algorithm with \"message\" and
 `serviceWorker` is true, then return.

6. Run these substeps [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel):

 1. If the result of running the [Run Service
 Worker](#run-service-worker) algorithm with `serviceWorker` is
 *failure*, then return.

 2. [Queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) on the [DOM manipulation task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source) to run the following steps:

 1. Let `source` be determined by switching on the
 type of `incumbentGlobal`:

 [`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`](#serviceworkerglobalscope)
 : The result of [getting the service worker
 object](#get-the-service-worker-object) that represents
 `incumbentGlobal`'s [service
 worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker) in the [relevant settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object) of `serviceWorker`'s [global
 object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object).

 [`Window`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#window)
 : a new
 [`WindowClient`](#windowclient) object that represents
 `incumbentGlobal`'s [relevant settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object).

 Otherwise
 : a new [`Client`](#client) object that represents
 `incumbentGlobal`'s associated worker

 2. Let `origin` be the
 [serialization](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#ascii-serialisation-of-an-origin) of `incumbentSettings`'s
 [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin).

 3. Let `destination` be the
 [`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`](#serviceworkerglobalscope) object associated with
 `serviceWorker`.

 4. Let `deserializeRecord` be
 [StructuredDeserializeWithTransfer](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/structured-data.html#structureddeserializewithtransfer)(`serializeWithTransferResult`,
 `destination`'s
 [Realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-global-object-realm)).

 If this throws an exception, let `e` be the
 result of [creating an
 event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-create) named
 [`messageerror`](#eventdef-serviceworkerglobalscope-messageerror), using
 [`ExtendableMessageEvent`](#extendablemessageevent), with the
 [`origin`](#dom-extendablemessageevent-origin) attribute initialized to
 `origin` and the
 [`source`](#dom-extendablemessageevent-source) attribute initialized to
 `source`.

 5. Else:

 1. Let `messageClone` be
 `deserializeRecord`.\[\[Deserialized\]\].

 2. Let `newPorts` be a new [frozen
 array](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-frozen-array-type) consisting of all
 [`MessagePort`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/web-messaging.html#messageport) objects in
 `deserializeRecord`.\[\[TransferredValues\]\],
 if any, maintaining their relative order.

 3. Let `e` be the result of [creating an
 event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-create) named
 [`message`](#eventdef-serviceworkerglobalscope-message), using
 [`ExtendableMessageEvent`](#extendablemessageevent), with the
 [`origin`](#dom-extendablemessageevent-origin) attribute initialized to
 `origin`, the
 [`source`](#dom-extendablemessageevent-source) attribute initialized to
 `source`, the
 [`data`](#dom-extendablemessageevent-data) attribute initialized to
 `messageClone`, and the
 [`ports`](#dom-extendablemessageevent-ports) attribute initialized to
 `newPorts`.

 6. [Dispatch](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-dispatch) `e` at `destination`.

 7. Invoke [Update Service Worker Extended Events
 Set](#update-service-worker-extended-events-set) with `serviceWorker` and
 `e`.

#### 3.1.6. Event handler

The following is the [event
handler](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handlers) (and its corresponding [event handler event
type](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handler-event-type)) that *must* be supported, as [event handler IDL
attributes](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handler-idl-attributes), by all objects implementing
[`ServiceWorker`](#serviceworker) interface:

[event
handler](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handlers)

[event handler event
type](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handler-event-type)

[`onstatechange`]

[`statechange`](#eventdef-serviceworker-statechange)

### 3.2. [`ServiceWorkerRegistration`]
```
[SecureContext, Exposed=(Window,Worker)]
interface ServiceWorkerRegistration : EventTarget {
 readonly attribute ServiceWorker? installing;
 readonly attribute ServiceWorker? waiting;
 readonly attribute ServiceWorker? active;
 [SameObject] readonly attribute NavigationPreloadManager navigationPreload;

 readonly attribute USVString scope;
 readonly attribute ServiceWorkerUpdateViaCache updateViaCache;

 [NewObject] Promise<ServiceWorkerRegistration> update();
 [NewObject] Promise<boolean> unregister();

 // event
 attribute EventHandler onupdatefound;
};

enum ServiceWorkerUpdateViaCache {
 "imports",
 "all",
 "none"
};
```

A
[`ServiceWorkerRegistration`](#serviceworkerregistration) has a [service worker
registration] (a [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration)).

#### 3.2.1. Getting [`ServiceWorkerRegistration` instances]
An [environment settings
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object) has a [service worker registration object
map], a
[map](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-map) where the
[keys](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-getting-the-keys) are [service worker
registrations](#dfn-service-worker-registration) and the
[values](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-getting-the-values) are
[`ServiceWorkerRegistration`](#serviceworkerregistration) objects.

To [get the service worker registration
object] representing `registration` (a [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration)) in `environment` (an [environment settings
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object)), run these steps:

1. Let `objectMap` be `environment`'s [service
 worker registration object
 map](#environment-settings-object-service-worker-registration-object-map).

2. If `objectMap`\[`registration`\] does not
 [exist](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), then:

 1. Let `registrationObject` be a new
 [`ServiceWorkerRegistration`](#serviceworkerregistration) in `environment`'s
 [Realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object's-realm).

 2. Set `registrationObject`'s [service worker
 registration](#serviceworkerregistration-service-worker-registration) to `registration`.

 3. Set `registrationObject`'s
 [`installing`](#dom-serviceworkerregistration-installing) attribute to null.

 4. Set `registrationObject`'s
 [`waiting`](#dom-serviceworkerregistration-waiting) attribute to null.

 5. Set `registrationObject`'s
 [`active`](#dom-serviceworkerregistration-active) attribute to null.

 6. If `registration`'s [installing
 worker](#dfn-installing-worker) is not null, then set
 `registrationObject`'s
 [`installing`](#dom-serviceworkerregistration-installing) attribute to the result of [getting the service
 worker
 object](#get-the-service-worker-object) that represents `registration`'s
 [installing
 worker](#dfn-installing-worker) in `environment`.

 7. If `registration`'s [waiting
 worker](#dfn-waiting-worker) is not null, then set
 `registrationObject`'s
 [`waiting`](#dom-serviceworkerregistration-waiting) attribute to the result of [getting the service
 worker
 object](#get-the-service-worker-object) that represents `registration`'s
 [waiting
 worker](#dfn-waiting-worker) in `environment`.

 8. If `registration`'s [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker) is not null, then set
 `registrationObject`'s
 [`active`](#dom-serviceworkerregistration-active) attribute to the result of [getting the service
 worker
 object](#get-the-service-worker-object) that represents `registration`'s
 [active worker](#dfn-active-worker) in `environment`.

 9. Set `objectMap`\[`registration`\] to
 `registrationObject`.

3. Return `objectMap`\[`registration`\].

#### 3.2.2. [`installing`]
[`installing`] attribute *must* return the value to
which it was last set.

 Within a
[Realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object's-realm), there is only one
[`ServiceWorker`](#serviceworker) object per associated [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker).

#### 3.2.3. [`waiting`]
[`waiting`] attribute *must* return the value to which it was last set.

 Within a
[Realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object's-realm), there is only one
[`ServiceWorker`](#serviceworker) object per associated [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker).

#### 3.2.4. [`active`]
[`active`] attribute *must* return the value to which it was last set.

 Within a
[Realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object's-realm), there is only one
[`ServiceWorker`](#serviceworker) object per associated [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker).

#### 3.2.5. [`navigationPreload`]
The
[`navigationPreload`] getter steps are to return the [service
worker
registration](#serviceworkerregistration-service-worker-registration)'s
[`NavigationPreloadManager`](#navigationpreloadmanager) object.

#### 3.2.6. [`scope`]
The [`scope`] getter steps are to return the [service worker
registration](#serviceworkerregistration-service-worker-registration)'s
[serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer) [scope url](#dfn-scope-url).

In the example in [§ 3.1.2
scriptURL](#service-worker-url), the value of `registration.scope`,
obtained from
`navigator.serviceWorker.ready.then(registration => console.log(registration.scope))`
for example, will be \"`https://example.com/`\".

#### 3.2.7. [`updateViaCache`]
The [`updateViaCache`] getter steps are to return the [service
worker
registration](#serviceworkerregistration-service-worker-registration)'s [update via cache
mode](#dfn-update-via-cache).

#### 3.2.8. [`update()`]
The [`update()`] method steps are:

1. Let `registration` be the [service worker
 registration](#serviceworkerregistration-service-worker-registration).

2. Let `newestWorker` be the result of running [Get Newest
 Worker](#get-newest-worker) algorithm passing `registration` as its
 argument.

3. If `newestWorker` is null, return [a promise rejected
 with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-rejected-with) an
 \"[`InvalidStateError`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#invalidstateerror)\"
 [`DOMException`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException) and abort these steps.

4. If [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [relevant global
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-global) `globalObject` is a
 [`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`](#serviceworkerglobalscope) object, and `globalObject`'s associated
 [service
 worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker)'s [state](#dfn-state) is \"`installing`\", return [a promise rejected
 with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-rejected-with) an
 \"[`InvalidStateError`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#invalidstateerror)\"
 [`DOMException`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException) and abort these steps.

5. Let `promise` be a
 [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects).

6. Let `job` be the result of running [Create
 Job](#create-job) with
 *update*, `registration`'s [storage
 key](#service-worker-registration-storage-key), `registration`'s [scope
 url](#dfn-scope-url),
 `newestWorker`'s [script
 url](#dfn-script-url),
 `promise`, and
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [relevant settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object).

7. Set `job`'s [worker
 type](#dfn-job-worker-type) to `newestWorker`'s
 [type](#dfn-type).

8. Invoke [Schedule Job](#schedule-job) with `job`.

9. Return `promise`.

#### 3.2.9. [`unregister()`]
 The
[`unregister()`](#dom-serviceworkerregistration-unregister) method unregisters the [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration). It is important to note that the currently
[controlled](#dfn-control)
[service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client)'s [active service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker)'s [containing service worker
registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration) is effective until all the [service worker
clients](#dfn-service-worker-client) (including itself) using this [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration) unload. That is, the
[`unregister()`](#dom-serviceworkerregistration-unregister) method only affects subsequent
[navigations](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsing-the-web.html#navigate).

The [`unregister()`] method steps are:

1. Let `registration` be the [service worker
 registration](#serviceworkerregistration-service-worker-registration).

2. Let `promise` be [a new
 promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise).

3. Let `job` be the result of running [Create
 Job](#create-job) with
 *unregister*, `registration`'s [storage
 key](#service-worker-registration-storage-key), `registration`'s [scope
 url](#dfn-scope-url), null,
 `promise`, and
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [relevant settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object).

4. Invoke [Schedule Job](#schedule-job) with `job`.

5. Return `promise`.

#### 3.2.10. Event handler

The following is the [event
handler](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handlers) (and its corresponding [event handler event
type](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handler-event-type)) that *must* be supported, as [event handler IDL
attributes](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handler-idl-attributes), by all objects implementing
[`ServiceWorkerRegistration`](#serviceworkerregistration) interface:

[event
handler](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handlers)

[event handler event
type](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handler-event-type)

[`onupdatefound`]

[`updatefound`](#service-worker-registration-updatefound-event)

### 3.3. [`navigator.serviceWorker`]
```
partial interface Navigator {
 [SecureContext, SameObject] readonly attribute ServiceWorkerContainer serviceWorker;
};

partial interface WorkerNavigator {
 [SecureContext, SameObject] readonly attribute ServiceWorkerContainer serviceWorker;
};
```

The [`serviceWorker`] getter steps are to return the
[`ServiceWorkerContainer`](#serviceworkercontainer) object that is associated with
[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this).

### 3.4. [`ServiceWorkerContainer`]
```
[SecureContext, Exposed=(Window,Worker)]
interface ServiceWorkerContainer : EventTarget {
 readonly attribute ServiceWorker? controller;
 readonly attribute Promise<ServiceWorkerRegistration> ready;

 [NewObject] Promise<ServiceWorkerRegistration> register((TrustedScriptURL or USVString) scriptURL, optional RegistrationOptions options = );

 [NewObject] Promise<(ServiceWorkerRegistration or undefined)> getRegistration(optional USVString clientURL = "");
 [NewObject] Promise<FrozenArray<ServiceWorkerRegistration>> getRegistrations();

 undefined startMessages();

 // events
 attribute EventHandler oncontrollerchange;
 attribute EventHandler onmessage; // event.source of message events is ServiceWorker object
 attribute EventHandler onmessageerror;
};
```

```
dictionary RegistrationOptions {
 USVString scope;
 WorkerType type = "classic";
 ServiceWorkerUpdateViaCache updateViaCache = "imports";
};
```

The user agent *must* create a
[`ServiceWorkerContainer`](#serviceworkercontainer) object when a
[`Navigator`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/system-state.html#navigator) object or a
[`WorkerNavigator`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#workernavigator) object is created and associate it with that object.

A
[`ServiceWorkerContainer`](#serviceworkercontainer) provides capabilities to register, unregister, and
update the [service worker
registrations](#dfn-service-worker-registration), and provides access to the state of the [service
worker
registrations](#dfn-service-worker-registration) and their associated [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker).

A
[`ServiceWorkerContainer`](#serviceworkercontainer) has an associated [service worker
client], which is a
[service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client) whose [global
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-global) is associated with the
[`Navigator`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/system-state.html#navigator) object or the
[`WorkerNavigator`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#workernavigator) object that the
[`ServiceWorkerContainer`](#serviceworkercontainer) is retrieved from.

A
[`ServiceWorkerContainer`](#serviceworkercontainer) object has an associated [ready
promise] (a
[promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects) or null). It is initially null.

A
[`ServiceWorkerContainer`](#serviceworkercontainer) object has a [task
source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#task-source) called the [client message
queue], initially
empty. A [client message
queue](#dfn-client-message-queue) can be enabled or disabled, and is initially disabled.
When a
[`ServiceWorkerContainer`](#serviceworkercontainer) object's [client message
queue](#dfn-client-message-queue) is enabled, the [event
loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop) *must* use it as one of its [task
sources](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#task-source). When the
[`ServiceWorkerContainer`](#serviceworkercontainer) object's [relevant global
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-global) is a
[`Window`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#window) object, all
[tasks](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-task)
[queued](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) on its [client message
queue](#dfn-client-message-queue) *must* be associated with its [relevant settings
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object)'s [associated
document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#concept-document-window).

#### 3.4.1. [`controller`]
[`controller`] attribute *must* run these steps:

1. Let `client` be
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [service worker
 client](#serviceworkercontainer-service-worker-client).

2. If `client`'s [active service
 worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker) is null, then return null.

3. Return the result of [getting the service worker
 object](#get-the-service-worker-object) that represents `client`'s [active
 service
 worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker) in
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [relevant settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object).

[`navigator.serviceWorker.controller`](#dom-serviceworkercontainer-controller) returns `null` if the request is a force refresh
(shift+refresh).

#### 3.4.2. [`ready`]
[`ready`]
attribute *must* run these steps:

1. If [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [ready
 promise](#serviceworkercontainer-ready-promise) is null, then set
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [ready
 promise](#serviceworkercontainer-ready-promise) to [a new
 promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise).

2. Let `readyPromise` be
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [ready
 promise](#serviceworkercontainer-ready-promise).

3. If `readyPromise` is pending, run the following substeps
 [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel):

 1. Let `client` by
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [service worker
 client](#serviceworkercontainer-service-worker-client).

 2. Let `storage key` be the result of running [obtain a
 storage
 key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#obtain-a-storage-key) given client.

 3. Let `registration` be the result of running [Match
 Service Worker
 Registration](#match-service-worker-registration) given `storage key` and
 `client`'s [creation
 URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-creation-url).

 4. If `registration` is not null, and
 `registration`'s [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker) is not null, [queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) on `readyPromise`'s [relevant
 settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object)'s [responsible event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop), using the [DOM manipulation task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source), to resolve `readyPromise` with the
 result of [getting the service worker registration
 object](#get-the-service-worker-registration-object) that represents `registration` in
 `readyPromise`'s [relevant settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object).

4. Return `readyPromise`.

 The returned [ready
promise](#serviceworkercontainer-ready-promise) will never reject. If it does not resolve in this
algorithm, it will eventually resolve when a matching [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration) is registered and its [active
worker](#dfn-active-worker) is set. (See the relevant [Activate algorithm
step](#activate-resolve-ready-step).)

#### 3.4.3. [`register(scriptURL, options)`]
 The
[`register(scriptURL, options)`](#dom-serviceworkercontainer-register) method creates or updates a [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration) for the given [scope
url](#dfn-scope-url). If
successful, a [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration) ties the provided `scriptURL` to a [scope
url](#dfn-scope-url), which is
subsequently used for [navigation
matching](#handle-fetch).

The
[`register(``scriptURL``, ``options``)`] method steps are:

1. Let `p` be a
 [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects).

2. Set `scriptURL` to the result of invoking [Get Trusted
 Type compliant
 string](https://w3c.github.io/trusted-types/dist/spec/#get-trusted-type-compliant-string) with
 [`TrustedScriptURL`](https://w3c.github.io/trusted-types/dist/spec/#trustedscripturl),
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [relevant global
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-global), `scriptURL`, \"ServiceWorkerContainer
 register\", and \"script\".

3. Let `client` be
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [service worker
 client](#serviceworkercontainer-service-worker-client).

4. Let `scriptURL` be the result of
 [parsing](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-parser) `scriptURL` with
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [relevant settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object)'s [API base
 URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#api-base-url).

5. Let `scopeURL` be null.

6. If
 `options`\[\"[`scope`](#dom-registrationoptions-scope)\"\]
 [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), set `scopeURL` to the result of
 [parsing](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-parser)
 `options`\[\"[`scope`](#dom-registrationoptions-scope)\"\] with
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [relevant settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object)'s [API base
 URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#api-base-url).

7. Invoke [Start Register](#start-register) with `scopeURL`, `scriptURL`,
 `p`, `client`, `client`'s [creation
 URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-creation-url),
 `options`\[\"[`type`](#dom-registrationoptions-type)\"\], and
 `options`\[\"[`updateViaCache`](#dom-registrationoptions-updateviacache)\"\].

8. Return `p`.

#### 3.4.4. [`getRegistration(clientURL)`]
[`getRegistration(``clientURL``)`] method steps are:

1. Let `client` be
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [service worker
 client](#serviceworkercontainer-service-worker-client).

2. Let `storage key` be the result of running [obtain a
 storage
 key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#obtain-a-storage-key) given `client`.

3. Let `clientURL` be the result of
 [parsing](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-parser) `clientURL` with
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [relevant settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object)'s [API base
 URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#api-base-url).

4. If `clientURL` is failure, return a
 [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects) rejected with a `TypeError`.

5. Set `clientURL`'s
 [fragment](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-fragment) to null.

6. If the
 [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin) of `clientURL` is not
 `client`'s
 [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin), return a `promise` rejected with a
 \"[`SecurityError`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#securityerror)\"
 [`DOMException`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException).

7. Let `promise` be a new
 [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects).

8. Run the following substeps [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel):

 1. Let `registration` be the result of running [Match
 Service Worker
 Registration](#match-service-worker-registration) given `storage key` and
 `clientURL`.

 2. If `registration` is null, resolve
 `promise` with undefined and abort these steps.

 3. Resolve `promise` with the result of [getting the
 service worker registration
 object](#get-the-service-worker-registration-object) that represents `registration` in
 `promise`'s [relevant settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object).

9. Return `promise`.

#### 3.4.5. [`getRegistrations()`]
[`getRegistrations()`] method steps are:

1. Let `client` be
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [service worker
 client](#serviceworkercontainer-service-worker-client).

2. Let `client storage key` be the result of running [obtain
 a storage
 key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#obtain-a-storage-key) given `client`.

3. Let `promise` be [a new
 promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise).

4. Run the following steps [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel):

 1. Let `registrations` be a new
 [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list).

 2. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) (`storage key`, `scope`)
 → `registration` of [registration
 map](#dfn-scope-to-registration-map):

 1. If `storage key`
 [equals](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#storage-key-equal) `client storage key`, then
 [append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append) `registration` to
 `registrations`.

 3. [Queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) on `promise`'s [relevant settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object)'s [responsible event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop), using the [DOM manipulation task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source), to run the following steps:

 1. Let `registrationObjects` be a new
 [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list).

 2. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) `registration` of
 `registrations`:

 1. Let `registrationObj` be the result of
 [getting the service worker registration
 object](#get-the-service-worker-registration-object) that represents
 `registration` in `promise`'s
 [relevant settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object).

 2. [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append) `registrationObj` to
 `registrationObjects`.

 3. Resolve `promise` with [a new frozen array
 of](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-create-frozen-array) `registrationObjects` in
 `promise`'s [relevant
 Realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm).

5. Return `promise`.

#### 3.4.6. [`startMessages()`]
The [`startMessages()`] method steps are to enable
[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [client message
queue](#dfn-client-message-queue) if it is not enabled.

#### 3.4.7. Event handlers

The following are the [event
handlers](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handlers) (and their corresponding [event handler event
types](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handler-event-type)) that *must* be supported, as [event handler IDL
attributes](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handler-idl-attributes), by all objects implementing the
[`ServiceWorkerContainer`](#serviceworkercontainer) interface:

[event
handler](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handlers)

[event handler event
type](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handler-event-type)

[`oncontrollerchange`]

[`controllerchange`](#service-worker-container-controllerchange-event)

[`onmessage`]

[`message`](#service-worker-container-message-event)

[`onmessageerror`]

[`messageerror`](#service-worker-container-messageerror-event)

The first time the
[`onmessage`](#dom-serviceworkercontainer-onmessage) setter steps are performed, enable
[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [client message
queue](#dfn-client-message-queue).

### 3.5. Events

The following event is dispatched on
[`ServiceWorker`](#serviceworker) object:

Event name

Interface

Dispatched when...

[`statechange`]

[`Event`](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#event)

The
[`state`](#dom-serviceworker-state) attribute of the
[`ServiceWorker`](#serviceworker) object is changed.

The following event is dispatched on
[`ServiceWorkerRegistration`](#serviceworkerregistration) object:

Event name

Interface

Dispatched when...

[`updatefound`]

[`Event`](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#event)

The [service worker
registration](#serviceworkerregistration-service-worker-registration)'s [installing
worker](#dfn-installing-worker) changes. (See step 8 of the
[Install](#install) algorithm.)

The following events are dispatched on
[`ServiceWorkerContainer`](#serviceworkercontainer) object:

Event name

Interface

Dispatched when...

[`controllerchange`]

[`Event`](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#event)

The [service worker
client](#serviceworkercontainer-service-worker-client)'s [active service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker) changes. (See step 9.2 of the
[Activate](#activate) algorithm. The
[skip waiting
flag](#dfn-skip-waiting-flag) of a [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker) causes [activation](#activate) of the [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration) to occur while [service worker
clients](#dfn-service-worker-client) are [using](#dfn-use)
the [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration),
[`navigator.serviceWorker.controller`](#dom-serviceworkercontainer-controller) immediately reflects the [active
worker](#dfn-active-worker) as the [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker) that [controls](#dfn-control) the [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client).)

[`message`]

[`Event`](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#event)

The [service worker
client](#serviceworkercontainer-service-worker-client) receives a message from a [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker). See
[`postMessage(message, options)`](#dom-client-postmessage-message-options).

[`messageerror`]

[`Event`](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#event)

The [service worker
client](#serviceworkercontainer-service-worker-client) is sent a message that cannot be deserialized from a
[service worker](#dfn-service-worker). See
[`postMessage(message, options)`](#dom-client-postmessage-message-options).

### 3.6. [`NavigationPreloadManager`]
```
[SecureContext, Exposed=(Window,Worker)]
interface NavigationPreloadManager {
 Promise<undefined> enable();
 Promise<undefined> disable();
 Promise<undefined> setHeaderValue(ByteString value);
 Promise<NavigationPreloadState> getState();
};

dictionary NavigationPreloadState {
 boolean enabled = false;
 ByteString headerValue;
};
```

#### 3.6.1. [`enable()`]
The [`enable()`] method steps are:

1. Let `promise` be [a new
 promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise).

2. Run the following steps [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel):

 1. Let `registration` be
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s associated [service worker
 registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration).

 2. If `registration`'s [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker) is null,
 [reject](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#reject) `promise` with an
 \"[`InvalidStateError`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#invalidstateerror)\"
 [`DOMException`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException), and abort these steps.

 3. Set `registration`'s [navigation preload enabled
 flag](#service-worker-registration-navigation-preload-enabled-flag).

 4. Resolve `promise` with undefined.

3. Return `promise`.

#### 3.6.2. [`disable()`]
The [`disable()`] method steps are:

1. Let `promise` be [a new
 promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise).

2. Run the following steps [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel):

 1. Let `registration` be
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s associated [service worker
 registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration).

 2. If `registration`'s [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker) is null,
 [reject](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#reject) `promise` with an
 \"[`InvalidStateError`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#invalidstateerror)\"
 [`DOMException`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException), and abort these steps.

 3. Unset `registration`'s [navigation preload enabled
 flag](#service-worker-registration-navigation-preload-enabled-flag).

 4. Resolve `promise` with undefined.

3. Return `promise`.

#### 3.6.3. [`setHeaderValue(value)`]
The
[`setHeaderValue(``value``)`] method steps are:

1. Let `promise` be [a new
 promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise).

2. Run the following steps [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel):

 1. Let `registration` be
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s associated [service worker
 registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration).

 2. If `registration`'s [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker) is null,
 [reject](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#reject) `promise` with an
 \"[`InvalidStateError`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#invalidstateerror)\"
 [`DOMException`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException), and abort these steps.

 3. Set `registration`'s [navigation preload header
 value](#service-worker-registration-navigation-preload-header-value) to `value`.

 4. Resolve `promise` with undefined.

3. Return `promise`.

#### 3.6.4. [`getState()`]
The [`getState()`] method steps are:

1. Let `promise` be [a new
 promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise).

2. Run the following steps [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel):

 1. Let `registration` be
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s associated [service worker
 registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration).

 2. Let `state` be a new
 [`NavigationPreloadState`](#dictdef-navigationpreloadstate) dictionary.

 3. If `registration`'s [navigation preload enabled
 flag](#service-worker-registration-navigation-preload-enabled-flag) is set, set
 `state`\[\"[`enabled`](#dom-navigationpreloadstate-enabled)\"\] to true.

 4. Set
 `state`\[\"[`headerValue`](#dom-navigationpreloadstate-headervalue)\"\] to `registration`'s [navigation
 preload header
 value](#service-worker-registration-navigation-preload-header-value).

 5. Resolve `promise` with `state`.

3. Return `promise`.

## 4. Execution Context

Serving Cached Resources:

``` highlight
// caching.js
self.addEventListener("install", event => {
 event.waitUntil(
 // Open a cache of resources.
 caches.open("shell-v1").then(cache => {
 // Begins the process of fetching them. Succeeds only once all
 // resources have been stored. Even just one failing resource
 // causes the entire operation to fail.
 return cache.addAll([
 "/app.html",
 "/assets/v1/base.css",
 "/assets/v1/app.js",
 "/assets/v1/logo.png",
 "/assets/v1/intro_video.webm"
 ]);
 })
 );
});

self.addEventListener("fetch", event => {
 // No "fetch" events are dispatched to the service worker until it
 // successfully installs and activates.

 // All operations on caches are async, including matching URLs, so we use
 // promises heavily. e.respondWith() even takes promises to enable this:
 event.respondWith(
 caches.match(e.request).then(response => {
 return response || fetch(e.request);
 }).catch(() => {
 return caches.match("/fallback.html");
 })
 );
});
```

### 4.1. [`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`]
```
[Global=(Worker,ServiceWorker), Exposed=ServiceWorker, SecureContext]
interface ServiceWorkerGlobalScope : WorkerGlobalScope {
 [SameObject] readonly attribute Clients clients;
 [SameObject] readonly attribute ServiceWorkerRegistration registration;
 [SameObject] readonly attribute ServiceWorker serviceWorker;

 [NewObject] Promise<undefined> skipWaiting();

 attribute EventHandler oninstall;
 attribute EventHandler onactivate;
 attribute EventHandler onfetch;

 attribute EventHandler onmessage;
 attribute EventHandler onmessageerror;
};
```

A
[`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`](#serviceworkerglobalscope) object represents the global execution context of a
[service worker](#dfn-service-worker).

A
[`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`](#serviceworkerglobalscope) object has an associated [service
worker] (a [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker)).

A
[`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`](#serviceworkerglobalscope) object has an associated [force bypass cache for import
scripts
flag]. It is initially unset.

A
[`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`](#serviceworkerglobalscope) object has an associated [race response
map] which is
an [ordered
map](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-map) where the
[keys](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-getting-the-keys) are
[requests](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request) and the
[values](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-getting-the-values) are [race
response](#dfn-race-response).

A [race response] is a
[struct](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct) used to contain the network response when
[`"race-network-and-fetch-handler"`](#dom-routersourceenum-race-network-and-fetch-handler) performs. It has a [value], which
is a
[response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response), \"`pending`\", or null.

[`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`](#serviceworkerglobalscope) object provides generic, event-driven, time-limited
script execution contexts that run at an origin. Once successfully
[registered](#register), a [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker) is started, kept alive and killed by their relationship
to events, not [service worker
clients](#dfn-service-worker-client). Any type of synchronous requests must not be initiated
inside of a [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker).

#### 4.1.1. [`clients`]
The [`clients`] getter steps are to return the
[`Clients`](#clients) object
that is associated with
[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this).

#### 4.1.2. [`registration`]
The [`registration`] getter steps are to return the result of
[getting the service worker registration
object](#get-the-service-worker-registration-object) representing
[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [service
worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker)'s [containing service worker
registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration) in
[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [relevant settings
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object).

#### 4.1.3. [`serviceWorker`]
The [`serviceWorker`] getter steps are to return the result of
[getting the service worker
object](#get-the-service-worker-object) that represents
[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [service
worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker) in
[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [relevant settings
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object).

#### 4.1.4. [`skipWaiting()`]
 The
[`skipWaiting()`](#dom-serviceworkerglobalscope-skipwaiting) method allows this [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker) to progress from the
[registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration)'s
[waiting](#dfn-waiting-worker) position to
[active](#dfn-active-worker) even while [service worker
clients](#dfn-service-worker-client) are [using](#dfn-use) the
[registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration).

The [`skipWaiting()`] method steps are:

1. Let `promise` be a new
 [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects).

2. Run the following substeps [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel):

 1. Set [service
 worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker)'s [skip waiting
 flag](#dfn-skip-waiting-flag).

 2. Invoke [Try Activate](#try-activate) with [service
 worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker)'s [containing service worker
 registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration).

 3. Resolve `promise` with undefined.

3. Return `promise`.

#### 4.1.5. Event handlers

The following are the [event
handlers](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handlers) (and their corresponding [event handler event
types](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handler-event-type)) that *must* be supported, as [event handler IDL
attributes](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handler-idl-attributes), by all objects implementing the
[`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`](#serviceworkerglobalscope) interface:

[event
handler](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handlers)

[event handler event
type](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handler-event-type)

[`oninstall`]

[`install`](#service-worker-global-scope-install-event)
