[`onactivate`]

[`activate`](#service-worker-global-scope-activate-event)

[`onfetch`]

[`fetch`](#service-worker-global-scope-fetch-event)

[`onmessage`]

[`message`](#eventdef-serviceworkerglobalscope-message)

[`onmessageerror`]

[`messageerror`](#eventdef-serviceworkerglobalscope-messageerror)

### 4.2. [`Client`]
```
[Exposed=ServiceWorker]
interface Client {
 readonly attribute USVString url;
 readonly attribute FrameType frameType;
 readonly attribute DOMString id;
 readonly attribute ClientType type;
 undefined postMessage(any message, sequence<object> transfer);
 undefined postMessage(any message, optional StructuredSerializeOptions options = );
};

[Exposed=ServiceWorker]
interface WindowClient : Client {
 readonly attribute VisibilityState visibilityState;
 readonly attribute boolean focused;
 [SameObject] readonly attribute FrozenArray<USVString> ancestorOrigins;
 [NewObject] Promise<WindowClient> focus();
 [NewObject] Promise<WindowClient?> navigate(USVString url);
};

enum FrameType {
 "auxiliary",
 "top-level",
 "nested",
 "none"
};
```

A [`Client`](#client) object has
an associated [service worker
client] (a [service
worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client)).

A [`Client`](#client) object has
an associated [frame type], which is one
of \"`auxiliary`\", \"`top-level`\", \"`nested`\", and \"`none`\".
Unless stated otherwise it is \"`none`\".

A [`WindowClient`](#windowclient) object has an associated [browsing
context], which is its
[service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client)'s [global
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-global)'s [browsing
context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#browsing-context).

A [`WindowClient`](#windowclient) object has an associated [visibility
state], which is one of
[`visibilityState`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#page-visibility) attribute value.

A [`WindowClient`](#windowclient) object has an associated [focus
state], which is either true or false (initially
false).

A [`WindowClient`](#windowclient) object has an associated [ancestor origins
array].

#### 4.2.1. [`url`]
The [`url`] getter steps are to return
[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s associated [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client)'s
[serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer) [creation
URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-creation-url).

#### 4.2.2. [`frameType`]
The [`frameType`] getter steps are to
return [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [frame
type](#dfn-service-worker-client-frame-type).

#### 4.2.3. [`id`]
The [`id`] getter steps are to return
[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s associated [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client)'s
[id](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-id).

#### 4.2.4. [`type`]
The [`type`] getter steps are:

1. Let `client` be
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [service worker
 client](#dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client).

2. If `client` is an [environment settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object), then:

 1. If `client` is a [window
 client](#dfn-window-client), return
 [`"window"`](#dom-clienttype-window).

 2. Else if `client` is a [dedicated worker
 client](#dfn-dedicatedworker-client), return
 [`"worker"`](#dom-clienttype-worker).

 3. Else if `client` is a [shared worker
 client](#dfn-sharedworker-client), return
 [`"sharedworker"`](#dom-clienttype-sharedworker).

3. Else:

 1. Return
 [`"window"`](#dom-clienttype-window).

#### 4.2.5. [`postMessage(message, transfer)`]
The
[`postMessage(``message``, ``transfer``)`] method steps are:

1. Let `options` be «\[ \"transfer\" → `transfer`
 \]».

2. Invoke
 [`postMessage(message, options)`](#dom-client-postmessage-message-options) with `message` and `options`
 as the arguments.

#### 4.2.6. [`postMessage(message, options)`]
The
[`postMessage(``message``, ``options``)`] method steps
are:

1. Let `contextObject` be
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this).

2. Let `sourceSettings` be the `contextObject`'s
 [relevant settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object).

3. Let `serializeWithTransferResult` be
 [StructuredSerializeWithTransfer](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/structured-data.html#structuredserializewithtransfer)(`message`,
 `options`\[\"[`transfer`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/web-messaging.html#dom-structuredserializeoptions-transfer)\"\]). Rethrow any exceptions.

4. Run the following steps [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel):

 1. Let `targetClient` be null.

 2. For each [service worker
 client](#dfn-service-worker-client) `client`:

 1. If `client` is the `contextObject`'s
 [service worker
 client](#dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client), set `targetClient` to
 `client`, and
 [break](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-break).

 3. If `targetClient` is null, return.

 4. Let `destination` be the
 [`ServiceWorkerContainer`](#serviceworkercontainer) object whose associated [service worker
 client](#serviceworkercontainer-service-worker-client) is `targetClient`.

 5. Add a
 [task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-task) that runs the following steps to
 `destination`'s [client message
 queue](#dfn-client-message-queue):

 1. Let `origin` be the
 [serialization](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#ascii-serialisation-of-an-origin) of `sourceSettings`'s
 [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin).

 2. Let `source` be the result of [getting the
 service worker
 object](#get-the-service-worker-object) that represents
 `contextObject`'s [relevant global
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-global)'s [service
 worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker) in `targetClient`.

 3. Let `deserializeRecord` be
 [StructuredDeserializeWithTransfer](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/structured-data.html#structureddeserializewithtransfer)(`serializeWithTransferResult`,
 `destination`'s [relevant
 Realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm)).

 If this throws an exception, catch it, [fire an
 event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-fire) named
 [`messageerror`](#service-worker-container-messageerror-event) at `destination`,
 using
 [`MessageEvent`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/comms.html#messageevent), with the
 [`origin`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/comms.html#dom-messageevent-origin) attribute initialized to
 `origin` and the
 [`source`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/comms.html#dom-messageevent-source) attribute initialized to
 `source`, and then abort these steps.

 4. Let `messageClone` be
 `deserializeRecord`.\[\[Deserialized\]\].

 5. Let `newPorts` be a new [frozen
 array](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-frozen-array-type) consisting of all
 [`MessagePort`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/web-messaging.html#messageport) objects in
 `deserializeRecord`.\[\[TransferredValues\]\], if
 any.

 6. [Dispatch an
 event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-dispatch) named
 [`message`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/indices.html#event-message) at `destination`,
 using
 [`MessageEvent`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/comms.html#messageevent), with the
 [`origin`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/comms.html#dom-messageevent-origin) attribute initialized to
 `origin`, the
 [`source`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/comms.html#dom-messageevent-source) attribute initialized to
 `source`, the
 [`data`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/comms.html#dom-messageevent-data) attribute initialized to
 `messageClone`, and the
 [`ports`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/comms.html#dom-messageevent-ports) attribute initialized to
 `newPorts`.

#### 4.2.7. [`visibilityState`]
The [`visibilityState`] getter steps are to return
[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [visibility
state](#dfn-service-worker-client-visibilitystate).

#### 4.2.8. [`focused`]
The [`focused`] getter steps are
to return [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [focus
state](#dfn-service-worker-client-focusstate).

#### 4.2.9. [`ancestorOrigins`]
The [`ancestorOrigins`] getter steps are to return
[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s associated [ancestor origins
array](#windowclient-ancestor-origins-array).

#### 4.2.10. [`focus()`]
The [`focus()`] method steps are:

1. If no
 [`Window`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#window) in this
 [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin) has [transient
 activation](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#transient-activation), return a
 [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects) rejected with an
 \"[`InvalidAccessError`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#invalidaccesserror)\"
 [`DOMException`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException).

2. Let `serviceWorkerEventLoop` be the [surrounding
 agent](https://tc39.es/ecma262/multipage/executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#surrounding-agent)'s [event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-agent-event-loop).

3. Let `promise` be a new
 [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects).

4. [Queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) to run the following steps on
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s associated [service worker
 client](#dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client)'s [responsible event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop) using the [user interaction task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#user-interaction-task-source):

 1. Run the [focusing
 steps](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#focusing-steps) with
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [browsing
 context](#dfn-service-worker-client-browsing-context).

 2. Let `frameType` be the result of running [Get Frame
 Type](#get-frame-type)
 with
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [browsing
 context](#dfn-service-worker-client-browsing-context).

 3. Let `visibilityState` be
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [browsing
 context](#dfn-service-worker-client-browsing-context)'s [active
 document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document)'s
 [`visibilityState`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#page-visibility) attribute value.

 4. Let `focusState` be the result of running the [has
 focus
 steps](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#has-focus-steps) with
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [browsing
 context](#dfn-service-worker-client-browsing-context)'s [active
 document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document).

 5. Let `ancestorOriginsList` be
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [browsing
 context](#dfn-service-worker-client-browsing-context)'s [active
 document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document)'s [relevant global
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-global)'s
 [`Location`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#location) object's [ancestor origins
 list](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#concept-location-ancestor-origins-list)'s associated list.

 6. [Queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) to run the following steps on
 `serviceWorkerEventLoop` using the [DOM manipulation
 task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source):

 1. Let `windowClient` be the result of running
 [Create Window
 Client](#create-window-client) with
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s associated [service worker
 client](#dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client), `frameType`,
 `visibilityState`, `focusState`, and
 `ancestorOriginsList`.

 2. If `windowClient`'s [focus
 state](#dfn-service-worker-client-focusstate) is true, resolve `promise` with
 `windowClient`.

 3. Else, reject `promise` with a `TypeError`.

5. Return `promise`.

#### 4.2.11. [`navigate(url)`]
The [`navigate(url)`] method
steps are:

1. Let `url` be the result of
 [parsing](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-parser) `url` with
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [relevant settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object)'s [API base
 URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#api-base-url).

2. If `url` is failure, return a
 [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects) rejected with a `TypeError`.

3. If `url` is `about:blank`, return a
 [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects) rejected with a `TypeError`.

4. If [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s associated [service worker
 client](#dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client)'s [active service
 worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker) is not
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [relevant global
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-global)'s [service
 worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker), return a
 [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects) rejected with a `TypeError`.

5. Let `serviceWorkerEventLoop` be the [current global
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#current-global-object)'s [event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop).

6. Let `promise` be a new
 [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects).

7. [Queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) to run the following steps on
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s associated [service worker
 client](#dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client)'s [responsible event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop) using the [user interaction task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#user-interaction-task-source):

 1. Let `browsingContext` be
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [browsing
 context](#dfn-service-worker-client-browsing-context).

 2. If `browsingContext`'s [associated
 document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#concept-document-window) is not [fully
 active](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#fully-active), [queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) to reject `promise` with a
 `TypeError`, on `serviceWorkerEventLoop` using the
 [DOM manipulation task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source), and abort these steps.

 3. *HandleNavigate*:
 [Navigate](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsing-the-web.html#navigate) `browsingContext` to
 `url`, using `browsingContext`'s
 [associated
 document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#concept-document-window), with
 [exceptionsEnabled](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsing-the-web.html#exceptions-enabled) true.

 4. If the algorithm steps invoked in the step labeled
 *HandleNavigate*
 [throws](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) an exception, [queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) to reject `promise` with the
 exception, on `serviceWorkerEventLoop` using the [DOM
 manipulation task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source), and abort these steps.

 5. Let `frameType` be the result of running [Get Frame
 Type](#get-frame-type)
 with `browsingContext`.

 6. Let `visibilityState` be
 `browsingContext`'s [active
 document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document)'s
 [`visibilityState`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#page-visibility) attribute value.

 7. Let `focusState` be the result of running the [has
 focus
 steps](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#has-focus-steps) with `browsingContext`'s [active
 document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document).

 8. Let `ancestorOriginsList` be
 `browsingContext`'s [active
 document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document)'s [relevant global
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-global)'s
 [`Location`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#location) object's [ancestor origins
 list](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#concept-location-ancestor-origins-list)'s associated list.

 9. [Queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) to run the following steps on
 `serviceWorkerEventLoop` using the [DOM manipulation
 task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source):

 1. If `browsingContext`'s
 [`Window`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#window) object's [environment settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object)'s [creation
 URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-creation-url)'s
 [origin](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-origin) is not the
 [same](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#same-origin) as the [service
 worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker)'s
 [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin), resolve `promise` with null and
 abort these steps.

 2. Let `windowClient` be the result of running
 [Create Window
 Client](#create-window-client) with
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [service worker
 client](#dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client), `frameType`,
 `visibilityState`, `focusState`, and
 `ancestorOriginsList`.

 3. Resolve `promise` with `windowClient`.

8. Return `promise`.

### 4.3. [`Clients`]
```
[Exposed=ServiceWorker]
interface Clients {
 // The objects returned will be new instances every time
 [NewObject] Promise<(Client or undefined)> get(DOMString id);
 [NewObject] Promise<FrozenArray<Client>> matchAll(optional ClientQueryOptions options = );
 [NewObject] Promise<WindowClient?> openWindow(USVString url);
 [NewObject] Promise<undefined> claim();
};
```

```
dictionary ClientQueryOptions {
 boolean includeUncontrolled = false;
 ClientType type = "window";
};
```

```
enum ClientType {
 "window",
 "worker",
 "sharedworker",
 "all"
};
```

The user agent *must* create a
[`Clients`](#clients) object
when a
[`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`](#serviceworkerglobalscope) object is created and associate it with that object.

#### 4.3.1. [`get(id)`]
The [`get(``id``)`] method steps
are:

1. Let `promise` be a new
 [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects).

2. Run these substeps [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel):

 1. For each [service worker
 client](#dfn-service-worker-client) `client` where the result of running
 [obtain a storage
 key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#obtain-a-storage-key) given `client`
 [equals](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#storage-key-equal) the associated [service
 worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker)'s [containing service worker
 registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration)'s [storage
 key](#service-worker-registration-storage-key):

 1. If `client`'s
 [id](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-id) is not `id`,
 [continue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-continue).

 2. Wait for either `client`'s [execution ready
 flag](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-execution-ready-flag) to be set or for `client`'s
 [discarded
 flag](#service-worker-client-discarded-flag) to be set.

 3. If `client`'s [execution ready
 flag](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-execution-ready-flag) is set, then invoke [Resolve Get Client
 Promise](#resolve-get-client-promise) with `client` and
 `promise`, and abort these steps.

 2. Resolve `promise` with undefined.

3. Return `promise`.

#### 4.3.2. [`matchAll(options)`]
The [`matchAll(``options``)`] method steps are:

1. Let `promise` be [a new
 promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise).

2. Run the following steps [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel):

 1. Let `targetClients` be a new
 [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list).

 2. For each [service worker
 client](#dfn-service-worker-client) `client` where the result of running
 [obtain a storage
 key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#obtain-a-storage-key) given `client`
 [equals](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#storage-key-equal) the associated [service
 worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker)'s [containing service worker
 registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration)'s [storage
 key](#service-worker-registration-storage-key):

 1. If `client`'s [execution ready
 flag](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-execution-ready-flag) is unset or `client`'s
 [discarded
 flag](#service-worker-client-discarded-flag) is set,
 [continue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-continue).

 2. If `client` is not a [secure
 context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#secure-context),
 [continue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-continue).

 3. If
 `options`\[\"[`includeUncontrolled`](#dom-clientqueryoptions-includeuncontrolled)\"\] is false, and if `client`'s
 [active service
 worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker) is not the associated [service
 worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker),
 [continue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-continue).

 4. Add `client` to `targetClients`.

 3. Let `matchedWindowData` be a new
 [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list).

 4. Let `matchedClients` be a new
 [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list).

 5. For each [service worker
 client](#dfn-service-worker-client) `client` in
 `targetClients`:

 1. If
 `options`\[\"[`type`](#dom-clientqueryoptions-type)\"\] is
 [`"window"`](#dom-clienttype-window) or
 [`"all"`](#dom-clienttype-all), and `client` is not an
 [environment settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object) or is a [window
 client](#dfn-window-client), then:

 1. Let `windowData` be «\[ \"client\" →
 `client`, \"ancestorOriginsList\" → a new
 [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list) \]».

 2. Let `browsingContext` be null.

 3. Let `isClientEnumerable` be true.

 4. If `client` is an [environment settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object), set `browsingContext` to
 `client`'s [global
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-global)'s [browsing
 context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#browsing-context).

 5. Else, set `browsingContext` to
 `client`'s [target browsing
 context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-target-browsing-context).

 6. [Queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) `task` to run the following
 substeps on `browsingContext`'s [event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop) using the [user interaction task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#user-interaction-task-source):

 1. If `browsingContext` has been
 [discarded](https://wicg.github.io/page-lifecycle/#discarded), then set
 `isClientEnumerable` to false and abort
 these steps.

 2. If `client` is a window client and
 `client`'s [associated
 document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#concept-document-window) is not
 `browsingContext`'s [active
 document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document), then set
 `isClientEnumerable` to false and abort
 these steps.

 3. Set `windowData`\[\"`frameType`\"\] to
 the result of running [Get Frame
 Type](#get-frame-type) with `browsingContext`.

 4. Set `windowData`\[\"`visibilityState`\"\]
 to `browsingContext`'s [active
 document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document)'s
 [`visibilityState`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#page-visibility) attribute
 value.

 5. Set `windowData`\[\"`focusState`\"\] to
 the result of running the [has focus
 steps](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#has-focus-steps) with `browsingContext`'s
 [active
 document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document) as the argument.

 6. If `client` is a [window
 client](#dfn-window-client), then set
 `windowData`\[\"`ancestorOriginsList`\"\]
 to `browsingContext`'s [active
 document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document)'s [relevant global
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-global)'s
 [`Location`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#location) object's [ancestor origins
 list](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#concept-location-ancestor-origins-list)'s associated list.

 7. Wait for `task` to have executed.

 Wait is a blocking wait, but
 implementers may run the iterations in parallel as long
 as the state is not broken.

 8. If `isClientEnumerable` is true, then:

 1. Add `windowData` to
 `matchedWindowData`.

 2. Else if
 `options`\[\"[`type`](#dom-clientqueryoptions-type)\"\] is
 [`"worker"`](#dom-clienttype-worker) or
 [`"all"`](#dom-clienttype-all) and `client` is a [dedicated
 worker
 client](#dfn-dedicatedworker-client), or
 `options`\[\"[`type`](#dom-clientqueryoptions-type)\"\] is
 [`"sharedworker"`](#dom-clienttype-sharedworker) or
 [`"all"`](#dom-clienttype-all) and `client` is a [shared worker
 client](#dfn-sharedworker-client), then:

 1. Add `client` to `matchedClients`.

 6. [Queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) to run the following steps on
 `promise`'s [relevant settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object)'s [responsible event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop) using the [DOM manipulation task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source):

 1. Let `clientObjects` be a new
 [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list).

 2. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) `windowData` in
 `matchedWindowData`:

 1. Let `windowClient` be the result of running
 [Create Window
 Client](#create-window-client) algorithm with
 `windowData`\[\"`client`\"\],
 `windowData`\[\"`frameType`\"\],
 `windowData`\[\"`visibilityState`\"\],
 `windowData`\[\"`focusState`\"\], and
 `windowData`\[\"`ancestorOriginsList`\"\] as
 the arguments.

 2. [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append) `windowClient` to
 `clientObjects`.

 3. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) `client` in
 `matchedClients`:

 1. Let `clientObject` be the result of running
 [Create Client](#create-client) algorithm with `client` as
 the argument.

 2. [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append) `clientObject` to
 `clientObjects`.

 4. Sort `clientObjects` such that:

 - [`WindowClient`](#windowclient) objects whose [browsing
 context](#dfn-service-worker-client-browsing-context) has been
 [focused](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#focusing-steps) are placed first, sorted in the most
 recently
 [focused](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#focusing-steps) order.

 - [`WindowClient`](#windowclient) objects whose [browsing
 context](#dfn-service-worker-client-browsing-context) has never been
 [focused](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#focusing-steps) are placed next, sorted in their [service
 worker
 client](#dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client)'s creation order.

 - [`Client`](#client) objects whose associated [service worker
 client](#dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client) is a [worker
 client](#dfn-worker-client) are placed next, sorted in their [service
 worker
 client](#dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client)'s creation order.

 [Window
 clients](#dfn-window-client) are always placed before [worker
 clients](#dfn-worker-client).

 5. Resolve `promise` with [a new frozen array
 of](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-create-frozen-array) `clientObjects` in
 `promise`'s [relevant
 Realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm).

3. Return `promise`.

#### 4.3.3. [`openWindow(url)`]
The [`openWindow(``url``)`]
method steps are:

1. Let `url` be the result of
 [parsing](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-parser) `url` with
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [relevant settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object)'s [API base
 URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#api-base-url).

2. If `url` is failure, return a
 [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects) rejected with a `TypeError`.

3. If `url` is `about:blank`, return a
 [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects) rejected with a `TypeError`.

4. If no
 [`Window`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#window) in this
 [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin) has [transient
 activation](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#transient-activation), return a
 [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects) rejected with an
 \"[`InvalidAccessError`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#invalidaccesserror)\"
 [`DOMException`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException).

5. Let `serviceWorkerEventLoop` be the [current global
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#current-global-object)'s [event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop).

6. Let `promise` be a new
 [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects).

7. Run these substeps [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel):

 1. Let `newContext` be a new [top-level browsing
 context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#top-level-browsing-context).

 2. [Queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) to run the following steps on
 `newContext`'s
 [`Window`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#window) object's [environment settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object)'s [responsible event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop) using the [user interaction task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#user-interaction-task-source):

 1. *HandleNavigate*:
 [Navigate](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsing-the-web.html#navigate) `newContext` to `url`
 with
 [exceptionsEnabled](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsing-the-web.html#exceptions-enabled) true, and
 *[historyHandling](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsing-the-web.html#navigation-hh)* \"`replace`\".

 2. If the algorithm steps invoked in the step labeled
 *HandleNavigate*
 [throws](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) an exception, [queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) to reject `promise` with the
 exception, on `serviceWorkerEventLoop` using the
 [DOM manipulation task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source), and abort these steps.

 3. Let `frameType` be the result of running [Get
 Frame Type](#get-frame-type) with `newContext`.

 4. Let `visibilityState` be
 `newContext`'s [active
 document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document)'s
 [`visibilityState`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#page-visibility) attribute value.

 5. Let `focusState` be the result of running the
 [has focus
 steps](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#has-focus-steps) with `newContext`'s [active
 document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document) as the argument.

 6. Let `ancestorOriginsList` be
 `newContext`'s [active
 document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document)'s [relevant global
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-global)'s
 [`Location`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#location) object's [ancestor origins
 list](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#concept-location-ancestor-origins-list)'s associated list.

 7. [Queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) to run the following steps on
 `serviceWorkerEventLoop` using the [DOM
 manipulation task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source):

 1. If the result of running [obtain a storage
 key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#obtain-a-storage-key) given `newContext`'s
 [`Window`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#window) object's [environment settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object) is not
 [equal](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#storage-key-equal) to the [service
 worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker)'s [containing service worker
 registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration)'s [storage
 key](#service-worker-registration-storage-key), then resolve `promise` with
 null and abort these steps.

 2. Let `client` be the result of running [Create
 Window
 Client](#create-window-client) with `newContext`'s
 [`Window`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#window) object's [environment settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object), `frameType`,
 `visibilityState`, `focusState`,
 and `ancestorOriginsList` as the arguments.

 3. Resolve `promise` with `client`.

8. Return `promise`.

#### 4.3.4. [`claim()`]
The [`claim()`] method steps are:

1. If the [service
 worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker) is not an [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker), return a
 [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects) rejected with an
 \"[`InvalidStateError`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#invalidstateerror)\"
 [`DOMException`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException).

2. Let `promise` be a new
 [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects).

3. Run the following substeps [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel):

 1. For each [service worker
 client](#dfn-service-worker-client) `client` where the result of running
 [obtain a storage
 key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#obtain-a-storage-key) given `client`
 [equals](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#storage-key-equal) the [service
 worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker)'s [containing service worker
 registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration)'s [storage
 key](#service-worker-registration-storage-key):

 1. If `client`'s [execution ready
 flag](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-execution-ready-flag) is unset or `client`'s
 [discarded
 flag](#service-worker-client-discarded-flag) is set,
 [continue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-continue).

 2. If `client` is not a [secure
 context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#secure-context),
 [continue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-continue).

 3. Let `storage key` be the result of running
 [obtain a storage
 key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#obtain-a-storage-key) given `client`.

 4. Let `registration` be the result of running
 [Match Service Worker
 Registration](#match-service-worker-registration) given `storage key` and
 `client`'s [creation
 URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-creation-url).

 5. If `registration` is not the [service
 worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker)'s [containing service worker
 registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration),
 [continue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-continue).

 `registration` will be null if
 the [service
 worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker)'s [containing service worker
 registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration) is
 [unregistered](#dfn-service-worker-registration-unregistered).

 6. If `client`'s [active service
 worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker) is not the [service
 worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker), then:

 1. Invoke [Handle Service Worker Client
 Unload](#handle-service-worker-client-unload) with `client` as the
 argument.

 2. Set `client`'s [active service
 worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker) to [service
 worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker).

 3. Invoke [Notify Controller
 Change](#notify-controller-change) algorithm with `client` as
 the argument.

 2. Resolve `promise` with undefined.

4. Return `promise`.

### 4.4. [`ExtendableEvent`]
```
[Exposed=ServiceWorker]
interface ExtendableEvent : Event {
 constructor(DOMString type, optional ExtendableEventInit eventInitDict = );
 undefined waitUntil(Promise<any> f);
};
```

```
dictionary ExtendableEventInit : EventInit {
 // Defined for the forward compatibility across the derived events
};
```

An [`ExtendableEvent`](#extendableevent) object has an associated [extend lifetime
promises] (an array of
[promises](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects)). It is initially an empty array.

An [`ExtendableEvent`](#extendableevent) object has an associated [pending promises
count] (the number of
pending promises in the [extend lifetime
promises](#extendableevent-extend-lifetime-promises)). It is initially set to zero.

An [`ExtendableEvent`](#extendableevent) object has an associated [timed out
flag]. It is initially
unset, and is set after an optional user agent imposed delay if the
[pending promises
count](#extendableevent-pending-promises-count) is greater than zero.

An [`ExtendableEvent`](#extendableevent) object is said to be [active]
when its [timed out
flag](#extendableevent-timed-out-flag) is unset and either its [pending promises
count](#extendableevent-pending-promises-count) is greater than zero or its [dispatch
flag](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#dispatch-flag) is set.

[Service workers](#dfn-service-worker) have two [lifecycle
events](#dfn-lifecycle-events),
[`install`](#service-worker-global-scope-install-event) and
[`activate`](#service-worker-global-scope-activate-event). [Service
workers](#dfn-service-worker) use the
[`ExtendableEvent`](#extendableevent) interface for
[`activate`](#service-worker-global-scope-activate-event) event and
[`install`](#service-worker-global-scope-install-event) event.

[Service worker extensions](#extensibility) that [define event
handlers](#extension-to-service-worker-global-scope) *may* also use or
extend the
[`ExtendableEvent`](#extendableevent) interface.

#### 4.4.1. [`event.waitUntil(f)`]
[`waitUntil()`](#dom-extendableevent-waituntil) method extends the lifetime of the event.

The [`waitUntil(``f``)`] method steps are to [add lifetime
promise](#extendableevent-add-lifetime-promise) `f` to
[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this).

To [add lifetime promise]
`promise` (a
[promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects)) to `event` (an
[`ExtendableEvent`](#extendableevent)), run these steps:

1. If `event`'s
 [`isTrusted`](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-event-istrusted) attribute is false,
 [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) an
 \"[`InvalidStateError`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#invalidstateerror)\"
 [`DOMException`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException).

2. If `event` is not
 [active](#extendableevent-active),
 [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) an
 \"[`InvalidStateError`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#invalidstateerror)\"
 [`DOMException`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException).

 If no lifetime extension promise has been added in
 the task that called the event handlers, calling
 [`waitUntil()`](#dom-extendableevent-waituntil) in subsequent asynchronous tasks will throw.

3. Add `promise` to `event`'s [extend lifetime
 promises](#extendableevent-extend-lifetime-promises).

4. Increment `event`'s [pending promises
 count](#extendableevent-pending-promises-count) by one.

 The [pending promises
 count](#extendableevent-pending-promises-count) is incremented even if the given promise has
 already been settled. The corresponding count decrement is done in
 the microtask queued by the reaction to the promise.

5. Upon
 [fulfillment](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#upon-fulfillment) or
 [rejection](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#upon-rejection) of `promise`, [queue a
 microtask](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-microtask) to run these substeps:

 1. Decrement `event`'s [pending promises
 count](#extendableevent-pending-promises-count) by one.

 2. If `event`'s [pending promises
 count](#extendableevent-pending-promises-count) is 0, then:

 1. Let `registration` be the [current global
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#current-global-object)'s associated [service
 worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker)'s [containing service worker
 registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration).

 2. If `registration` is
 [unregistered](#dfn-service-worker-registration-unregistered), invoke [Try Clear
 Registration](#try-clear-registration) with `registration`.

 3. If `registration` is not null, invoke [Try
 Activate](#try-activate) with `registration`.

The user agent *should not*
[terminate](#terminate-service-worker) a [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker) if [Service Worker Has No Pending
Events](#service-worker-has-no-pending-events) returns false for that [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker).

[Service workers](#dfn-service-worker) and [extensions](#extensibility) that [define event
handlers](#extension-to-service-worker-global-scope) *may* define their
own behaviors, allowing the [extend lifetime
promises](#extendableevent-extend-lifetime-promises) to suggest operation length, and the rejected state of
any of the
[promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects) in [extend lifetime
promises](#extendableevent-extend-lifetime-promises) to suggest operation failure.

 [Service
workers](#dfn-service-worker) delay treating the [installing
worker](#dfn-installing-worker) as \"`installed`\" until all the
[promises](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects) in the
[`install`](#service-worker-global-scope-install-event) event's [extend lifetime
promises](#extendableevent-extend-lifetime-promises) resolve successfully. (See the relevant [Install
algorithm step](#install-settle-step).) If any of the promises rejects,
the installation fails. This is primarily used to ensure that a [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker) is not considered \"`installed`\" until all of the core
caches it depends on are populated. Likewise, [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker) delay treating the [active
worker](#dfn-active-worker) as \"`activated`\" until all the
[promises](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects) in the
[`activate`](#service-worker-global-scope-activate-event) event's [extend lifetime
promises](#extendableevent-extend-lifetime-promises) settle. (See the relevant [Activate algorithm
step](#activate-settle-step).) This is primarily used to ensure that any
[functional
events](#dfn-functional-events) are not dispatched to the [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker) until it upgrades database schemas and deletes the
outdated cache entries.

### 4.5. [`InstallEvent`]
```
[Exposed=ServiceWorker]
interface InstallEvent : ExtendableEvent {
 constructor(DOMString type, optional ExtendableEventInit eventInitDict = );
 Promise<undefined> addRoutes((RouterRule or sequence<RouterRule>) rules);
};

dictionary RouterRule {
 required RouterCondition condition;
 required RouterSource source;
};

dictionary RouterCondition {
 URLPatternCompatible urlPattern;
 ByteString requestMethod;
 RequestMode requestMode;
 RequestDestination requestDestination;
 RunningStatus runningStatus;

 sequence<RouterCondition> _or;
 RouterCondition not;
};

typedef (RouterSourceDict or RouterSourceEnum) RouterSource;

dictionary RouterSourceDict {
 DOMString cacheName;
};

enum RunningStatus { "running", "not-running" };
enum RouterSourceEnum {
 "cache",
 "fetch-event",
 "network",
 "race-network-and-fetch-handler"
};
```

A [count router condition result] is a
[struct](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct) that consists of:

- A [condition count] (a number).

- A [quota
 exceeded] (a boolean).

#### 4.5.1. [`event.addRoutes(rules)`]
[`addRoutes(rules)`](#dom-installevent-addroutes) registers rules for this service worker to offload
simple tasks that the fetch event handler ordinarily does.

The [`addRoutes(``rules``)`] method steps are:

1. If `rules` is a
 [`RouterRule`](#dictdef-routerrule) dictionary, set `rules` to «
 `rules` ».

2. Let `serviceWorker` be the [current global
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#current-global-object)'s associated [service
 worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker).

3. For each `rule` of `rules`:

 1. If running the [Verify Router
 Condition](#verify-router-condition) algorithm with
 `rule`\[\"[`condition`](#dom-routerrule-condition)\"\] and `serviceWorker` returns
 false, return [a promise rejected
 with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-rejected-with) a
 [`TypeError`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror).

 2. If
 `rule`\[\"[`source`](#dom-routerrule-source)\"\] is either of
 \"[`fetch-event`](#dom-routersourceenum-fetch-event)\" or
 \"[`race-network-and-fetch-handler`](#dom-routersourceenum-race-network-and-fetch-handler)\", and `serviceWorker`'s [set of
 event types to
 handle](#dfn-set-of-event-types-to-handle) does not
 [contain](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-contain)
 [`fetch`](#service-worker-global-scope-fetch-event), return [a promise rejected
 with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-rejected-with) a
 [`TypeError`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror).

4. Let `lifetimePromise` be a new
 [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects).

5. [Add lifetime
 promise](#extendableevent-add-lifetime-promise) `lifetimePromise` to
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this).

 [`event.addRoutes(rules)`](#dom-installevent-addroutes) extends the lifetime of the event by default as if
 [`event.waitUntil(promise)`](#dom-extendableevent-waituntil) is called.

6. Let `promise` be a new
 [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects).

7. Upon
 [fulfillment](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#upon-fulfillment) or
 [rejection](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#upon-rejection) of `promise`, resolve
 `lifetimePromise` with undefined.

 this step is for making
 `lifetimePromise` always fullfilled to avoid the install
 event failure.

8. [Enqueue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#queue-enqueue) the following steps to [\[\[service worker
 queue\]\]](#service-worker-service-worker-queue):

 1. Let `allRules` be a copy of
 `serviceWorker`'s [list of router
 rules](#service-worker-list-of-router-rules).

 2. For each `rule` of `rules`:

 1. Append `rule` to `allRules`.

 3. If running the [Check Router Registration
 Limit](#check-router-registration-limit) with `allRules` returns false,
 reject `promise` with a
 [`TypeError`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror).

 4. Set `serviceWorker`'s [list of router
 rules](#service-worker-list-of-router-rules) to `allRules`.

 5. Let `serviceWorkerEventLoop` be the [current global
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#current-global-object)'s [event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop).

 6. [Queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) to run the following steps on
 `serviceWorkerEventLoop` using the [DOM manipulation
 task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source):

 1. Resolve `promise` with undefined.

9. Return `promise`.

### 4.6. [`FetchEvent`]
```
[Exposed=ServiceWorker]
interface FetchEvent : ExtendableEvent {
 constructor(DOMString type, FetchEventInit eventInitDict);
 [SameObject] readonly attribute Request request;
 readonly attribute Promise<any> preloadResponse;
 readonly attribute DOMString clientId;
 readonly attribute DOMString resultingClientId;
 readonly attribute DOMString replacesClientId;
 readonly attribute Promise<undefined> handled;

 undefined respondWith(Promise<Response> r);
};
```

```
dictionary FetchEventInit : ExtendableEventInit {
 required Request request;
 Promise<any> preloadResponse;
 DOMString clientId = "";
 DOMString resultingClientId = "";
 DOMString replacesClientId = "";
 Promise<undefined> handled;
};
```

[Service workers](#dfn-service-worker) have an essential [functional
event](#dfn-functional-events)
[`fetch`](#service-worker-global-scope-fetch-event). For
[`fetch`](#service-worker-global-scope-fetch-event) event, [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker) use the
[`FetchEvent`](#fetchevent)
interface which extends the
[`ExtendableEvent`](#extendableevent) interface.

Each event using [`FetchEvent`](#fetchevent) interface has an associated [potential
response] (a
[response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response)), initially set to null, and the following associated
flags that are initially unset:

- [wait to respond flag]

- [respond-with entered flag]

- [respond-with error flag]

#### 4.6.1. [`event.request`]
[`request`] attribute *must*
return the value it was initialized to.

#### 4.6.2. [`event.preloadResponse`]
[`preloadResponse`] attribute
*must* return the value it was initialized to. When an
[event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event) is created the attribute *must* be initialized to [a
promise resolved
with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-resolved-with) undefined.

#### 4.6.3. [`event.clientId`]
[`clientId`] attribute *must*
return the value it was initialized to. When an
[event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event) is created the attribute *must* be initialized to the
empty string.

#### 4.6.4. [`event.resultingClientId`]
[`resultingClientId`] attribute *must* return the value it was initialized to. When
an
[event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event) is created the attribute *must* be initialized to the
empty string.

#### 4.6.5. [`event.replacesClientId`]
[`replacesClientId`] attribute
*must* return the value it was initialized to. When an
[event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event) is created the attribute *must* be initialized to the
empty string.

#### 4.6.6. [`event.handled`]
[`handled`] attribute *must*
return the value it was initialized to. When an
[event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event) is created the attribute *must* be initialized to a
pending
[promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects).

#### 4.6.7. [`event.respondWith(r)`]
 Developers can set the argument `r` with
either a
[promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects) that resolves with a
[`Response`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response) object or a
[`Response`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response) object (which is automatically cast to a promise).
Otherwise, a [network
error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error) is returned to
[Fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch). Renderer-side security checks about tainting for
cross-origin content are tied to the types of [filtered
responses](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-filtered-response) defined in
[Fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch).

[`respondWith(``r``)`]
method steps are:

1. Let `event` be
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this).

2. If `event`'s [dispatch
 flag](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#dispatch-flag) is unset,
 [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) an
 \"[`InvalidStateError`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#invalidstateerror)\"
 [`DOMException`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException).

3. If `event`'s [respond-with entered
 flag](#fetchevent-respond-with-entered-flag) is set,
 [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) an
 \"[`InvalidStateError`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#invalidstateerror)\"
 [`DOMException`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException).

4. [Add lifetime
 promise](#extendableevent-add-lifetime-promise) `r` to `event`.

 [`event.respondWith(r)`](#dom-fetchevent-respondwith) extends the lifetime of the event by default as if
 [`event.waitUntil(r)`](#dom-extendableevent-waituntil) is called.

5. Set `event`'s [stop propagation
 flag](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#stop-propagation-flag) and [stop immediate propagation
 flag](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#stop-immediate-propagation-flag).

6. Set `event`'s [respond-with entered
 flag](#fetchevent-respond-with-entered-flag).

7. Set `event`'s [wait to respond
 flag](#fetchevent-wait-to-respond-flag).

8. Let `targetRealm` be `event`'s [relevant
 Realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm).

9. [Upon
 rejection](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#upon-rejection) of `r`:

 1. Set `event`'s [respond-with error
 flag](#fetchevent-respond-with-error-flag).

 2. Unset `event`'s [wait to respond
 flag](#fetchevent-wait-to-respond-flag).

10. [Upon
 fulfillment](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#upon-fulfillment) of `r` with `response`:

 1. If `response` is not a
 [`Response`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response) object, then set the [respond-with error
 flag](#fetchevent-respond-with-error-flag).

 If the [respond-with error
 flag](#fetchevent-respond-with-error-flag) is set, a [network
 error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error) is returned to
 [Fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch) through [Handle
 Fetch](#handle-fetch)
 algorithm. (See the step 21.1.) Otherwise, the value
 `response` is returned to
 [Fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch) through [Handle
 Fetch](#handle-fetch)
 algorithm. (See the step 22.1.)

 2. Else:

 1. Let `bytes` be an empty byte sequence.

 2. Let `end-of-body` be false.

 3. Let `done` be false.

 4. Let `potentialResponse` be a copy of
 `response`'s associated
 [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-response), except for its
 [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body).

 5. If `response`'s
 [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body) is non-null, run these substeps:

 1. Let `reader` be the result of [getting a
 reader](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-get-a-reader) from `response`'s
 [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body)'s
 [stream](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-stream).

 2. Let `pullAlgorithm` be an action that runs
 these steps:

 1. Let `readRequest` be a new [read
 request](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#read-request) with the following
 [items](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct-item):

 [chunk steps](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#read-request-chunk-steps), given `chunk`

 : 1. Assert: `chunk` is a
 [`Uint8Array`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-Uint8Array).

 2. Append the bytes represented by
 `chunk` to `bytes`.

 3. Increment `potentialResponse`'s
 [body
 info](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body-info)'s [encoded
 size](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-timing-info-encoded-body-size) by `bytes`'s
 [byte
 length](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#buffersource-byte-length).

 4. Increment `potentialResponse`'s
 [body
 info](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body-info)'s [decoded
 size](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-timing-info-decoded-body-size) by `bytes`'s
 [byte
 length](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#buffersource-byte-length).

 5. Perform !
 [DetachArrayBuffer](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-detacharraybuffer)(`chunk`.\[\[ViewedArrayBuffer\]\]).

 [close steps](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#read-request-close-steps)

 : 1. Set `end-of-body` to true.

 [error steps](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#read-request-error-steps)

 : 1. [error](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-error) `newStream` with
 a
 [`TypeError`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror).

 2. [Read a
 chunk](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestreamdefaultreader-read-a-chunk) from `reader` given
 `readRequest`.

 3. Let `cancelAlgorithm` be an action that
 [cancels](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestreamdefaultreader-cancel) `reader`.

 4. Let `highWaterMark` be a non-negative,
 non-NaN number, chosen by the user agent.

 5. Let `sizeAlgorithm` be an algorithm that
 accepts a
 [chunk](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#chunk) object and returns a non-negative,
 non-NaN, non-infinite number, chosen by the user agent.

 6. Let `newStream` be a new
 [`ReadableStream`](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream), [set
 up](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-set-up) with the
 [pullAlgorithm](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-set-up-pullalgorithm) `pullAlgorithm`, the
 [cancelAlgorithm](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-set-up-cancelalgorithm) `cancelAlgorithm`, the
 [highWaterMark](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-set-up-highwatermark) `highWaterMark`, and
 [sizeAlgorithm](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-set-up-sizealgorithm) `sizeAlgorithm`, in
 `targetRealm`.

 7. Set `potentialResponse`'s
 [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body) to a new
 [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body) whose
 [stream](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-stream) is `newStream`.

 8. Run these subsubsteps repeatedly [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel) while `done` is false:

 1. If `newStream` is
 [errored](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-errored), then set `done` to
 true.

 2. Otherwise, if `bytes` is empty and
 `end-of-body` is true, then
 [close](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-close) `newStream` and set
 `done` to true.

 3. Otherwise, if `bytes` is not empty, run
 these subsubsubsteps:

 1. Let `chunk` be a subsequence of
 `bytes` starting from the beginning
 of `bytes`.

 2. Remove `chunk` from
 `bytes`.

 3. Let `buffer` be an
 [`ArrayBuffer`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-ArrayBuffer) object created in
 `targetRealm` and containing
 `chunk`.

 4. [Enqueue](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-enqueue) a
 [`Uint8Array`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-Uint8Array) object created in
 `targetRealm` and wrapping
 `buffer` to `newStream`.

 These substeps are meant to produce the
 observable equivalent of \"piping\" `response`'s
 [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body)'s
 [stream](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-stream) into `potentialResponse`.

 The data written by the service worker in
 chunks are not guaranteed to read in identical chunks by the
 client that receives the data. That is, the client will read
 the same data that was written, but it may be chunked
 differently by the browser.

 6. Set `event`'s [potential
 response](#fetchevent-potential-response) to `potentialResponse`.

 3. Unset `event`'s [wait to respond
 flag](#fetchevent-wait-to-respond-flag).

### 4.7. [`ExtendableMessageEvent`]
```
[Exposed=ServiceWorker]
interface ExtendableMessageEvent : ExtendableEvent {
 constructor(DOMString type, optional ExtendableMessageEventInit eventInitDict = );
 readonly attribute any data;
 readonly attribute USVString origin;
 readonly attribute DOMString lastEventId;
 [SameObject] readonly attribute (Client or ServiceWorker or MessagePort)? source;
 readonly attribute FrozenArray<MessagePort> ports;
};
```

```
dictionary ExtendableMessageEventInit : ExtendableEventInit {
 any data = null;
 USVString origin = "";
 DOMString lastEventId = "";
 (Client or ServiceWorker or MessagePort)? source = null;
 sequence<MessagePort> ports = ;
};
```

[Service workers](#dfn-service-worker) define the
[extendable](#dom-extendableevent-waituntil)
[`message`](#eventdef-serviceworkerglobalscope-message) event to allow extending the lifetime of
the event. For the
[`message`](#eventdef-serviceworkerglobalscope-message) event, [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker) use the
[`ExtendableMessageEvent`](#extendablemessageevent) interface which extends the
[`ExtendableEvent`](#extendableevent) interface.

#### 4.7.1. [`event.data`]
The [`data`] attribute *must* return the value it was initialized to. When
the object is created, this attribute *must* be initialized to null. It
represents the message being sent.

#### 4.7.2. [`event.origin`]
The [`origin`] attribute *must* return the value it was initialized to. When
the object is created, this attribute *must* be initialized to the empty
string. It represents the
[origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin) of the [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client) that sent the message.

#### 4.7.3. [`event.lastEventId`]
The [`lastEventId`] attribute *must* return the value it was
initialized to. When the object is created, this attribute *must* be
initialized to the empty string.

#### 4.7.4. [`event.source`]
The [`source`] attribute *must* return the value it was initialized to. When
the object is created, this attribute *must* be initialized to null. It
represents the [`Client`](#client) object from which the message is sent.

#### 4.7.5. [`event.ports`]
The [`ports`] attribute *must* return the value it was initialized to. When
the object is created, this attribute *must* be initialized to the empty
array. It represents the
[`MessagePort`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/web-messaging.html#messageport) array being sent.

[Install](#install) algorithm.)

worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker)'s [containing service worker

## 5. Caches

To allow authors to fully manage their content caches for offline use,
the
[`Window`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#window) and the
[`WorkerGlobalScope`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#workerglobalscope) provide the asynchronous caching methods that open and
manipulate [`Cache`](#cache)
objects. An
[origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin) can have multiple, named
[`Cache`](#cache) objects, whose
contents are entirely under the control of scripts. Caches are not
shared across
[origins](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin), and they are completely isolated from the browser's
HTTP cache.

### 5.1. Constructs

A [request response list] is a
[list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list) of
[tuples](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#tuple) consisting of a request (a
[request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request)) and a response (a
[response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response)).

The [relevant request response list] is the instance that
[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this) represents.

A [name to cache map] is an [ordered
map](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-map) whose
[entry](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-entry) consists of a
[key](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-key) (a string that represents the name of a [request
response
list](#dfn-request-response-list)) and a
[value](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-value) (a [request response
list](#dfn-request-response-list)).

The [relevant name to cache map] for a
[`CacheStorage`](#cachestorage) object is the [name to cache
map](#dfn-name-to-cache-map) associated with the result of running [obtain a local
storage bottle
map](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#obtain-a-local-storage-bottle-map) with the object's [relevant settings
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object) and \"`caches`\".

### 5.2. Understanding Cache Lifetimes

The [`Cache`](#cache) instances
are not part of the browser's HTTP cache. The
[`Cache`](#cache) objects are
exactly what authors have to manage themselves. The
[`Cache`](#cache) objects do not
get updated unless authors explicitly request them to be. The
[`Cache`](#cache) objects do not
expire unless authors delete the entries. The
[`Cache`](#cache) objects do not
disappear just because the [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker) script is updated. That is, caches are not updated
automatically. Updates must be manually managed. This implies that
authors should version their caches by name and make sure to use the
caches only from the version of the [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker) that can safely operate on.

### 5.3. [`self.caches`]
```
partial interface mixin WindowOrWorkerGlobalScope {
 [SecureContext, SameObject] readonly attribute CacheStorage caches;
};
```

#### 5.3.1. [`caches`]
[`caches`]
getter steps are to return
[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s associated
[`CacheStorage`](#cachestorage) object.

### 5.4. [`Cache`]
```
[SecureContext, Exposed=(Window,Worker)]
interface Cache {
 [NewObject] Promise<(Response or undefined)> match(RequestInfo request, optional CacheQueryOptions options = );
 [NewObject] Promise<FrozenArray<Response>> matchAll(optional RequestInfo request, optional CacheQueryOptions options = );
 [NewObject] Promise<undefined> add(RequestInfo request);
 [NewObject] Promise<undefined> addAll(sequence<RequestInfo> requests);
 [NewObject] Promise<undefined> put(RequestInfo request, Response response);
 [NewObject] Promise<boolean> delete(RequestInfo request, optional CacheQueryOptions options = );
 [NewObject] Promise<FrozenArray<Request>> keys(optional RequestInfo request, optional CacheQueryOptions options = );
};
```

```
dictionary CacheQueryOptions {
 boolean ignoreSearch = false;
 boolean ignoreMethod = false;
 boolean ignoreVary = false;
};
```

A [`Cache`](#cache) object
represents a [request response
list](#dfn-request-response-list). Multiple separate objects implementing the
[`Cache`](#cache) interface
across documents and workers can all be associated with the same
[request response
list](#dfn-request-response-list) simultaneously.

A [cache batch operation] is a
[struct](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct) that consists of:

- A [type]
 (\"`delete`\" or \"`put`\").

- A [request] (a
 [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request)).

- A [response] (a
 [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response)).

- An [options] (a
 [`CacheQueryOptions`](#dictdef-cachequeryoptions)).

#### 5.4.1. [`match(request, options)`]
The
[`match(``request``, ``options``)`] method steps are:

1. Let `promise` be [a new
 promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise).

2. Run these substeps [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel):

 1. Let `p` be the result of running the algorithm
 specified in
 [`matchAll(request, options)`](#dom-cache-matchall) method with `request` and
 `options`.

 2. Wait until `p` settles.

 3. If `p` rejects with an exception, then:

 1. Reject `promise` with that exception.

 4. Else if `p` resolves with an array,
 `responses`, then:

 1. If `responses` is an empty array, then:

 1. Resolve `promise` with undefined.

 2. Else:

 1. Resolve `promise` with the first element of
 `responses`.

3. Return `promise`.

#### 5.4.2. [`matchAll(request, options)`]
The
[`matchAll(``request``, ``options``)`] method
steps are:

1. Let `r` be null.

2. If the optional argument `request` is not omitted, then:

 1. If `request` is a
 [`Request`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request) object, then:

 1. Set `r` to `request`'s
 [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request).

 2. If `r`'s
 [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method) is not \``GET`\` and
 `options`.ignoreMethod is false, return [a
 promise resolved
 with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-resolved-with) an empty array.

 2. Else if `request` is a string, then:

 1. Set `r` to the associated
 [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request) of the result of invoking the initial value
 of
 [`Request`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request) as constructor with `request` as
 its argument. If this
 [throws](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) an exception, return [a promise rejected
 with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-rejected-with) that exception.

3. Let `realm` be
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [relevant
 realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm).

4. Let `promise` be [a new
 promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise).

5. Run these substeps [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel):

 1. Let `responses` be an empty
 [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list).

 2. If the optional argument `request` is omitted, then:

 1. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) `requestResponse` of the
 [relevant request response
 list](#dfn-relevant-request-response-list):

 1. Add a copy of `requestResponse`'s response to
 `responses`.

 3. Else:

 1. Let `requestResponses` be the result of running
 [Query Cache](#query-cache) with `r` and
 `options`.

 2. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) `requestResponse` of
 `requestResponses`:

 1. Add a copy of `requestResponse`'s response to
 `responses`.

 4. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) `response` of
 `responses`:

 1. If `response`'s
 [type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-type) is \"`opaque`\" and [cross-origin resource
 policy
 check](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#cross-origin-resource-policy-check) with `promise`'s [relevant
 settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object)'s
 [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin), `promise`'s [relevant settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object), \"\", and `response`'s
 [internal
 response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-internal-response) returns **blocked**, then reject
 `promise` with a `TypeError` and abort these
 steps.

 5. [Queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task), on `promise`'s [relevant settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object)'s [responsible event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop) using the [DOM manipulation task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source), to perform the following steps:

 1. Let `responseList` be a
 [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list).

 2. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) `response` of
 `responses`:

 1. Add a new
 [`Response`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response) object associated with
 `response` and a new
 [`Headers`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#headers) object whose
 [guard](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-guard) is \"`immutable`\" to
 `responseList`.

 3. Resolve `promise` with a [frozen
 array](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-frozen-array-type)
 [created](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-create-frozen-array) from `responseList`, in
 `realm`.

6. Return `promise`.

#### 5.4.3. [`add(request)`]
The [`add(``request``)`] method steps are:

1. Let `requests` be an array containing only
 `request`.

2. Let `responseArrayPromise` be the result of running the
 algorithm specified in
 [`addAll(requests)`](#dom-cache-addall) passing `requests` as the argument.

3. Return the result of
 [reacting](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-perform-steps-once-promise-is-settled) to `responseArrayPromise` with a
 fulfillment handler that returns undefined.

#### 5.4.4. [`addAll(requests)`]
The [`addAll(``requests``)`]
method steps are:

1. Let `responsePromises` be an empty
 [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list).

2. Let `requestList` be an empty
 [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list).

3. For each `request` whose type is
 [`Request`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request) in `requests`:

 1. Let `r` be `request`'s
 [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request).

 2. If `r`'s
 [url](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url)'s
 [scheme](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-scheme) is not one of \"`http`\" and \"`https`\", or
 `r`'s
 [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method) is not \``GET`\`, return [a promise rejected
 with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-rejected-with) a `TypeError`.

4. Let `fetchControllers` be a
 [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list) of [fetch
 controllers](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller).

5. For each `request` in `requests`:

 1. Let `r` be the associated
 [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request) of the result of invoking the initial value of
 [`Request`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request) as constructor with `request` as its
 argument. If this
 [throws](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) an exception, return [a promise rejected
 with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-rejected-with) that exception.

 2. If `r`'s
 [url](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url)'s
 [scheme](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-scheme) is not one of \"`http`\" and \"`https`\", then:

 1. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) `fetchController` of
 `fetchControllers`,
 [abort](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-abort) `fetchController`.

 2. Return [a promise rejected
 with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-rejected-with) a `TypeError`.

 3. If `r`'s
 [client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-client)'s [global
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-global) is a
 [`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`](#serviceworkerglobalscope) object, set `request`'s
 [service-workers
 mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-service-workers-mode) to \"`none`\".

 4. Set `r`'s
 [initiator](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-initiator) to \"`fetch`\" and
 [destination](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-destination) to \"`subresource`\".

 5. Add `r` to `requestList`.

 6. Let `responsePromise` be [a new
 promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise).

 7. Run the following substeps [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel):

 - [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append) the result of
 [fetching](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch) `r`.

 - To
 [processResponse](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#process-response) for `response`, run these
 substeps:

 1. If `response`'s
 [type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-type) is \"`error`\", or
 `response`'s
 [status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status) is not an [ok
 status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#ok-status) or is `206`, reject
 `responsePromise` with a `TypeError`.

 2. Else if `response`'s [header
 list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list) contains a
 [header](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header)
 [named](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-name) \``Vary`\`, then:

 1. Let `fieldValues` be the
 [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list) containing the elements corresponding
 to the
 [field-values](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7230#section-3.2) of the
 [Vary](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7231#section-7.1.4) header.

 2. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) `fieldValue` of
 `fieldValues`:

 1. If `fieldValue` matches \"`*`\", then:

 1. Reject `responsePromise` with a
 `TypeError`.

 2. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) `fetchController`
 of `fetchControllers`,
 [abort](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-abort) `fetchController`.

 3. Abort these steps.

 - To
 [processResponseEndOfBody](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-processresponseendofbody) for `response`, run these
 substeps:

 1. If `response`'s [aborted
 flag](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-aborted) is set, reject
 `responsePromise` with an
 \"[`AbortError`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#aborterror)\"
 [`DOMException`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException) and abort these steps.

 2. Resolve `responsePromise` with
 `response`.

 The cache commit is allowed when the
 response's body is fully received.

 8. Add `responsePromise` to
 `responsePromises`.

6. Let `p` be the result of [getting a promise to wait for
 all](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#waiting-for-all-promise) of `responsePromises`.

7. Return the result of
 [reacting](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-perform-steps-once-promise-is-settled) to `p` with a fulfillment handler that,
 when called with argument `responses`, performs the
 following substeps:

 1. Let `operations` be an empty
 [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list).

 2. Let `index` be zero.

 3. For each `response` in `responses`:

 1. Let `operation` be a [cache batch
 operation](#dfn-cache-batch-operation).

 2. Set `operation`'s
 [type](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-type) to \"`put`\".

 3. Set `operation`'s
 [request](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-request) to
 `requestList`\[`index`\].

 4. Set `operation`'s
 [response](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-response) to `response`.

 5. [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append) `operation` to
 `operations`.

 6. Increment `index` by one.

 4. Let `realm` be
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [relevant
 realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm).

 5. Let `cacheJobPromise` be [a new
 promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise).

 6. Run the following substeps [in
