 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel):

 1. Let `errorData` be null.

 2. Invoke [Batch Cache
 Operations](#batch-cache-operations) with `operations`. If this
 [throws](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) an exception, set `errorData` to
 the exception.

 3. [Queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task), on `cacheJobPromise`'s
 [relevant settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object)'s [responsible event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop) using the [DOM manipulation task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source), to perform the following substeps:

 1. If `errorData` is null, resolve
 `cacheJobPromise` with undefined.

 2. Else, reject `cacheJobPromise` with a
 [new](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-create-exception)
 [exception](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-exception) with `errorData`, in
 `realm`.

 7. Return `cacheJobPromise`.

#### 5.4.5. [`put(request, response)`]
The
[`put(``request``, ``response``)`]
method steps are:

1. Let `innerRequest` be null.

2. If `request` is a
 [`Request`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request) object, then set `innerRequest` to
 `request`'s
 [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request).

3. Else:

 1. Let `requestObj` be the result of invoking
 [`Request`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request)'s constructor with `request` as its
 argument. If this
 [throws](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) an `exception`, return [a promise
 rejected
 with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-rejected-with) `exception`.

 2. Set `innerRequest` to `requestObj`'s
 [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request).

4. If `innerRequest`'s
 [url](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url)'s
 [scheme](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-scheme) is not one of \"`http`\" and \"`https`\", or
 `innerRequest`'s
 [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method) is not \``GET`\`, return [a promise rejected
 with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-rejected-with) a `TypeError`.

5. Let `innerResponse` be `response`'s
 [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-response).

6. If `innerResponse`'s
 [status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status) is `206`, return [a promise rejected
 with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-rejected-with) a `TypeError`.

7. If `innerResponse`'s [header
 list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list) contains a
 [header](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header)
 [named](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-name) \``Vary`\`, then:

 1. Let `fieldValues` be the
 [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list) containing the
 [items](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-item) corresponding to the
 [Vary](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7231#section-7.1.4) header's
 [field-values](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7230#section-3.2).

 2. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) `fieldValue` in
 `fieldValues`:

 1. If `fieldValue` matches \"`*`\", return [a
 promise rejected
 with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-rejected-with) a `TypeError`.

8. If `innerResponse`'s
 [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body) is
 [disturbed](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#is-readable-stream-disturbed) or
 [locked](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-locked), return [a promise rejected
 with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-rejected-with) a `TypeError`.

9. Let `clonedResponse` be a
 [clone](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-clone) of `innerResponse`.

10. Let `bodyReadPromise` be [a promise resolved
 with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-resolved-with) undefined.

11. If `innerResponse`'s
 [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body) is non-null, run these substeps:

 1. Let `stream` be `innerResponse`'s
 [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body)'s
 [stream](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-stream).

 2. Let `reader` be the result of [getting a
 reader](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-get-a-reader) for `stream`.

 3. Set `bodyReadPromise` to the result of [reading all
 bytes](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestreamdefaultreader-read-all-bytes) from `reader`.

 This ensures that `innerResponse`'s
 [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body) is
 [locked](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-locked), and we have a full buffered copy of the body in
 `clonedResponse`. An implementation could optimize by
 streaming directly to disk rather than memory.

12. Let `operations` be an empty
 [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list).

13. Let `operation` be a [cache batch
 operation](#dfn-cache-batch-operation).

14. Set `operation`'s
 [type](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-type) to \"`put`\".

15. Set `operation`'s
 [request](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-request) to `innerRequest`.

16. Set `operation`'s
 [response](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-response) to `clonedResponse`.

17. [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append) `operation` to `operations`.

18. Let `realm` be
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [relevant
 realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm).

19. Return the result of the
 [fulfillment](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#upon-fulfillment) of `bodyReadPromise`:

 1. Let `cacheJobPromise` be [a new
 promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise).

 2. Return `cacheJobPromise` and run these steps [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel):

 1. Let `errorData` be null.

 2. Invoke [Batch Cache
 Operations](#batch-cache-operations) with `operations`. If this
 [throws](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) an exception, set `errorData` to
 the exception.

 3. [Queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task), on `cacheJobPromise`'s
 [relevant settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object)'s [responsible event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop) using the [DOM manipulation task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source), to perform the following substeps:

 1. If `errorData` is null, resolve
 `cacheJobPromise` with undefined.

 2. Else, reject `cacheJobPromise` with a
 [new](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-create-exception)
 [exception](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-exception) with `errorData`, in
 `realm`.

#### 5.4.6. [`delete(request, options)`]
The
[`delete(``request``, ``options``)`] method steps are:

1. Let `r` be null.

2. If `request` is a
 [`Request`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request) object, then:

 1. Set `r` to `request`'s
 [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request).

 2. If `r`'s
 [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method) is not \``GET`\` and
 `options`.ignoreMethod is false, return [a promise
 resolved
 with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-resolved-with) false.

3. Else if `request` is a string, then:

 1. Set `r` to the associated
 [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request) of the result of invoking the initial value of
 [`Request`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request) as constructor with `request` as its
 argument. If this
 [throws](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) an exception, return [a promise rejected
 with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-rejected-with) that exception.

4. Let `operations` be an empty
 [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list).

5. Let `operation` be a [cache batch
 operation](#dfn-cache-batch-operation).

6. Set `operation`'s
 [type](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-type) to \"`delete`\".

7. Set `operation`'s
 [request](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-request) to `r`.

8. Set `operation`'s
 [options](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-options) to `options`.

9. [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append) `operation` to `operations`.

10. Let `realm` be
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [relevant
 realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm).

11. Let `cacheJobPromise` be [a new
 promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise).

12. Run the following substeps [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel):

 1. Let `errorData` be null.

 2. Let `requestResponses` be the result of running
 [Batch Cache
 Operations](#batch-cache-operations) with `operations`. If this
 [throws](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) an exception, set `errorData` to the
 exception.

 3. [Queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task), on `cacheJobPromise`'s [relevant
 settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object)'s [responsible event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop) using the [DOM manipulation task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source), to perform the following substeps:

 1. If `errorData` is null, then:

 1. If `requestResponses` [is not
 empty](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-is-empty), resolve `cacheJobPromise`
 with true.

 2. Else, resolve `cacheJobPromise` with false.

 2. Else, reject `cacheJobPromise` with a
 [new](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-create-exception)
 [exception](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-exception) with `errorData`, in
 `realm`.

13. Return `cacheJobPromise`.

#### 5.4.7. [`keys(request, options)`]
The
[`keys(``request``, ``options``)`] method steps are:

1. Let `r` be null.

2. If the optional argument `request` is not omitted, then:

 1. If `request` is a
 [`Request`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request) object, then:

 1. Set `r` to `request`'s
 [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request).

 2. If `r`'s
 [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method) is not \``GET`\` and
 `options`.ignoreMethod is false, return [a
 promise resolved
 with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-resolved-with) an empty array.

 2. Else if `request` is a string, then:

 1. Set `r` to the associated
 [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request) of the result of invoking the initial value
 of
 [`Request`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request) as constructor with `request` as
 its argument. If this
 [throws](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) an exception, return [a promise rejected
 with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-rejected-with) that exception.

3. Let `realm` be
 [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)'s [relevant
 realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm).

4. Let `promise` be [a new
 promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise).

5. Run these substeps [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel):

 1. Let `requests` be an empty
 [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list).

 2. If the optional argument `request` is omitted, then:

 1. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) `requestResponse` of the
 [relevant request response
 list](#dfn-relevant-request-response-list):

 1. Add `requestResponse`'s request to
 `requests`.

 3. Else:

 1. Let `requestResponses` be the result of running
 [Query Cache](#query-cache) with `r` and
 `options`.

 2. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) `requestResponse` of
 `requestResponses`:

 1. Add `requestResponse`'s request to
 `requests`.

 4. [Queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task), on `promise`'s [relevant settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object)'s [responsible event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop) using the [DOM manipulation task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source), to perform the following steps:

 1. Let `requestList` be a
 [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list).

 2. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) `request` of
 `requests`:

 1. Add a new
 [`Request`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request) object associated with
 `request` and a new associated
 [`Headers`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#headers) object whose
 [guard](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-guard) is \"`immutable`\" to
 `requestList`.

 3. Resolve `promise` with a [frozen
 array](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-frozen-array-type)
 [created](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-create-frozen-array) from `requestList`, in
 `realm`.

6. Return `promise`.

### 5.5. [`CacheStorage`]
```
[SecureContext, Exposed=(Window,Worker)]
interface CacheStorage {
 [NewObject] Promise<(Response or undefined)> match(RequestInfo request, optional MultiCacheQueryOptions options = );
 [NewObject] Promise<boolean> has(DOMString cacheName);
 [NewObject] Promise<Cache> open(DOMString cacheName);
 [NewObject] Promise<boolean> delete(DOMString cacheName);
 [NewObject] Promise<sequence<DOMString>> keys();
};

dictionary MultiCacheQueryOptions : CacheQueryOptions {
 DOMString cacheName;
};
```

[`CacheStorage`](#cachestorage) interface is designed to largely conform to [ECMAScript
6 Map
objects](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-map-objects) but entirely async, and with additional convenience
methods. The methods, `clear`, `forEach`, `entries` and `values`, are
intentionally excluded from the scope of the first version resorting to
the ongoing discussion about the async iteration by TC39.

The user agent *must* create a
[`CacheStorage`](#cachestorage) object when a
[`Window`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#window) object or a
[`WorkerGlobalScope`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#workerglobalscope) object is created.

A [`CacheStorage`](#cachestorage) object represents the [name to cache
map](#dfn-name-to-cache-map) associated with the result of running [obtain a local
storage bottle
map](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#obtain-a-local-storage-bottle-map) with its [relevant settings
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object) and \"`caches`\". Multiple separate objects
implementing the
[`CacheStorage`](#cachestorage) interface across documents and workers can all be
associated with the same [name to cache
map](#dfn-name-to-cache-map) simultaneously.

#### 5.5.1. [`match(request, options)`]
The
[`match(``request``, ``options``)`] method steps are:

1. If
 `options`\[\"[`cacheName`](#dom-multicachequeryoptions-cachename)\"\]
 [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), then:

 1. Return [a new
 promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise) `promise` and run the following
 substeps [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel):

 1. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-iterate) `cacheName` → `cache`
 of the [relevant name to cache
 map](#relevant-name-to-cache-map):

 1. If
 `options`\[\"[`cacheName`](#dom-multicachequeryoptions-cachename)\"\] matches `cacheName`,
 then:

 1. Resolve `promise` with the result of
 running the algorithm specified in
 [`match(request, options)`](#dom-cache-match) method of
 [`Cache`](#cache) interface with `request`
 and `options` (providing
 `cache` as thisArgument to the
 `\[[Call]]` internal method of
 [`match(request, options)`](#dom-cache-match).)

 2. Abort these steps.

 2. Resolve `promise` with undefined.

2. Else:

 1. Let `promise` be [a promise resolved
 with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-resolved-with) undefined.

 2. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-iterate) `cacheName` → `cache` of
 the [relevant name to cache
 map](#relevant-name-to-cache-map):

 1. Set `promise` to the result of
 [reacting](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-perform-steps-once-promise-is-settled) to itself with a fulfillment handler that,
 when called with argument `response`, performs
 the following substeps:

 1. If `response` is not undefined, return
 `response`.

 2. Return the result of running the algorithm specified in
 [`match(request, options)`](#dom-cache-match) method of
 [`Cache`](#cache) interface with `request` and
 `options` as the arguments (providing
 `cache` as thisArgument to the `\[[Call]]`
 internal method of
 [`match(request, options)`](#dom-cache-match).)

 3. Return `promise`.

#### 5.5.2. [`has(cacheName)`]
The [`has(``cacheName``)`] method steps are:

1. Let `promise` be [a new
 promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise).

2. Run the following substeps [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel):

 1. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-iterate) `key` → `value` of the
 [relevant name to cache
 map](#relevant-name-to-cache-map):

 1. If `cacheName` matches `key`, resolve
 `promise` with true and abort these steps.

 2. Resolve `promise` with false.

3. Return `promise`.

#### 5.5.3. [`open(cacheName)`]
The [`open(``cacheName``)`] method steps are:

1. Let `promise` be [a new
 promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise).

2. Run the following substeps [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel):

 1. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-iterate) `key` → `value` of the
 [relevant name to cache
 map](#relevant-name-to-cache-map):

 1. If `cacheName` matches `key`, then:

 1. Resolve `promise` with a new
 [`Cache`](#cache) object that represents
 `value`.

 2. Abort these steps.

 2. Let `cache` be a new [request response
 list](#dfn-request-response-list).

 3. [Set](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-set) the [relevant name to cache
 map](#relevant-name-to-cache-map)\[`cacheName`\] to
 `cache`. If this cache write operation failed due to
 exceeding the granted quota limit, reject `promise`
 with a
 [`QuotaExceededError`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#quotaexceedederror) and abort these steps.

 4. Resolve `promise` with a new
 [`Cache`](#cache) object
 that represents `cache`.

3. Return `promise`.

#### 5.5.4. [`delete(cacheName)`]
The [`delete(``cacheName``)`] method steps are:

1. Let `promise` be the result of running the algorithm
 specified in
 [`has(cacheName)`](#dom-cachestorage-has) method with `cacheName`.

2. Return the result of
 [reacting](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-perform-steps-once-promise-is-settled) to `promise` with a fulfillment handler
 that, when called with argument `cacheExists`, performs
 the following substeps:

 1. If `cacheExists` is false, then:

 1. Return false.

 2. Let `cacheJobPromise` be [a new
 promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise).

 3. Run the following substeps [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel):

 1. [Remove](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-remove) the [relevant name to cache
 map](#relevant-name-to-cache-map)\[`cacheName`\].

 2. Resolve `cacheJobPromise` with true.

 After this step, the existing DOM objects (i.e.
 the currently referenced Cache, Request, and Response objects)
 should remain functional.

 4. Return `cacheJobPromise`.

#### 5.5.5. [`keys()`]
The [`keys()`] method steps are:

1. Let `promise` be [a new
 promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise).

2. Run the following substeps [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel):

 1. Let `cacheKeys` be the result of [getting the
 keys](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-getting-the-keys) of the [relevant name to cache
 map](#relevant-name-to-cache-map).

 The
 [items](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-item) in the result [ordered
 set](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-set) are in the order that their corresponding entry
 was added to the [name to cache
 map](#dfn-name-to-cache-map).

 2. Resolve `promise` with `cacheKeys`.

3. Return `promise`.

## 6. Security Considerations

### 6.1. Secure Context

[Service workers](#dfn-service-worker) *must* execute in [secure
contexts](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#secure-context). [Service worker
clients](#dfn-service-worker-client) *must* also be [secure
contexts](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#secure-context) to register a [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration), to get access to the [service worker
registrations](#dfn-service-worker-registration) and the [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker), to do messaging with the [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker), and to be manipulated by the [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker).

 This effectively means that [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker) and their [service worker
clients](#dfn-service-worker-client) need to be hosted over HTTPS. A user agent can allow
`localhost` (see [the
requirements](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-secure-contexts/#localhost)),
`127.0.0.0/8`, and `::1/128` for development purposes. The primary
reason for this restriction is to protect users from the [risks
associated with insecure
contexts](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-secure-contexts/#threat-risks).

### 6.2. Content Security Policy

Whenever a user agent invokes the [Run Service
Worker](#run-service-worker) algorithm with a [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker) `serviceWorker`:

- If `serviceWorker`'s [script
 resource](#dfn-script-resource) was delivered with a `Content-Security-Policy` HTTP
 header containing the value `policy`, the user agent *must*
 [enforce](https://wicg.github.io/csp-next/scripting-policy.html#scripting-policy-pair-enforced) `policy` for `serviceWorker`.

- If `serviceWorker`'s [script
 resource](#dfn-script-resource) was delivered with a
 `Content-Security-Policy-Report-Only` HTTP header containing the value
 `policy`, the user agent *must*
 [monitor](https://w3c.github.io/mediacapture-screen-share/#dfn-monitor) `policy` for `serviceWorker`.

The primary reason for this restriction is to mitigate a broad class of
content injection vulnerabilities, such as cross-site scripting (XSS).

### 6.3. Origin Relativity

#### 6.3.1. Origin restriction

*This section is non-normative.*

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker) executes in the registering [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client)'s
[origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin). One of the advanced concerns that major applications
would encounter is whether they can be hosted from a CDN. By definition,
these are servers in other places, often on other
[origins](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin). Therefore, [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker) cannot be hosted on CDNs. But they can include
resources via
[importScripts()](#importscripts-method). The reason for this restriction is that
[service workers](#dfn-service-worker) create the opportunity for a bad actor to turn a bad
day into a bad eternity.

#### 6.3.2. [`importScripts(urls)`]
When the [`importScripts(``urls``)`] method is called on a
[`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`](#serviceworkerglobalscope) object, the user agent *must* [import scripts into
worker global
scope](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#import-scripts-into-worker-global-scope), given this
[`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`](#serviceworkerglobalscope) object and `urls`, and with the following
[perform the fetch
hook](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#fetching-scripts-perform-fetch) steps, given the
[request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request) `request`:

1. Let `serviceWorker` be `request`'s
 [client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-client)'s [global
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-global)'s [service
 worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker).

2. Let `map` be `serviceWorker`'s [script
 resource
 map](#dfn-script-resource-map).

3. Let `url` be `request`'s
 [url](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url).

4. If `serviceWorker`'s
 [state](#dfn-state) is not
 \"`parsed`\" or \"`installing`\":

 1. Return `map`\[`url`\] if it
 [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists) and a [network
 error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error) otherwise.

5. If `map`\[`url`\]
 [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists):

 1. [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#set-append) `url` to
 `serviceWorker`'s [set of used
 scripts](#dfn-set-of-used-scripts).

 2. Return `map`\[`url`\].

6. Let `registration` be `serviceWorker`'s
 [containing service worker
 registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration).

7. Set `request`'s [service-workers
 mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-service-workers-mode) to \"`none`\".

8. Set `request`'s [cache
 mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-cache-mode) to \"`no-cache`\" if any of the following are true:

 - `registration`'s [update via cache
 mode](#dfn-update-via-cache) is \"`none`\".

 - The [current global
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#current-global-object)'s [force bypass cache for import scripts
 flag](#serviceworkerglobalscope-force-bypass-cache-for-import-scripts-flag) is set.

 - `registration` is
 [stale](#service-worker-registration-stale).

9. Let `response` be the result of
 [fetching](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch) `request`.

10. If `response`'s [cache
 state](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-cache-state) is not \"`local`\", set `registration`'s
 [last update check
 time](#dfn-last-update-check-time) to the current time.

11. If `response`'s [unsafe
 response](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/urls-and-fetching.html#unsafe-response) is a [bad import script
 response](#dfn-bad-import-script-response), then return a [network
 error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error).

12. [Set](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-set) `map`\[`url`\] to
 `response`.

13. [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#set-append) `url` to `serviceWorker`'s
 [set of used
 scripts](#dfn-set-of-used-scripts).

14. Set `serviceWorker`'s [classic scripts imported
 flag](#dfn-classic-scripts-imported-flag).

15. Return `response`.

### 6.4. Cross-Origin Resources and CORS

*This section is non-normative.*

Applications tend to cache items that come from a CDN or other
[origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin). It is possible to request many of them directly using
`<script>`, `<img>`, `<video>` and `<link>` elements. It would be hugely
limiting if this sort of runtime collaboration broke when offline.
Similarly, it is possible to
[fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch) many sorts of
off-[origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin) resources when appropriate CORS headers are set.
[Service workers](#dfn-service-worker) enable this by allowing
[`Caches`](#cache) to
[fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch) and cache off-origin items. Some restrictions apply,
however. First, unlike same-origin resources which are managed in the
[`Cache`](#cache) as
[`Response`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response) objects whose corresponding
[responses](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-response) are [basic filtered
response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-filtered-response-basic), the objects stored are
[`Response`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response) objects whose corresponding
[responses](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-response) are either [CORS filtered
responses](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-filtered-response-cors) or [opaque filtered
responses](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-filtered-response-opaque). They can be passed to
[`event.respondWith(r)`](#dom-fetchevent-respondwith) method in the same manner as the
[`Response`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response) objects whose corresponding
[responses](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-response) are [basic filtered
responses](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-filtered-response-basic), but cannot be meaningfully created programmatically.
These limitations are necessary to preserve the security invariants of
the platform. Allowing [`Caches`](#cache) to store them allows applications to avoid
re-architecting in most cases.

### 6.5. Path restriction

*This section is non-normative.*

In addition to the [origin restriction](#origin-restriction), service
workers are restricted by the
[path](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-path) of the service worker script. For example, a service
worker script at `https://www.example.com/~bob/sw.js` can be registered
for the [scope url](#dfn-scope-url) `https://www.example.com/~bob/` but not for the scope
`https://www.example.com/` or `https://www.example.com/~alice/`. This
provides some protection for sites that host multiple-user content in
separated directories on the same origin. However, the path restriction
is not considered a hard security boundary, as only origins are. Sites
are encouraged to use different origins to securely isolate segments of
the site if appropriate.

Servers can remove the path restriction by setting a
[Service-Worker-Allowed](#service-worker-allowed) header on the service worker script.

### 6.6. Service worker script request

*This section is non-normative.*

To further defend against malicious registration of a service worker on
a site, this specification requires that:

- The [Service-Worker](#service-worker) header is present on service worker script requests,
 and

- Service worker scripts are served with a [JavaScript MIME
 type](https://mimesniff.spec.whatwg.org/#javascript-mime-type).

### 6.7. Implementer Concerns

*This section is non-normative.*

The implementers are encouraged to note:

- Plug-ins should not load via [service
 workers](#dfn-service-worker). As plug-ins may get their security origins from
 their own urls, the embedding [service
 worker](#dfn-service-worker) cannot handle it. For this reason, the [Handle
 Fetch](#handle-fetch)
 algorithm makes `<embed>` and `<object>` requests immediately fallback
 to the network without dispatching
 [`fetch`](#service-worker-global-scope-fetch-event) event.

- Some of the legacy networking stack code may need to be carefully
 audited to understand the ramifications of interactions with [service
 workers](#dfn-service-worker).

### 6.8. Privacy

[Service workers](#dfn-service-worker) introduce new persistent storage features including
[registration
map](#dfn-scope-to-registration-map) (for [service worker
registrations](#dfn-service-worker-registration) and their [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker)), [request response
list](#dfn-request-response-list) and [name to cache
map](#dfn-name-to-cache-map) (for caches), and [script resource
map](#dfn-script-resource-map) (for script resources). In order to protect users from
any potential [unsanctioned
tracking](#biblio-unsanctioned-tracking "Unsanctioned Web Tracking")
threat, these persistent storages *should* be cleared when users intend
to clear them and *should* maintain and interoperate with existing user
controls e.g. purging all existing persistent storages.

## 7. Extensibility

Service Workers specification is extensible from other specifications.

### 7.1. Define API bound to Service Worker Registration

Specifications *may* define an API tied to a [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration) by using [partial
interface](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-partial-interface) definition to the
[`ServiceWorkerRegistration`](#serviceworkerregistration) interface where it *may* define the specification
specific attributes and methods:

```
partial interface ServiceWorkerRegistration {
 // e.g. define an API namespace
 readonly attribute APISpaceType APISpace;
 // e.g. define a method
 Promise<T> methodName(/* list of arguments */);
};
```

### 7.2. Define Functional Event

Specifications *may* define a [functional
event](#dfn-functional-events) by extending
[`ExtendableEvent`](#extendableevent) interface:

```
// e.g. define FunctionalEvent interface
interface FunctionalEvent : ExtendableEvent {
 // add a functional event’s own attributes and methods
};
```

### 7.3. Define Event Handler

Specifications *may* define an event handler attribute for the
corresponding [functional
event](#dfn-functional-events) using [partial
interface](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-partial-interface) definition to the
[`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`](#serviceworkerglobalscope) interface:

```
partial interface ServiceWorkerGlobalScope {
 attribute EventHandler onfunctionalevent;
};
```

### 7.4. Firing Functional Events

To request a [functional
event](#dfn-functional-events) dispatch to the [active
worker](#dfn-active-worker) of a [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration), specifications *should* invoke [Fire Functional
Event](#fire-functional-event).

## [Appendix A: Algorithms]
The following definitions are the user agent's internal data structures
used throughout the specification.

A [registration map] is an [ordered
map](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-map) where the keys are ([storage
key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#storage-key),
[serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer) [scope urls](#dfn-scope-url)) and the values are [service worker
registrations](#dfn-service-worker-registration).

A [job] is an
abstraction of one of register, update, and unregister request for a
[service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration).

A [job](#dfn-job) has a [job
type], which is one of *register*, *update*, and *unregister*.

A [job](#dfn-job) has a [storage
key] (a [storage
key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#storage-key)).

A [job](#dfn-job) has a [scope
url] (a
[URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url)).

A [job](#dfn-job) has a [script
url] (a
[URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url)).

A [job](#dfn-job) has a [worker
type] (\"`classic`\" or \"`module`\").

A [job](#dfn-job) has an [update via
cache mode], which is \"`imports`\",
\"`all`\", or \"`none`\".

A [job](#dfn-job) has a
[client] (a [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client)). It is initially null.

A [job](#dfn-job) has a
[referrer] (a
[URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url) or null).

A [job](#dfn-job) has a [job
promise] (a
[promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects)). It is initially null.

A [job](#dfn-job) has a [containing
job queue] (a [job
queue](#dfn-job-queue) or null).
It is initially null.

A [job](#dfn-job) has a [list of
equivalent jobs] (a list of
[jobs](#dfn-job)). It is initially
the empty list.

A [job](#dfn-job) has a [force
bypass cache flag]. It is initially unset.

Two [jobs](#dfn-job) are
[equivalent] when their [job type](#dfn-job-type) is the same and:

- For *register* and *update* [jobs](#dfn-job), their [scope
 url](#dfn-job-scope-url),
 [script url](#dfn-job-script-url), [worker
 type](#dfn-job-worker-type), and [update via cache
 mode](#dfn-job-update-via-cache-mode) are the same.

- For *unregister* [jobs](#dfn-job),
 their [scope url](#dfn-job-scope-url) is the same.

A [job queue] is a thread safe
[queue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#queue) used to synchronize the set of concurrent
[jobs](#dfn-job). The [job
queue](#dfn-job-queue) contains
[jobs](#dfn-job) as its
[items](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-item). A [job queue](#dfn-job-queue) is initially empty.

A [scope to job queue map] is an [ordered
map](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-map) where the keys are [scope
urls](#dfn-scope-url),
[serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer), and the values are [job
queues](#dfn-job-queue).

A [bad import script response] is a
[response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response) for which any of the following conditions are met:

- `response`'s
 [type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-type) is \"`error`\"

- `response`'s
 [status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status) is not an [ok
 status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#ok-status)

- The result of [extracting a MIME
 type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-extract-mime-type) from `response`'s [header
 list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list) is not a [JavaScript MIME
 type](https://mimesniff.spec.whatwg.org/#javascript-mime-type)

 Keep this definition in sync with [fetch a classic
 worker-imported
 script](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#fetch-a-classic-worker-imported-script).

A [race result] is a
[tuple](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#tuple) of a [routed
response](#race-result-routed-response) and [used
route](#race-result-used-route).

A [race result](#dfn-race-result) has an associated [routed
response] (a
[response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response)).

A [race result](#dfn-race-result) has an associated [used route] (a
[`RouterSourceEnum`](#enumdef-routersourceenum)).

### [[Create Job]]
Input

: `jobType`, a [job
 type](#dfn-job-type)

: `storage key`, a [storage
 key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#storage-key)

: `scopeURL`, a
 [URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url)

: `scriptURL`, a
 [URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url)

: `promise`, a
 [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects)

: `client`, a [service worker
 client](#dfn-service-worker-client)

Output

: `job`, a [job](#dfn-job)

1. Let `job` be a new [job](#dfn-job).

2. Set `job`'s [job
 type](#dfn-job-type) to
 `jobType`.

3. Set `job`'s [storage
 key](#job-storage-key) to
 `storage key`.

4. Set `job`'s [scope
 url](#dfn-job-scope-url) to `scopeURL`.

5. Set `job`'s [script
 url](#dfn-job-script-url) to `scriptURL`.

6. Set `job`'s [job
 promise](#dfn-job-promise)
 to `promise`.

7. Set `job`'s
 [client](#dfn-job-client)
 to `client`.

8. If `client` is not null, set `job`'s
 [referrer](#job-referrer) to
 `client`'s [creation
 URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-creation-url).

9. Return `job`.

### [[Schedule Job]]
Input

: `job`, a [job](#dfn-job)

Output

: none

1. Let `jobQueue` be null.

2. Let `jobScope` be `job`'s [scope
 url](#dfn-job-scope-url),
 [serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer).

3. If [scope to job queue
 map](#dfn-scope-to-job-queue-map)\[`jobScope`\] does not
 [exist](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists),
 [set](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-set) [scope to job queue
 map](#dfn-scope-to-job-queue-map)\[`jobScope`\] to a new [job
 queue](#dfn-job-queue).

4. Set `jobQueue` to [scope to job queue
 map](#dfn-scope-to-job-queue-map)\[`jobScope`\].

5. If `jobQueue` is empty, then:

 1. Set `job`'s [containing job
 queue](#dfn-containing-job-queue) to `jobQueue`, and
 [enqueue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#queue-enqueue) `job` to `jobQueue`.

 2. Invoke [Run Job](#run-job)
 with `jobQueue`.

6. Else:

 1. Let `lastJob` be the element at the back of
 `jobQueue`.

 2. If `job` is
 [equivalent](#dfn-job-equivalent) to `lastJob` and
 `lastJob`'s [job
 promise](#dfn-job-promise) has not settled, append `job` to
 `lastJob`'s [list of equivalent
 jobs](#dfn-job-list-of-equivalent-jobs).

 3. Else, set `job`'s [containing job
 queue](#dfn-containing-job-queue) to `jobQueue`, and
 [enqueue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#queue-enqueue) `job` to `jobQueue`.

### [[Run Job]]
Input

: jobQueue, a [job queue](#dfn-job-queue)

Output

: none

1. Assert: `jobQueue` [is not
 empty](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-is-empty).

2. [Queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) to run these steps:

 1. Let `job` be the first
 [item](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-item) in `jobQueue`.

 2. If `job`'s [job
 type](#dfn-job-type) is
 *register*, run [Register](#register) with `job` [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel).

 3. Else if `job`'s [job
 type](#dfn-job-type) is
 *update*, run [Update](#update)
 with `job` [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel).

 For a register job and an update job, the user
 agent delays queuing a task for running the job until after a
 [`DOMContentLoaded`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/indices.html#event-domcontentloaded) event has been dispatched to the document that
 initiated the job.

 4. Else if `job`'s [job
 type](#dfn-job-type) is
 *unregister*, run [Unregister](#unregister) with `job` [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel).

### [[Finish Job]]
Input

: `job`, a [job](#dfn-job)

Output

: none

1. Let `jobQueue` be `job`'s [containing job
 queue](#dfn-containing-job-queue).

2. Assert: the first
 [item](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-item) in `jobQueue` is `job`.

3. [Dequeue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#queue-dequeue) from `jobQueue`.

4. If `jobQueue` [is not
 empty](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-is-empty), invoke [Run Job](#run-job) with `jobQueue`.

### [[Resolve Job Promise]]
Input

: `job`, a [job](#dfn-job)

: `value`, any

Output

: none

1. If `job`'s
 [client](#dfn-job-client)
 is not null, [queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task), on `job`'s
 [client](#dfn-job-client)'s [responsible event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop) using the [DOM manipulation task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source), to run the following substeps:

 1. Let `convertedValue` be null.

 2. If `job`'s [job
 type](#dfn-job-type) is
 either *register* or *update*, set `convertedValue`
 to the result of [getting the service worker registration
 object](#get-the-service-worker-registration-object) that represents `value` in
 `job`'s
 [client](#dfn-job-client).

 3. Else, set `convertedValue` to `value`, in
 `job`'s
 [client](#dfn-job-client)'s
 [Realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object's-realm).

 4. Resolve `job`'s [job
 promise](#dfn-job-promise) with `convertedValue`.

2. For each `equivalentJob` in `job`'s [list of
 equivalent
 jobs](#dfn-job-list-of-equivalent-jobs):

 1. If `equivalentJob`'s
 [client](#dfn-job-client) is null, continue to the next iteration of the
 loop.

 2. [Queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task), on `equivalentJob`'s
 [client](#dfn-job-client)'s [responsible event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop) using the [DOM manipulation task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source), to run the following substeps:

 1. Let `convertedValue` be null.

 2. If `equivalentJob`'s [job
 type](#dfn-job-type)
 is either *register* or *update*, set
 `convertedValue` to the result of [getting the
 service worker registration
 object](#get-the-service-worker-registration-object) that represents `value` in
 `equivalentJob`'s
 [client](#dfn-job-client).

 3. Else, set `convertedValue` to `value`,
 in `equivalentJob`'s
 [client](#dfn-job-client)'s
 [Realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object's-realm).

 4. Resolve `equivalentJob`'s [job
 promise](#dfn-job-promise) with `convertedValue`.

### [[Reject Job Promise]]
Input

: `job`, a [job](#dfn-job)

: `errorData`, the information necessary to [create an
 exception](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-create-exception)

Output

: none

1. If `job`'s
 [client](#dfn-job-client)
 is not null, [queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task), on `job`'s
 [client](#dfn-job-client)'s [responsible event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop) using the [DOM manipulation task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source), to reject `job`'s [job
 promise](#dfn-job-promise) with a
 [new](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-create-exception)
 [exception](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-exception) with `errorData`, in `job`'s
 [client](#dfn-job-client)'s
 [Realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object's-realm).

2. For each `equivalentJob` in `job`'s [list of
 equivalent
 jobs](#dfn-job-list-of-equivalent-jobs):

 1. If `equivalentJob`'s
 [client](#dfn-job-client) is null,
 [continue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-continue).

 2. [Queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task), on `equivalentJob`'s
 [client](#dfn-job-client)'s [responsible event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop) using the [DOM manipulation task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source), to reject `equivalentJob`'s [job
 promise](#dfn-job-promise) with a
 [new](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-create-exception)
 [exception](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-exception) with `errorData`, in
 `equivalentJob`'s
 [client](#dfn-job-client)'s
 [Realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object's-realm).

### [[Start Register]]
Input

: `scopeURL`, a
 [URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url) or failure or null

: `scriptURL`, a
 [URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url) or failure

: `promise`, a
 [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects)

: `client`, a [service worker
 client](#dfn-service-worker-client)

: `referrer`, a
 [URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url)

: `workerType`, a [worker
 type](#dfn-job-worker-type)

: `updateViaCache`, an [update via cache
 mode](#dfn-update-via-cache)

Output

: none

1. If `scriptURL` is failure, reject `promise`
 with a `TypeError` and abort these steps.

2. Set `scriptURL`'s
 [fragment](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-fragment) to null.

 The user agent does not store the
 [fragment](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-fragment) of the script's url. This means that the
 [fragment](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-fragment) does not have an effect on identifying [service
 workers](#dfn-service-worker).

3. If `scriptURL`'s
 [scheme](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-scheme) is not one of \"`http`\" and \"`https`\", reject
 `promise` with a `TypeError` and abort these steps.

4. If any of the strings in `scriptURL`'s
 [path](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-path) contains either [ASCII
 case-insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-case-insensitive) \"`%2f`\" or [ASCII
 case-insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-case-insensitive) \"`%5c`\", reject `promise` with a
 `TypeError` and abort these steps.

5. If `scopeURL` is null, set `scopeURL` to the
 result of
 [parsing](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-parser) the string \"`./`\" with `scriptURL`.

 The scope url for the registration is set to the
 location of the service worker script by default.

6. If `scopeURL` is failure, reject `promise`
 with a `TypeError` and abort these steps.

7. Set `scopeURL`'s
 [fragment](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-fragment) to null.

 The user agent does not store the
 [fragment](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-fragment) of the scope url. This means that the
 [fragment](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-fragment) does not have an effect on identifying [service
 worker
 registrations](#dfn-service-worker-registration).

8. If `scopeURL`'s
 [scheme](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-scheme) is not one of \"`http`\" and \"`https`\", reject
 `promise` with a `TypeError` and abort these steps.

9. If any of the strings in `scopeURL`'s
 [path](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-path) contains either [ASCII
 case-insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-case-insensitive) \"`%2f`\" or [ASCII
 case-insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-case-insensitive) \"`%5c`\", reject `promise` with a
 `TypeError` and abort these steps.

10. Let `storage key` be the result of running [obtain a
 storage
 key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#obtain-a-storage-key) given `client`.

11. Let `job` be the result of running [Create
 Job](#create-job) with
 *register*, `storage key`, `scopeURL`,
 `scriptURL`, `promise`, and
 `client`.

12. Set `job`'s [worker
 type](#dfn-job-worker-type) to `workerType`.

13. Set `job`'s [update via cache
 mode](#dfn-job-update-via-cache-mode) to `updateViaCache`.

14. Set `job`'s
 [referrer](#job-referrer) to
 `referrer`.

15. Invoke [Schedule Job](#schedule-job) with `job`.

### [[Register]]
Input

: `job`, a [job](#dfn-job)

Output

: none

1. If the result of running [potentially trustworthy
 origin](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-secure-contexts/#potentially-trustworthy-origin) with the
 [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin) of `job`'s [script
 url](#dfn-job-script-url) as the argument is `Not Trusted`, then:

 1. Invoke [Reject Job
 Promise](#reject-job-promise) with `job` and
 \"[`SecurityError`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#securityerror)\"
 [`DOMException`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException).

 2. Invoke [Finish Job](#finish-job) with `job` and abort these steps.

2. If `job`'s [script
 url](#dfn-job-script-url)'s
 [origin](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-origin) and `job`'s
 [referrer](#job-referrer)'s
 [origin](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-origin) are not [same
 origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#same-origin), then:

 1. Invoke [Reject Job
 Promise](#reject-job-promise) with `job` and
 \"[`SecurityError`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#securityerror)\"
 [`DOMException`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException).

 2. Invoke [Finish Job](#finish-job) with `job` and abort these steps.

3. If `job`'s [scope
 url](#dfn-job-scope-url)'s
 [origin](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-origin) and `job`'s
 [referrer](#job-referrer)'s
 [origin](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-origin) are not [same
 origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#same-origin), then:

 1. Invoke [Reject Job
 Promise](#reject-job-promise) with `job` and
 \"[`SecurityError`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#securityerror)\"
 [`DOMException`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException).

 2. Invoke [Finish Job](#finish-job) with `job` and abort these steps.

4. Let `registration` be the result of running [Get
 Registration](#get-registration) given `job`'s [storage
 key](#job-storage-key)
 and `job`'s [scope
 url](#dfn-job-scope-url).

5. If `registration` is not null, then:

 1. Let `newestWorker` be the result of running the [Get
 Newest Worker](#get-newest-worker) algorithm passing `registration` as
 the argument.

 2. If `newestWorker` is not null, `job`'s
 [script url](#dfn-job-script-url)
 [equals](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-equals) `newestWorker`'s [script
 url](#dfn-script-url),
 `job`'s [worker
 type](#dfn-job-worker-type) equals `newestWorker`'s
 [type](#dfn-type), and
 `job`'s [update via cache
 mode](#dfn-job-update-via-cache-mode)'s value equals `registration`'s
 [update via cache
 mode](#dfn-update-via-cache), then:

 1. Invoke [Resolve Job
 Promise](#resolve-job-promise) with `job` and
 `registration`.

 2. Invoke [Finish Job](#finish-job) with `job` and abort these
 steps.

6. Else:

 1. Invoke [Set
 Registration](#set-registration) algorithm with `job`'s [storage
 key](#job-storage-key), `job`'s [scope
 url](#dfn-job-scope-url), and `job`'s [update via cache
 mode](#dfn-job-update-via-cache-mode).

7. Invoke [Update](#update) algorithm
 passing `job` as the argument.

### [[Update]]
Input

: `job`, a [job](#dfn-job)

Output

: none

1. Let `registration` be the result of running [Get
 Registration](#get-registration) given `job`'s [storage
 key](#job-storage-key)
 and `job`'s [scope
 url](#dfn-job-scope-url).

2. If `registration` is null, then:

 1. Invoke [Reject Job
 Promise](#reject-job-promise) with `job` and `TypeError`.

 2. Invoke [Finish Job](#finish-job) with `job` and abort these steps.

3. Let `newestWorker` be the result of running [Get Newest
 Worker](#get-newest-worker) algorithm passing `registration` as the
 argument.

4. If `job`'s [job
 type](#dfn-job-type) is
 *update*, and `newestWorker` is not null and its [script
 url](#dfn-script-url) does
 not
 [equal](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-equals) `job`'s [script
 url](#dfn-job-script-url), then:

 1. Invoke [Reject Job
 Promise](#reject-job-promise) with `job` and `TypeError`.

 2. Invoke [Finish Job](#finish-job) with `job` and abort these steps.

5. Let `hasUpdatedResources` be false.

6. Let `updatedResourceMap` be an [ordered
 map](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-map) where the
 [keys](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-getting-the-keys) are
 [URLs](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url) and the
 [values](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-getting-the-values) are
 [responses](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response).

7. Switching on `job`'s [worker
 type](#dfn-job-worker-type), run these substeps with the following options:

 \"`classic`\"

 : [Fetch a classic worker
 script](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#fetch-a-classic-worker-script) given `job`'s
 [serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer) [script
 url](#dfn-job-script-url), `job`'s
 [client](#dfn-job-client), \"`serviceworker`\", and the to-be-created
 [environment settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object) for this service worker.

 \"`module`\"

 : [Fetch a module worker script
 graph](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#fetch-a-module-worker-script-tree) given `job`'s
 [serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer) [script
 url](#dfn-job-script-url), `job`'s
 [client](#dfn-job-client), \"`serviceworker`\", \"`omit`\", and the
 to-be-created [environment settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object) for this service worker.

 (#issue-02e2fe82) Using the to-be-created [environment
 settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object) rather than a concrete [environment settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object). This is used due to the unique processing model of
 service workers compared to the processing model of other [web
 workers](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#workers). The script fetching algorithms of HTML standard
 originally designed for other [web
 workers](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#workers) require an [environment settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object) of the execution environment, but service workers
 fetch a script separately in the [Update](#update) algorithm before the script later runs multiple
 times through the [Run Service
 Worker](#run-service-worker) algorithm.

 (#issue-9d9e65b5) The [fetch a classic worker
 script](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#fetch-a-classic-worker-script) algorithm and the [fetch a module worker script
 graph](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#fetch-a-module-worker-script-tree) algorithm in HTML take `job`'s
 [client](#dfn-job-client)
 as an argument. `job`'s
 [client](#dfn-job-client)
 is null when passed from the [Soft
 Update](#soft-update)
 algorithm.

 To [perform the fetch
 hook](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#fetching-scripts-perform-fetch) given `request`, run the following
 steps:

 1. Append \``Service-Worker`\`/\``script`\` to
 `request`'s [header
 list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list).

 See the definition of the Service-Worker header
 in Appendix B: Extended HTTP headers.

 2. Set `request`'s [cache
 mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-cache-mode) to \"`no-cache`\" if any of the following are
 true:

 - `registration`'s [update via cache
 mode](#dfn-update-via-cache) is not \"`all`\".

 - `job`'s [force bypass cache
 flag](#dfn-job-force-bypass-cache-flag) is set.

 - `newestWorker` is not null and
 `registration` is
 [stale](#service-worker-registration-stale).

 Even if the cache mode is not set to
 \"`no-cache`\", the user agent obeys Cache-Control header's
 max-age value in the network layer to determine if it should
 bypass the browser cache.

 3. Set `request`'s [service-workers
 mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-service-workers-mode) to \"`none`\".

 4. If the
 [isTopLevel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#fetching-scripts-is-top-level) flag is unset, then return the result of
 [fetching](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch) `request`.

 5. Set `request`'s [redirect
 mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-redirect-mode) to \"`error`\".

 6. [Fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch) `request`, and asynchronously wait
 to run the remaining steps as part of fetch's
 [processResponse](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#process-response) for the
 [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response) `response`.

 7. [Extract a MIME
 type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-extract-mime-type) from the `response`'s [header
 list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list). If this MIME type (ignoring parameters) is not
 a [JavaScript MIME
 type](https://mimesniff.spec.whatwg.org/#javascript-mime-type), then:

 1. Invoke [Reject Job
 Promise](#reject-job-promise) with `job` and
 \"[`SecurityError`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#securityerror)\"
 [`DOMException`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException).

 2. Asynchronously complete these steps with a [network
 error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error).

 8. Let `serviceWorkerAllowed` be the result of
 [extracting header list
 values](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#extract-header-list-values) given \``Service-Worker-Allowed`\` and
 `response`'s [header
 list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list).

 See the definition of the
 [Service-Worker-Allowed](#service-worker-allowed) header in Appendix B: Extended HTTP headers.

 9. Set `policyContainer` to the result of [creating a
 policy container from a fetch
 response](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#creating-a-policy-container-from-a-fetch-response) given `response`.

 10. If `serviceWorkerAllowed` is failure, then:

 1. Asynchronously complete these steps with a [network
 error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error).

 11. Let `scopeURL` be `registration`'s [scope
 url](#dfn-scope-url).

 12. Let `maxScopeString` be null.

 13. If `serviceWorkerAllowed` is null, then:

 1. Let `resolvedScope` be the result of
 [parsing](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-parser) \"`./`\" using `job`'s [script
 url](#dfn-job-script-url) as the [base
 URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-script-base-url).

 2. Set `maxScopeString` to \"`/`\", followed by the
 strings in `resolvedScope`'s
 [path](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-path) (including empty strings), separated from
 each other by \"`/`\".

 The final item in
 `resolvedScope`'s
 [path](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-path) will always be an empty string, so
 `maxScopeString` will have a trailing \"`/`\".

 14. Else:

 1. Let `maxScope` be the result of
 [parsing](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-parser) `serviceWorkerAllowed` using
 `job`'s [script
 url](#dfn-job-script-url) as the [base
 URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-script-base-url).

 2. If `maxScope`'s
 [origin](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-origin) is `job`'s [script
 url](#dfn-job-script-url)'s
 [origin](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-origin), then:

 1. Set `maxScopeString` to \"`/`\", followed by
 the strings in `maxScope`'s
 [path](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-path) (including empty strings), separated
 from each other by \"`/`\".

 15. Let `scopeString` be \"`/`\", followed by the strings
 in `scopeURL`'s
 [path](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-path) (including empty strings), separated from each
 other by \"`/`\".

 16. If `maxScopeString` is null or
 `scopeString` does not start with
 `maxScopeString`, then:

 1. Invoke [Reject Job
 Promise](#reject-job-promise) with `job` and
 \"[`SecurityError`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#securityerror)\"
 [`DOMException`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException).

 2. Asynchronously complete these steps with a [network
 error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error).

 17. Let `url` be `request`'s
 [url](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url).

 18. Set `updatedResourceMap`\[`url`\] to
 `response`.

 19. If `response`'s [cache
 state](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-cache-state) is not \"`local`\", set
 `registration`'s [last update check
 time](#dfn-last-update-check-time) to the current time.

 20. Set `hasUpdatedResources` to true if any of the
 following are true:

 - `newestWorker` is null.

 - `newestWorker`'s [script
 url](#dfn-script-url) is not `url` or
 `newestWorker`'s
 [type](#dfn-type) is not
 `job`'s [worker
 type](#dfn-job-worker-type).

 - `newestWorker`'s [script resource
 map](#dfn-script-resource-map)\[`url`\]\'s
 [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body) is not byte-for-byte identical with
 `response`'s
 [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body).

 21. If `hasUpdatedResources` is false and
 `newestWorker`'s [classic scripts imported
 flag](#dfn-classic-scripts-imported-flag) is set, then:

 The following checks to see if an imported
 script has been updated, since the main script has not changed.

 1. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-iterate) `importUrl` →
 `storedResponse` of `newestWorker`'s
 [script resource
 map](#dfn-script-resource-map):

 1. If `importUrl` is `url`, then
 continue.

 2. Let `importRequest` be a new
 [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request) whose
 [url](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url) is `importUrl`,
 [client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-client) is `job`'s
 [client](#dfn-job-client),
 [destination](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-destination) is \"`script`\", [parser
 metadata](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-parser-metadata) is \"`not parser-inserted`\", and whose
 [use-URL-credentials
 flag](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-use-url-credentials-flag) is set.

 3. Set `importRequest`'s [cache
 mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-cache-mode) to \"`no-cache`\" if any of the
 following are true:

 - `registration`'s [update via cache
 mode](#dfn-update-via-cache) is \"`none`\".

 - `job`'s [force bypass cache
 flag](#dfn-job-force-bypass-cache-flag) is set.

 - `registration` is
 [stale](#service-worker-registration-stale).

 4. Let `fetchedResponse` be the result of
 [fetching](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch) `importRequest`.

 5. Set
 `updatedResourceMap`\[`importRequest`'s
 [url](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url)\] to `fetchedResponse`.

 6. Set `fetchedResponse` to
 `fetchedResponse`'s [unsafe
 response](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/urls-and-fetching.html#unsafe-response).

 7. If `fetchedResponse`'s [cache
 state](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-cache-state) is not \"`local`\", set
 `registration`'s [last update check
 time](#dfn-last-update-check-time) to the current time.

 8. If `fetchedResponse` is a [bad import script
 response](#dfn-bad-import-script-response), continue.

 Bad responses for
 [importScripts()](#importscripts-method) are ignored for the purpose of
 the byte-to-byte check. Only good responses for the
 incumbent worker and good responses for the potential
 update worker are considered. See [issue
 #1374](https://github.com/w3c/ServiceWorker/issues/1374)
 for some rationale.

 9. If `fetchedResponse`'s
 [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body) is not byte-for-byte identical with
 `storedResponse`'s [unsafe
 response](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/urls-and-fetching.html#unsafe-response)'s
 [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body), set `hasUpdatedResources`
 to true.

 The control does not break the loop in
 this step to continue with all the imported scripts to
 populate the cache.

 22. Asynchronously complete these steps with `response`.

 When the algorithm asynchronously completes, continue the rest of
 these steps, with `script` being the asynchronous
 completion value.

8. If `script` is null or [Is Async
 Module](#is-async-module)
 with `script`'s
 [record](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-script-record), `script`'s [base
 URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-script-base-url), and « » is true, then:

 1. Invoke [Reject Job
 Promise](#reject-job-promise) with `job` and `TypeError`.

 This will do nothing if [Reject Job
 Promise](#reject-job-promise) was previously invoked with
 \"[`SecurityError`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#securityerror)\"
 [`DOMException`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException).

 2. If `newestWorker` is null, then
 [remove](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-remove) [registration
 map](#dfn-scope-to-registration-map)\[(`registration`'s [storage
 key](#service-worker-registration-storage-key),
 [serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer) `scopeURL`)\].

 3. Invoke [Finish Job](#finish-job) with `job` and abort these steps.

9. If `hasUpdatedResources` is false, then:

 1. Set `registration`'s [update via cache
 mode](#dfn-update-via-cache) to `job`'s [update via cache
 mode](#dfn-job-update-via-cache-mode).

 2. Invoke [Resolve Job
 Promise](#resolve-job-promise) with `job` and
 `registration`.

 3. Invoke [Finish Job](#finish-job) with `job` and abort these steps.

10. Let `worker` be a new [service
 worker](#dfn-service-worker).

11. Set `worker`'s [script
 url](#dfn-script-url) to
 `job`'s [script
 url](#dfn-job-script-url), `worker`'s [script
 resource](#dfn-script-resource) to `script`, `worker`'s
 [type](#dfn-type) to
 `job`'s [worker
 type](#dfn-job-worker-type), and `worker`'s [script resource
 map](#dfn-script-resource-map) to `updatedResourceMap`.

12. Append `url` to `worker`'s [set of used
 scripts](#dfn-set-of-used-scripts).

13. Set `worker`'s [script
 resource](#dfn-script-resource)'s [policy
 container](#dfn-policy-container) to `policyContainer`.

14. Let `forceBypassCache` be true if `job`'s
 [force bypass cache
 flag](#dfn-job-force-bypass-cache-flag) is set, and false otherwise.

15. Let `runResult` be the result of running the [Run Service
 Worker](#run-service-worker) algorithm with `worker` and
 `forceBypassCache`.

16. If `runResult` is *failure* or an [abrupt
 completion](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-completion-record-specification-type), then:

 1. Invoke [Reject Job
 Promise](#reject-job-promise) with `job` and `TypeError`.

 2. If `newestWorker` is null, then
 [remove](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-remove) [registration
 map](#dfn-scope-to-registration-map)\[(`registration`'s [storage
 key](#service-worker-registration-storage-key),
 [serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer) `scopeURL`)\].

 3. Invoke [Finish Job](#finish-job) with `job`.

17. Else, invoke [Install](#install)
 algorithm with `job`, `worker`, and
 `registration` as its arguments.

### [[Soft Update]]
The user agent *may* call this as often as it likes to check for
updates.

Input

: `registration`, a [service worker
 registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration)

: `forceBypassCache`, an optional boolean, false by default

 Implementers may use `forceBypassCache`
 to aid debugging (e.g. invocations from developer tools), and other
 specifications that extend service workers may also use the flag on
 their own needs.

Output

: None

1. Let `newestWorker` be the result of running [Get Newest
 Worker](#get-newest-worker) algorithm passing `registration` as its
 argument.

2. If `newestWorker` is null, abort these steps.

3. Let `job` be the result of running [Create
 Job](#create-job) with
 *update*, `registration`'s [storage
 key](#service-worker-registration-storage-key), `registration`'s [scope
 url](#dfn-scope-url),
 `newestWorker`'s [script
 url](#dfn-script-url),
 null, and null.

4. Set `job`'s [worker
 type](#dfn-job-worker-type) to `newestWorker`'s
 [type](#dfn-type).

5. Set `job`'s [force bypass cache
 flag](#dfn-job-force-bypass-cache-flag) if `forceBypassCache` is true.

6. Invoke [Schedule Job](#schedule-job) with `job`.

### [[Install]]
Input

: `job`, a [job](#dfn-job)

: `worker`, a [service
 worker](#dfn-service-worker)

: `registration`, a [service worker
 registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration)

Output

: none

1. Let `installFailed` be false.

2. Let `newestWorker` be the result of running [Get Newest
 Worker](#get-newest-worker) algorithm passing `registration` as its
 argument.

3. Set `registration`'s [update via cache
 mode](#dfn-update-via-cache) to `job`'s [update via cache
 mode](#dfn-job-update-via-cache-mode).

4. Run the [Update Registration
 State](#update-registration-state) algorithm passing `registration`,
 \"`installing`\" and `worker` as the arguments.

5. Run the [Update Worker
 State](#update-worker-state) algorithm passing `registration`'s
 [installing
 worker](#dfn-installing-worker) and \"`installing`\" as the arguments.

6. Assert: `job`'s [job
 promise](#dfn-job-promise) is not null.

7. Invoke [Resolve Job
 Promise](#resolve-job-promise) with `job` and
 `registration`.

8. Let `settingsObjects` be all [environment settings
 objects](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object) whose
 [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin) is `registration`'s [scope
 url](#dfn-scope-url)'s
 [origin](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-origin).

9. For each `settingsObject` of
 `settingsObjects`, [queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) on `settingsObject`'s [responsible event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop) in the [DOM manipulation task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source) to run the following steps:

 1. Let `registrationObjects` be every
 [`ServiceWorkerRegistration`](#serviceworkerregistration) object in `settingsObject`'s
 [realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object's-realm), whose [service worker
 registration](#serviceworkerregistration-service-worker-registration) is `registration`.

 2. For each `registrationObject` of
 `registrationObjects`, [fire an
 event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-fire) on `registrationObject` named
 `updatefound`.

10. Let `installingWorker` be `registration`'s
 [installing
 worker](#dfn-installing-worker).

11. If the result of running the [Should Skip
 Event](#should-skip-event) algorithm with `installingWorker` and
 \"install\" is false, then:

 1. Let `forceBypassCache` be true if `job`'s
 [force bypass cache
 flag](#dfn-job-force-bypass-cache-flag) is set, and false otherwise.

 2. If the result of running the [Run Service
 Worker](#run-service-worker) algorithm with `installingWorker`
 and `forceBypassCache` is *failure*, then:

 1. Set `installFailed` to true.

 3. Else:

 1. [Queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) `task` on
 `installingWorker`'s [event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop) using the [DOM manipulation task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source) to run the following steps:

 1. Let `e` be the result of [creating an
 event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-create) with
 [`InstallEvent`](#installevent).

 2. Initialize `e`'s
 [`type`](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-event-type) attribute to
 [`install`](#service-worker-global-scope-install-event).

 3. [Dispatch](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-dispatch) `e` at
 `installingWorker`'s [global
 object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object).

 4. *WaitForAsynchronousExtensions*: Run the following
 substeps [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel):

 1. [Wait until `e` is not
 [active](#extendableevent-active).]

 2. If `e`'s [timed out
 flag](#extendableevent-timed-out-flag) is set, set
 `installFailed` to true.

 3. Let `p` be the result of [getting a
 promise to wait for
 all](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#waiting-for-all-promise) of `e`'s [extend
 lifetime
 promises](#extendableevent-extend-lifetime-promises).

 4. [Upon
 rejection](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#upon-rejection) of `p`, set
 `installFailed` to true.

 If `task` is discarded, set
 `installFailed` to true.

 2. Wait for `task` to have executed or been
 discarded.

 3. Wait for the step labeled *WaitForAsynchronousExtensions* to
 complete.

12. If `installFailed` is true, then:

 1. Run the [Update Worker
 State](#update-worker-state) algorithm passing `registration`'s
 [installing
 worker](#dfn-installing-worker) and \"`redundant`\" as the arguments.

 2. Run the [Update Registration
 State](#update-registration-state) algorithm passing `registration`,
 \"`installing`\" and null as the arguments.

 3. If `newestWorker` is null, then
 [remove](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-remove) [registration
 map](#dfn-scope-to-registration-map)\[(`registration`'s [storage
 key](#service-worker-registration-storage-key),
 [serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer) `registration`'s [scope
 url](#dfn-scope-url))\].

 4. Invoke [Finish Job](#finish-job) with `job` and abort these steps.

13. Let `map` be `registration`'s [installing
 worker](#dfn-installing-worker)'s [script resource
 map](#dfn-script-resource-map).

14. Let `usedSet` be `registration`'s [installing
 worker](#dfn-installing-worker)'s [set of used
 scripts](#dfn-set-of-used-scripts).

15. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-iterate) `url` of `map`:

 1. If `usedSet` does not
 [contain](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-contain) `url`, then
 [remove](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-remove) `map`\[`url`\].

16. If `registration`'s [waiting
 worker](#dfn-waiting-worker) is not null, then:

 1. [Terminate](#terminate-service-worker) `registration`'s [waiting
 worker](#dfn-waiting-worker).

 2. Run the [Update Worker
 State](#update-worker-state) algorithm passing `registration`'s
 [waiting
 worker](#dfn-waiting-worker) and \"`redundant`\" as the arguments.

17. Run the [Update Registration
 State](#update-registration-state) algorithm passing `registration`,
 \"`waiting`\" and `registration`'s [installing
 worker](#dfn-installing-worker) as the arguments.

18. Run the [Update Registration
 State](#update-registration-state) algorithm passing `registration`,
 \"`installing`\" and null as the arguments.

19. Run the [Update Worker
 State](#update-worker-state) algorithm passing `registration`'s
 [waiting worker](#dfn-waiting-worker) and \"`installed`\" as the arguments.

20. Invoke [Finish Job](#finish-job) with `job`.

21. Wait for all the
 [tasks](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-task)
 [queued](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) by [Update Worker
 State](#update-worker-state) invoked in this algorithm to have executed.

22. Invoke [Try Activate](#try-activate) with `registration`.

 If [Try
 Activate](#try-activate)
 does not trigger [Activate](#activate) here, [Activate](#activate) is tried again when the last client controlled by
 the existing [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker) is
 [unloaded](#handle-service-worker-client-unload),
 [`skipWaiting()`](#dom-serviceworkerglobalscope-skipwaiting) is asynchronously called, or the [extend lifetime
 promises](#extendableevent-extend-lifetime-promises) for the existing [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker) settle.

### [[Activate]]
Input

: `registration`, a [service worker
 registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration)

Output

: None

1. If `registration`'s [waiting
 worker](#dfn-waiting-worker) is null, abort these steps.

2. If `registration`'s [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker) is not null, then:

 1. [Terminate](#terminate-service-worker) `registration`'s [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker).

 2. Run the [Update Worker
 State](#update-worker-state) algorithm passing `registration`'s
 [active worker](#dfn-active-worker) and \"`redundant`\" as the arguments.

3. Run the [Update Registration
 State](#update-registration-state) algorithm passing `registration`,
 \"`active`\" and `registration`'s [waiting
 worker](#dfn-waiting-worker) as the arguments.

4. Run the [Update Registration
 State](#update-registration-state) algorithm passing `registration`,
 \"`waiting`\" and null as the arguments.

5. Run the [Update Worker
 State](#update-worker-state) algorithm passing `registration`'s
 [active worker](#dfn-active-worker) and \"`activating`\" as the arguments.

 Once an active worker is activating, neither a
 runtime script error nor a force termination of the active worker
 prevents the active worker from getting activated.

 Make sure to design activation handlers to do
 non-essential work (like cleanup). This is because activation
 handlers may not all run to completion, especially in the case of
 browser termination during activation. A Service Worker should be
 designed to function properly, even if the activation handlers do
 not all complete successfully.

6. Let `matchedClients` be a
 [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list) of [service worker
 clients](#dfn-service-worker-client) whose [creation
 URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-creation-url)
 [matches](#match-service-worker-registration) `registration`'s [storage
 key](#service-worker-registration-storage-key) and `registration`'s [scope
 url](#dfn-scope-url).

7. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) `client` of `matchedClients`,
 [queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) on `client`'s [responsible event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop), using the [DOM manipulation task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source), to run the following substeps:

 1. Let `readyPromise` be `client`'s [global
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-global)'s
