 [`ServiceWorkerContainer`](#serviceworkercontainer) object's [ready
 promise](#serviceworkercontainer-ready-promise).

 2. If `readyPromise` is null, then
 [continue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-continue).

 3. [If `readyPromise` is pending, resolve
 `readyPromise` with the the result of [getting the
 service worker registration
 object](#get-the-service-worker-registration-object) that represents `registration` in
 `readyPromise`'s [relevant settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object)].

8. For each [service worker
 client](#dfn-service-worker-client) `client` who is
 [using](#dfn-use)
 `registration`:

 1. Set `client`'s [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker) to `registration`'s [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker).

 2. Invoke [Notify Controller
 Change](#notify-controller-change) algorithm with `client` as the
 argument.

9. Let `activeWorker` be `registration`'s [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker).

10. If the result of running the [Should Skip
 Event](#should-skip-event) algorithm with `activeWorker` and
 \"activate\" is false, then:

 1. If the result of running the [Run Service
 Worker](#run-service-worker) algorithm with `activeWorker` is not
 *failure*, then:

 1. [Queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) `task` on
 `activeWorker`'s [event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop) using the [DOM manipulation task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source) to run the following steps:

 1. Let `e` be the result of [creating an
 event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-create) with
 [`ExtendableEvent`](#extendableevent).

 2. Initialize `e`'s
 [`type`](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-event-type) attribute to
 [`activate`](#service-worker-global-scope-activate-event).

 3. [Dispatch](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-dispatch) `e` at
 `activeWorker`'s [global
 object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object).

 4. [*WaitForAsynchronousExtensions*: Wait, [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel), until `e` is not
 [active](#extendableevent-active).]

 2. Wait for `task` to have executed or been
 discarded.

 3. Wait for the step labeled *WaitForAsynchronousExtensions* to
 complete.

11. Run the [Update Worker
 State](#update-worker-state) algorithm passing `registration`'s
 [active worker](#dfn-active-worker) and \"`activated`\" as the arguments.

### [[Try Activate]]
Input

: `registration`, a [service worker
 registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration)

Output

: None

1. If `registration`'s [waiting
 worker](#dfn-waiting-worker) is null, return.

2. If `registration`'s [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker) is not null and `registration`'s [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker)'s [state](#dfn-state) is \"`activating`\", return.

 If the existing active worker is still in
 activating state, the activation of the waiting worker is delayed.

3. Invoke [Activate](#activate)
 with `registration` if either of the following is true:

 - `registration`'s [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker) is null.

 - The result of running [Service Worker Has No Pending
 Events](#service-worker-has-no-pending-events) with `registration`'s [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker) is true, and no [service worker
 client](#dfn-service-worker-client) is [using](#dfn-use) `registration` or
 `registration`'s [waiting
 worker](#dfn-waiting-worker)'s [skip waiting
 flag](#dfn-skip-waiting-flag) is set.

### [[Setup ServiceWorkerGlobalScope]]
Input

: `serviceWorker`, a [service
 worker](#dfn-service-worker)

Output

: a
 [`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`](#serviceworkerglobalscope) object or null

 This algorithm returns a
[`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`](#serviceworkerglobalscope) usable for a CSP check, or null. If
`serviceWorker` has an active
[`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`](#serviceworkerglobalscope), then it is returned. Otherwise, the object will be
newly created.

This algorithm does the minimal setup for the service worker that is
necessary to create something usable for security checks like CSP and
COEP. This specification ensures that this algorithm is called before
any such checks are performed.

In specifications, such security checks require creating a
[`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`](#serviceworkerglobalscope), a [relevant settings
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object), a
[realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-global-object-realm), and an
[agent](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#agent).
In implementations, the amount of work required might be much less.
Therefore, implementations could do less work in their equivalent of
this algorithm, and more work in [Run Service
Worker](#run-service-worker), as long as the results are observably equivalent. (And
in particular, as long as all security checks have the same result.)

1. Let `unsafeCreationTime` be the [unsafe shared current
 time](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dfn-unsafe-shared-current-time).

2. If `serviceWorker` is
 [running](#service-worker-running), then return `serviceWorker`'s [global
 object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object).

3. If `serviceWorker`'s
 [state](#dfn-state) is
 \"`redundant`\", then return null.

4. If `serviceWorker`'s [global
 object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object) is not null, then return
 `serviceWorker`'s [global
 object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object).

5. Assert: `serviceWorker`'s [start
 status](#service-worker-start-status) is null.

6. Let `setupFailed` be false.

7. Let `globalObject` be null.

8. Let `agent` be the result of [obtaining a service worker
 agent](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#obtain-a-service-worker-agent), and run the following steps in that context:

 1. Let `realmExecutionContext` be the result of
 [creating a new
 realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#creating-a-new-javascript-realm) given `agent` and the following
 customizations:

 - For the global object, create a new
 [`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`](#serviceworkerglobalscope) object. Let `workerGlobalScope` be
 the created object.

 2. Let `settingsObject` be a new [environment settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object) whose algorithms are defined as follows:

 The [realm execution context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#realm-execution-context)

 : Return `realmExecutionContext`.

 The [module map](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-module-map)

 : Return `workerGlobalScope`'s [module
 map](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#concept-workerglobalscope-module-map).

 The [API base URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#api-base-url)

 : Return `serviceWorker`'s [script
 url](#dfn-script-url).

 The [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin)

 : Return its registering [service worker
 client](#dfn-service-worker-client)'s
 [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin).

 The [has cross-site ancestor](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-has-cross-site-ancestor)

 : Return its registering [service worker
 client](#dfn-service-worker-client)'s [has cross-site
 ancestor](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-has-cross-site-ancestor).

 The [policy container](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-policy-container)

 : Return `workerGlobalScope`'s [policy
 container](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#concept-workerglobalscope-policy-container).

 The [time origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-time-origin)

 : Return the result of
 [coarsening](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dfn-coarsen-time) `unsafeCreationTime` given
 `workerGlobalScope`'s [cross-origin isolated
 capability](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#concept-workerglobalscope-cross-origin-isolated-capability).

 3. Set `settingsObject`'s
 [id](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-id) to a new unique opaque string, [creation
 URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-creation-url) to `serviceWorker`'s [script
 url](#dfn-script-url),
 [top-level creation
 URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-top-level-creation-url) to null, [top-level
 origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-top-level-origin) to an
 [implementation-defined](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#implementation-defined) value, [target browsing
 context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-target-browsing-context) to null, and [active service
 worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker) to null.

 4. Set `workerGlobalScope`'s
 [url](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#concept-workerglobalscope-url) to `serviceWorker`'s [script
 url](#dfn-script-url).

 5. Set `workerGlobalScope`'s [policy
 container](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#concept-workerglobalscope-policy-container) to `serviceWorker`'s [script
 resource](#dfn-script-resource)'s [policy
 container](#dfn-policy-container).

 6. Set `workerGlobalScope`'s
 [type](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#concept-workerglobalscope-type) to `serviceWorker`'s
 [type](#dfn-type).

 7. Create a new
 [`WorkerLocation`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#workerlocation) object and associate it with
 `workerGlobalScope`.

 8. If the [run CSP initialization for a global
 object](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-csp/#run-global-object-csp-initialization) algorithm returns \"`Blocked`\" when executed
 upon `workerGlobalScope`, set
 `setupFailed` to true and abort these steps.

 9. Set `globalObject` to `workerGlobalScope`.

9. Wait for `globalObject` is not null, or for
 `setupFailed` to be true.

10. If `setupFailed` is true, then return null.

11. Return `globalObject`.

### [[Run Service Worker]]
Input

: `serviceWorker`, a [service
 worker](#dfn-service-worker)

: `forceBypassCache`, an optional boolean, false by default

Output

: a
 [Completion](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-completion-record-specification-type) or *failure*

 This algorithm blocks until the service worker is
[running](#service-worker-running) or fails to start.

1. If `serviceWorker` is
 [running](#service-worker-running), then return `serviceWorker`'s [start
 status](#service-worker-start-status).

2. If `serviceWorker`'s
 [state](#dfn-state) is
 \"`redundant`\", then return *failure*.

3. Assert: `serviceWorker`'s [start
 status](#service-worker-start-status) is null.

4. Let `script` be `serviceWorker`'s [script
 resource](#dfn-script-resource).

5. Assert: `script` is not null.

6. Let `startFailed` be false.

7. Let `workerGlobalScope` be `serviceWorker`'s
 [global
 object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object).

8. If `workerGlobalScope` is null:

 1. Set `workerGlobalScope` to be the result of running
 the [Setup
 ServiceWorkerGlobalScope](#setup-serviceworkerglobalscope) algorithm with `serviceWorker`.

 2. If `workerGlobalScope` is null, then return
 *failure*.

 3. Set `serviceWorker`'s [global
 object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object) to `workerGlobalScope`.

9. Obtain agent for `workerGlobalScope`'s [realm execution
 context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#realm-execution-context), and run the following steps in that context:

 1. Set `workerGlobalScope`'s [force bypass cache for
 import scripts
 flag](#serviceworkerglobalscope-force-bypass-cache-for-import-scripts-flag) if `forceBypassCache` is true.

 2. If `serviceWorker` is an [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker), and there are any
 [tasks](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-task) queued in `serviceWorker`'s
 [containing service worker
 registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration)'s [task
 queues](#dfn-service-worker-registration-task-queue),
 [queue](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) them to `serviceWorker`'s [event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop)'s [task
 queues](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#task-queue) in the same order using their original [task
 sources](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#task-source).

 3. Let `evaluationStatus` be null.

 4. If `script` is a [classic
 script](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#classic-script), then:

 1. Set `evaluationStatus` to the result of [running
 the classic
 script](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#run-a-classic-script) `script`.

 2. If `evaluationStatus`.\[\[Value\]\] is empty,
 this means the script was not evaluated. Set
 `startFailed` to true and abort these steps.

 5. Otherwise, if `script` is a [module
 script](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#module-script), then:

 1. Let `evaluationPromise` be the result of [running
 the module
 script](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#run-a-module-script) `script`, with report errors set
 to false.

 2. Assert: `evaluationPromise`.\[\[PromiseState\]\]
 is not \"pending\".

 3. If `evaluationPromise`.\[\[PromiseState\]\] is
 \"rejected\":

 1. Set `evaluationStatus` to
 [ThrowCompletion](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-throwcompletion)(`evaluationPromise`.\[\[PromiseResult\]\]).

 4. Otherwise:

 1. Set `evaluationStatus` to
 [NormalCompletion](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-normalcompletion)(undefined).

 6. If the script was aborted by the [Terminate Service
 Worker](#terminate-service-worker) algorithm, set `startFailed` to true
 and abort these steps.

 7. Set `serviceWorker`'s [start
 status](#service-worker-start-status) to `evaluationStatus`.

 8. If `script`'s [has ever been evaluated
 flag](#dfn-has-ever-been-evaluated-flag) is unset, then:

 1. For each `eventType` of
 `settingsObject`'s [global
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-global)'s associated list of [event
 listeners](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-listener)\' event types:

 1. [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#set-append) `eventType` to
 `workerGlobalScope`'s associated [service
 worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker)'s [set of event types to
 handle](#dfn-set-of-event-types-to-handle).

 If the global object's associated list of
 event listeners does not have any event listener added at
 this moment, the service worker's [set of event types to
 handle](#dfn-set-of-event-types-to-handle) remains an empty set.

 2. Set `script`'s [has ever been evaluated
 flag](#dfn-has-ever-been-evaluated-flag).

 3. Unset the `serviceWorker`'s [all fetch listeners
 are empty
 flag](#service-worker-all-fetch-listeners-are-empty-flag).

 4. The user agent may, if the [All Fetch Listeners Are
 Empty](#all-fetch-listeners-are-empty) algorithm with
 `workerGlobalScope` returns true, set
 `serviceWorker`'s [all fetch listeners are empty
 flag](#service-worker-all-fetch-listeners-are-empty-flag).

 9. Run the [responsible event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop) specified by `settingsObject` until
 it is destroyed.

 10. [Clear](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-clear) `workerGlobalScope`'s [map of active
 timers](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/timers-and-user-prompts.html#map-of-active-timers).

10. Wait for `serviceWorker` to be
 [running](#service-worker-running), or for `startFailed` to be true.

11. If `startFailed` is true, then return *failure*.

12. Return `serviceWorker`'s [start
 status](#service-worker-start-status).

### [[All Fetch Listeners Are Empty]]
Input

: `workerGlobalScope`, a [global
 object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object).

Output

: a boolean

1. If `workerGlobalScope`'s [set of event types to
 handle](#dfn-set-of-event-types-to-handle) does not
 [contain](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-contain) `fetch`, then return true.

2. Let `eventHandler` be `workerGlobalScope`'s
 [event handler
 map](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handler-map)\[\"onfetch\"\]\'s value.

3. Let `eventListenerCallbacks` be the result of calling
 [legacy-obtain service worker fetch event listener
 callbacks](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#legacy-obtain-service-worker-fetch-event-listener-callbacks) given `workerGlobalScope`.

4. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) `eventListenerCallback` of
 `eventListenerCallbacks`:

 1. Let `callback` be null.

 2. If `eventHandler` is not null and
 `eventListenerCallback` equals
 `eventHandler`'s
 [listener](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handler-listener)'s
 [callback](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#event-listener-callback), then set `callback` to the result
 of [converting to an ECMAScript
 value](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-convert-idl-to-javascript-value) `eventHandler`'s
 [value](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handler-value).

 3. Otherwise, set `callback` to the result of
 [converting to an ECMAScript
 value](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-convert-idl-to-javascript-value) `eventListenerCallback`.

 4. If
 [IsCallable](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-iscallable)(`callback`) is false, then
 return false.

 [Callback](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#event-listener-callback) objects that use
 [`handleEvent(event)`](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-eventlistener-handleevent) are assumed to be non-empty. This avoids
 calling the
 [`handleEvent(event)`](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-eventlistener-handleevent) getters, which could modify the event listeners
 during this check.

 5. If `callback`'s [function
 body](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#prod-FunctionBody) is not empty (i.e. either a
 [statement](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations) or
 [declaration](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations) exist), then return
 false.

 This detects \"`fetch`\" listeners like `() => `.
 Some sites have a fetch event listener with empty body to make them
 recognized by Chromium as a progressive web application (PWA).

5. Return true.

 User agents are encouraged to show a warning indicating
that empty \"`fetch`\" listeners are unnecessary, and may have a
negative performance impact.

### [[Terminate Service Worker]]
Input

: `serviceWorker`, a [service
 worker](#dfn-service-worker)

Output

: None

1. Run the following steps [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel) with `serviceWorker`'s main loop:

 1. Let `serviceWorkerGlobalScope` be
 `serviceWorker`'s [global
 object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object).

 2. Set `serviceWorkerGlobalScope`'s
 [closing](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#dom-workerglobalscope-closing) flag to true.

 3. [Remove](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-remove) all the
 [items](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-item) from `serviceWorker`'s [set of
 extended
 events](#dfn-set-of-extended-events).

 4. If there are any
 [tasks](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-task), whose [task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#task-source) is either the [handle fetch task
 source](#dfn-handle-fetch-task-source) or the [handle functional event task
 source](#dfn-handle-functional-event-task-source), queued in
 `serviceWorkerGlobalScope`'s [event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop)'s [task
 queues](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#task-queue),
 [queue](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) them to `serviceWorker`'s
 [containing service worker
 registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration)'s corresponding [task
 queues](#dfn-service-worker-registration-task-queue) in the same order using their original [task
 sources](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#task-source), and discard all the
 [tasks](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-task) (including
 [tasks](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-task) whose [task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#task-source) is neither the [handle fetch task
 source](#dfn-handle-fetch-task-source) nor the [handle functional event task
 source](#dfn-handle-functional-event-task-source)) from `serviceWorkerGlobalScope`'s
 [event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop)'s [task
 queues](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#task-queue) without processing them.

 This effectively means that the fetch events
 and the other functional events such as push events are backed
 up by the registration's task queues while the other tasks
 including message events are discarded.

 5. [Abort the
 script](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#abort-a-running-script) currently running in
 `serviceWorker`.

 6. Set `serviceWorker`'s [start
 status](#service-worker-start-status) to null.

### [[Handle Fetch]]
The [Handle Fetch](#handle-fetch) algorithm is the entry point for the
[fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch) handling handed to the [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker) context.

Input

: `request`, a
 [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request)

: `fetchController`, a [fetch
 controller](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller)

: `useHighResPerformanceTimers`, a boolean

Output

: a
 [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response) or [service worker timing
 info](#service-worker-timing-info)

1. Let `registration` be null.

2. Let `client` be `request`'s
 [client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-client).

3. Let `reservedClient` be `request`'s [reserved
 client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-reserved-client).

4. Let `preloadResponse` be a new
 [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects).

5. Let `workerRealm` be null.

6. Let `timingInfo` be a new [service worker timing
 info](#service-worker-timing-info).

7. Assert: `request`'s
 [destination](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-destination) is not \"`serviceworker`\".

8. If `request`'s
 [destination](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-destination) is either \"`embed`\" or \"`object`\", then:

 1. Return null.

9. Else if `request` is a [non-subresource
 request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#non-subresource-request), then:

 1. If `reservedClient` is not null and is an
 [environment settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object), then:

 1. If `reservedClient` is not a [secure
 context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#secure-context), return null.

 2. Else:

 1. If `request`'s
 [url](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url) is not a [potentially trustworthy
 URL](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-secure-contexts/#potentially-trustworthy-url), return null.

 3. If `request` is a [navigation
 request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#navigation-request) and the
 [navigation](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsing-the-web.html#navigate) triggering it was initiated with a shift+reload
 or equivalent, return null.

 4. [Assert](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-algorithm-conventions) `reservedClient` is not null.

 5. Let `storage key` be the result of running [obtain a
 storage
 key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#obtain-a-storage-key) given `reservedClient`.

 6. Set `registration` to the result of running [Match
 Service Worker
 Registration](#match-service-worker-registration) given `storage key` and
 `request`'s
 [url](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url).

 7. If `registration` is null or
 `registration`'s [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker) is null, return null.

 8. If `request`'s
 [destination](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-destination) is not
 [`"report"`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestdestination-report), set `reservedClient`'s [active
 service
 worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker) to `registration`'s [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker).

 From this point, the [service worker
 client](#dfn-service-worker-client) starts to [use](#dfn-use) its [active service
 worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker)'s [containing service worker
 registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration).

10. Else if `request` is a [subresource
 request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#subresource-request), then:

 1. If `client`'s [active service
 worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker) is non-null, set `registration` to
 `client`'s [active service
 worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker)'s [containing service worker
 registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration).

 2. Else, return null.

11. Let `activeWorker` be `registration`'s [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker).

12. Let `shouldSoftUpdate` be true if any of the following
 are true, and false otherwise:

 - `request` is a [non-subresource
 request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#non-subresource-request).

 - `request` is a [subresource
 request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#subresource-request) and `registration` is
 [stale](#service-worker-registration-stale).

13. If `activeWorker`'s [list of router
 rules](#service-worker-list-of-router-rules) [is not
 empty](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-is-empty):

 1. Set `timingInfo`'s [worker router evaluation
 start](#service-worker-timing-info-worker-router-evaluation-start) to the [coarsened shared current
 time](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dfn-coarsened-shared-current-time) given `useHighResPerformanceTimers`.

 2. Let `source` be the result of running the [Get Router
 Source](#get-router-source) algorithm with `registration`'s
 [active worker](#dfn-active-worker) and `request`.

 3. If `source` is non-null, then:

 1. Set `timingInfo`'s [worker matched router
 source](#service-worker-timing-info-worker-matched-router-source) be set to `source`, and [worker
 final router
 source](#service-worker-timing-info-worker-final-router-source) be set to
 [`"network"`](#dom-routersourceenum-network).

 2. If `source` is
 [`"network"`](#dom-routersourceenum-network):

 1. If `shouldSoftUpdate` is true, then [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel) run the [Soft
 Update](#soft-update) algorithm with
 `registration`.

 2. Return `timingInfo`.

 3. Else if `source` is
 [`"cache"`](#dom-routersourceenum-cache), or
 `source`\[\"[`cacheName`](#dom-routersourcedict-cachename)\"\]
 [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), then:

 1. If `shouldSoftUpdate` is true, then [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel) run the [Soft
 Update](#soft-update) algorithm with
 `registration`.

 2. Set `timingInfo`'s [worker cache lookup
 start](#service-worker-timing-info-worker-cache-lookup-start) to the [coarsened shared current
 time](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dfn-coarsened-shared-current-time) given
 `useHighResPerformanceTimers`.

 3. Let `environment` be null.

 4. If `request` is a [non-subresource
 request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#non-subresource-request), then:

 1. Set `environment` to
 `reservedClient`.

 5. Else:

 1. Set `environment` to `client`.

 6. Let `caches` be the result of running [obtain
 a local storage bottle
 map](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#obtain-a-local-storage-bottle-map) with `environment` and
 \"`caches`\".

 7. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-iterate) `cacheName` →
 `cache` of `caches`.

 1. If
 `source`\[\"[`cacheName`](#dom-routersourcedict-cachename)\"\]
 [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists) and
 `source`\[\"[`cacheName`](#dom-routersourcedict-cachename)\"\]
 [is](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#string-is) not `cacheName`,
 [continue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-continue).

 2. Let `requestResponses` be the result of
 running [Query
 Cache](#query-cache) with `request`, a new
 [`CacheQueryOptions`](#dictdef-cachequeryoptions), and `cache`.

 3. If `requestResponses` is an empty
 [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list), return `timingInfo`.

 4. Else:

 1. Let `requestResponse` be the first
 element of `requestResponses`.

 2. Let `response` be
 `requestResponse`'s response.

 3. Let `globalObject` be
 `activeWorker`'s [global
 object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object).

 4. If `globalObject` is null:

 1. Set `globalObject` to the result
 of running [Setup
 ServiceWorkerGlobalScope](#setup-serviceworkerglobalscope) with
 `activeWorker`.

 5. If `globalObject` is null, return
 `timingInfo`.

 This only creates a
 ServiceWorkerGlobalScope because CORS checks require
 that. It is not expected that implementations will
 actually create a ServiceWorkerGlobalScope here.

 1. If `response`'s
 [type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-type) is \"`opaque`\", and
 [cross-origin resource policy
 check](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#cross-origin-resource-policy-check) with
 `globalObject`'s
 [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin), `globalObject`,
 \"\", and `response`'s [internal
 response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-internal-response) returns **blocked**, then
 return `timingInfo`.

 2. Set `timingInfo`'s [worker final
 router
 source](#service-worker-timing-info-worker-final-router-source) be set to
 [`"cache"`](#dom-routersourceenum-cache).

 3. Set `result`'s [service worker timing
 info](#service-worker-timing-info) be set to
 `timingInfo`.

 4. Return `response`.

 8. Return `timingInfo`.

 4. Else if `source` is
 [`"race-network-and-fetch-handler"`](#dom-routersourceenum-race-network-and-fetch-handler), and `request`'s
 [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method) is \``GET`\` then:

 1. If `shouldSoftUpdate` is true, then [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel) run the [Soft
 Update](#soft-update) algorithm with
 `registration`.

 2. Let `queue` be an empty
 [queue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#queue) of [race
 result](#dfn-race-result).

 3. Let `raceFetchController` be null.

 4. Let `raceResponse` be a [race
 response](#dfn-race-response) whose
 [value](#race-response-value) is \"`pending`\".

 5. Run the following substeps [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel):

 1. If `fetchController`'s
 [state](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-state) is \"`terminated`\" or
 \"`aborted`\", then set `raceResponse` to
 a [race
 response](#dfn-race-response) whose
 [value](#race-response-value) is null, and abort these steps.

 2. Set `raceFetchController` to the result
 of calling
 [fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch) given `request`, with
 [processResponse](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#process-response) set to the following steps given a
 [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response)
 `raceNetworkRequestResponse`:

 1. Run these steps, but [abort
 when](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#abort-when) `fetchController`'s
 [state](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-state) is \"`terminated`\" or
 \"`aborted`\".

 1. Set `raceResponse`'s
 [value](#race-response-value) to
 `raceNetworkRequestResponse`.

 2. If `raceNetworkRequestResponse`'s
 [status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status) is [ok
 status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#ok-status), then:

 1. Let `raceNetworkResult` be a
 [race
 result](#dfn-race-result) whose [routed
 response](#race-result-routed-response) is
 `raceNetworkRequestResponse`
 and [used
 route](#race-result-used-route) is
 [`"network"`](#dom-routersourceenum-network).

 2. [Enqueue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#queue-enqueue)
 `raceNetworkResult` to
 `queue`.

 2. [If
 aborted](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#if-aborted) and
 `raceFetchController` is not null,
 then:

 1. [Abort](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-abort)
 `raceFetchController`.

 2. Set `raceResponse` to a [race
 response](#dfn-race-response) whose
 [value](#race-response-value) is null.

 6. Resolve `preloadResponse` with undefined.

 7. Run the following substeps [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel):

 1. Let `fetchHandlerResponse` be the result
 of [Create Fetch Event and
 Dispatch](#create-fetch-event-and-dispatch) with `request`,
 `registration`,
 `useHighResPerformanceTimers`,
 `timingInfo`, `workerRealm`,
 `reservedClient`,
 `preloadResponse`, and
 `raceResponse`.

 2. If `fetchHandlerResponse` is not null and
 not a [network
 error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error), and
 `raceFetchController` is not null,
 [abort](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-abort) `raceFetchController`.

 3. Let `raceFetchHandlerResult` be a [race
 result](#dfn-race-result) whose [routed
 response](#race-result-routed-response) is
 `fetchHandlerResponse` and [used
 route](#race-result-used-route) is
 [`"fetch-event"`](#dom-routersourceenum-fetch-event).

 4. [Enqueue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#queue-enqueue) `raceFetchHandlerResult`
 to `queue`.

 8. Wait until `queue` is not empty.

 9. Let `result` be the result of
 [dequeue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#queue-dequeue) `queue`.

 10. Let `routedResponse` be `result`'s
 [routed
 response](#race-result-routed-response).

 11. If `routedResponse` is null:

 1. Return `timingInfo`.

 12. If `result`'s [used
 route](#race-result-used-route) is
 [`"network"`](#dom-routersourceenum-network), then:

 1. Set `routedResponse`'s [service worker
 timing
 info](#service-worker-timing-info) be set to `timingInfo`.

 13. Set `routedResponse`'s [service worker timing
 info](#service-worker-timing-info)'s [worker final router
 source](#service-worker-timing-info-worker-final-router-source) be set to `result`'s [used
 route](#race-result-used-route).

 14. Return `routedResponse`.

 5. Assert: `source` is
 \"[`fetch-event`](#dom-routersourceenum-fetch-event)\"

14. Let `responseForAutoPreload` be null.

15. If `request` is a [navigation
 request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#navigation-request), `request`'s
 [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method) is \``GET`\`, `registration`'s [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker)'s [set of event types to
 handle](#dfn-set-of-event-types-to-handle)
 [contains](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-contain) `fetch`, and `registration`'s [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker)'s [all fetch listeners are empty
 flag](#service-worker-all-fetch-listeners-are-empty-flag) is not set then:

 1. If `registration`'s [navigation preload enabled
 flag](#service-worker-registration-navigation-preload-enabled-flag) is set then:

 If the above is true except
 `registration`'s [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker)'s [set of event types to
 handle](#dfn-set-of-event-types-to-handle) **does not** contain `fetch`, then the user
 agent may wish to show a console warning, as the developer's
 intent isn't clear.

 1. Let `preloadRequest` be the result of
 [cloning](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-clone) the request `request`.

 2. Let `preloadRequestHeaders` be
 `preloadRequest`'s [header
 list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list).

 3. Let `preloadResponseObject` be a new
 [`Response`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response) object associated with a new
 [`Headers`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#headers) object whose
 [guard](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-guard) is \"`immutable`\".

 4. [Append](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-append) to `preloadRequestHeaders` a new
 [header](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header) whose
 [name](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-name) is \``Service-Worker-Navigation-Preload`\`
 and
 [value](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-value) is `registration`'s [navigation
 preload header
 value](#service-worker-registration-navigation-preload-header-value).

 5. Set `preloadRequest`'s [service-workers
 mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-service-workers-mode) to \"`none`\".

 6. Let `preloadFetchController` be null.

 7. Run the following substeps [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel), but [abort
 when](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#abort-when) `fetchController`'s
 [state](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-state) is \"`terminated`\" or \"`aborted`\":

 1. Set `preloadFetchController` to the result of
 [fetching](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch) `preloadRequest`.

 To
 [processResponse](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#process-response) for
 `navigationPreloadResponse`, run these
 substeps:

 1. If `navigationPreloadResponse`'s
 [type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-type) is \"`error`\", reject
 `preloadResponse` with a `TypeError` and
 terminate these substeps.

 2. Associate `preloadResponseObject` with
 `navigationPreloadResponse`.

 3. Resolve `preloadResponse` with
 `preloadResponseObject`.

 8. [If
 aborted](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#if-aborted), then:

 1. Let `deserializedError` be the result of
 [deserialize a serialized abort
 reason](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#deserialize-a-serialized-abort-reason) given null and
 `workerRealm`.

 2. [Abort](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-abort) `preloadFetchController`
 with `deserializedError`.

 2. Else if `timingInfo`'s [worker matched router
 source](#service-worker-timing-info-worker-matched-router-source) is not
 \"[`fetch-event`](#dom-routersourceenum-fetch-event)\", a user agent may run the following substeps:

 A user agent may speculatively dispatch a
 network request in parallel with creating a fetch event in order
 to minimize the bootstrap cost.

 1. Assert: `timingInfo`'s [worker matched router
 source](#service-worker-timing-info-worker-matched-router-source) is null.

 2. Let `autoPreloadFetchController` be null.

 3. Set `responseForAutoPreload` to be a [race
 response](#dfn-race-response) whose
 [value](#race-response-value) is \"`pending`\".

 4. Run the following substeps [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel), but [abort
 when](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#abort-when) `fetchController`'s
 [state](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-state) is \"`terminated`\" or \"`aborted`\":

 1. Set `autoPreloadFetchController` to the
 result of calling
 [fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch) given `request`, with
 [processResponse](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#process-response) set to the following steps given a
 [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response)
 `autoPreloadRequestResponse`:

 1. Set `responseForAutoPreload`'s
 [value](#race-response-value) to
 `autoPreloadRequestResponse`.

 5. [If
 aborted](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#if-aborted) and `autoPreloadFetchController`
 is not null, then:

 1. [Abort](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-abort)
 `autoPreloadFetchController`.

 2. Set `responseForAutoPreload` to a [race
 response](#dfn-race-response) whose
 [value](#race-response-value) is null.

 6. Resolve `preloadResponse` with undefined.

16. Else, resolve `preloadResponse` with undefined.

17. Let `fetchResult` be the result of [Create Fetch Event
 and
 Dispatch](#create-fetch-event-and-dispatch) with `request`,
 `registration`, `useHighResPerformanceTimers`,
 `timingInfo`, `workerRealm`,
 `reservedClient`, `preloadResponse`, and
 `responseForAutoPreload`.

18. If `timingInfo`'s [worker final router
 source](#service-worker-timing-info-worker-final-router-source) is not an empty string:

 1. Assert `timingInfo`'s [worker final router
 source](#service-worker-timing-info-worker-final-router-source) is
 [`"network"`](#dom-routersourceenum-network).

 2. If `fetchResult` is null, then return
 `timingInfo`.

 3. Else:

 1. Assert `fetchResult`'s [service worker timing
 info](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response-service-worker-timing-info)'s [worker final router
 source](#service-worker-timing-info-worker-final-router-source) is
 [`"network"`](#dom-routersourceenum-network).

 2. Set `fetchResult`'s [service worker timing
 info](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response-service-worker-timing-info)'s [worker final router
 source](#service-worker-timing-info-worker-final-router-source) to
 [`"fetch-event"`](#dom-routersourceenum-fetch-event).

19. Return `fetchResult`.

### [[Create Fetch Event and Dispatch]]
Input

: `request`, a
 [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request)

: `registration`, a [service worker
 registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration)

: `useHighResPerformanceTimers`, a boolean

: `timingInfo`, a [service worker timing
 info](#service-worker-timing-info)

: `workerRealm`, a [relevant
 realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm) of the [global
 object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object)

: `reservedClient`, a [reserved
 client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-reserved-client)

: `preloadResponse`, a
 [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects)

: `raceResponse`, a [race
 response](#dfn-race-response) or null

Output

: a
 [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response) or null

1. Let `response` be null.

2. Let `eventCanceled` be false.

3. Let `client` be `request`'s
 [client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-client).

4. Let `activeWorker` be `registration`'s [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker).

5. Let `eventHandled` be null.

6. Let `handleFetchFailed` be false.

7. Let `respondWithEntered` be false.

8. Let `networkError` be a [network
 error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error).

9. If `raceResponse` is not null:

 1. Set `networkError`'s [service worker timing
 info](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response-service-worker-timing-info) to `timingInfo`.

10. Let `shouldSoftUpdate` be true if any of the following
 are true, and false otherwise:

 - `request` is a [non-subresource
 request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#non-subresource-request).

 - `request` is a [subresource
 request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#subresource-request) and `registration` is
 [stale](#service-worker-registration-stale).

11. If the result of running the [Should Skip
 Event](#should-skip-event) algorithm with \"fetch\" and
 `activeWorker` is true, then:

 1. If `shouldSoftUpdate` is true, then [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel) run the [Soft
 Update](#soft-update)
 algorithm with `registration`.

 2. Return null.

12. If `activeWorker`'s [all fetch listeners are empty
 flag](#service-worker-all-fetch-listeners-are-empty-flag) is set:

 1. [In
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel):

 1. If `activeWorker`'s
 [state](#dfn-state) is
 \"activating\", then wait for `activeWorker`'s
 [state](#dfn-state) to
 become \"activated\".

 2. Run the [Run Service
 Worker](#run-service-worker) algorithm with `activeWorker`.

 3. If `shouldSoftUpdate` is true, then run the [Soft
 Update](#soft-update)
 algorithm with `registration`.

 2. Return null.

13. If `useHighResPerformanceTimers` is true, then set
 `useHighResPerformanceTimers` to
 `activeWorker`'s [global
 object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object)'s [cross-origin isolated
 capability](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#concept-workerglobalscope-cross-origin-isolated-capability).

14. Let `timingInfo`'s [start
 time](#service-worker-timing-info-start-time) be the [coarsened shared current
 time](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dfn-coarsened-shared-current-time) given `useHighResPerformanceTimers`.

15. If `activeWorker`'s
 [state](#dfn-state) is
 \"`activating`\", wait for `activeWorker`'s
 [state](#dfn-state) to become
 \"`activated`\".

16. If the result of running the [Run Service
 Worker](#run-service-worker) algorithm with `activeWorker` is
 *failure*, then set `handleFetchFailed` to true.

17. Else:

 1. Set `workerRealm` to the [relevant
 realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm) of the `activeWorker`'s [global
 object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object).

 2. Set `eventHandled` to [a new
 promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise) in `workerRealm`.

 3. If `raceResponse` is not null,
 [set](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-set) `activeWorker`'s [global
 object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object)'s [race response
 map](#serviceworkerglobalscope-race-response-map)\[`request`\] to
 `raceResponse`.

 4. [Queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) `task` to run the following
 substeps:

 1. Let `e` be the result of [creating an
 event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-create) with
 [`FetchEvent`](#fetchevent).

 2. Let `abortController` be a
 [new](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#new)
 [`AbortController`](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#abortcontroller) object with `workerRealm`.

 3. Let `requestObject` be the result of
 [creating](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-create) a
 [`Request`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request) object, given `request`, a new
 [`Headers`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#headers) object's
 [guard](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-guard) which is \"`immutable`\",
 `abortController`'s
 [signal](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#abortcontroller-signal), and `workerRealm`.

 4. Initialize `e`'s
 [`type`](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-event-type) attribute to
 [`fetch`](#service-worker-global-scope-fetch-event).

 5. Initialize `e`'s
 [`cancelable`](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-event-cancelable) attribute to true.

 6. Initialize `e`'s
 [`request`](#dom-fetchevent-request) attribute to `requestObject`.

 7. Initialize `e`'s
 [`preloadResponse`](#dom-fetchevent-preloadresponse) to `preloadResponse`.

 8. Initialize `e`'s
 [`clientId`](#dom-fetchevent-clientid) attribute to `client`'s
 [id](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-id).

 9. If `request` is a [non-subresource
 request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#non-subresource-request) and `request`'s
 [destination](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-destination) is not
 [`"report"`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestdestination-report), initialize `e`'s
 [`resultingClientId`](#dom-fetchevent-resultingclientid) attribute to `reservedClient`'s
 [id](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-id), and to the empty string otherwise.

 10. If `request` is a [navigation
 request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#navigation-request), initialize `e`'s
 [`replacesClientId`](#dom-fetchevent-replacesclientid) attribute to `request`'s
 [replaces client
 id](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-replaces-client-id), and to the empty string otherwise.

 11. Initialize `e`'s
 [`handled`](#dom-fetchevent-handled) to `eventHandled`.

 12. Let `timingInfo`'s [fetch event dispatch
 time](#service-worker-timing-info-fetch-event-dispatch-time) to the [coarsened shared current
 time](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dfn-coarsened-shared-current-time) given
 `useHighResPerformanceTimers`.

 13. [Dispatch](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-dispatch) `e` at
 `activeWorker`'s [global
 object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object).

 14. Invoke [Update Service Worker Extended Events
 Set](#update-service-worker-extended-events-set) with `activeWorker` and
 `e`.

 15. If `e`'s [respond-with entered
 flag](#fetchevent-respond-with-entered-flag) is set, set `respondWithEntered`
 to true.

 16. If `e`'s [wait to respond
 flag](#fetchevent-wait-to-respond-flag) is set, then:

 1. Wait until `e`'s [wait to respond
 flag](#fetchevent-wait-to-respond-flag) is unset.

 2. If `e`'s [respond-with error
 flag](#fetchevent-respond-with-error-flag) is set, set
 `handleFetchFailed` to true.

 3. Else, set `response` to `e`'s
 [potential
 response](#fetchevent-potential-response).

 17. If `response` is null, `request`'s
 [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body) is not null, and `request`'s
 [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body)'s
 [source](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-source) is null, then:

 1. If `request`'s
 [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body) is
 [unusable](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#body-unusable), set `handleFetchFailed` to
 true.

 2. Else,
 [cancel](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-cancel) `request`'s
 [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body) with undefined.

 18. If `response` is not null, then set
 `response`'s [service worker timing
 info](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response-service-worker-timing-info) to `timingInfo`.

 19. If `e`'s [canceled
 flag](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#canceled-flag) is set, set `eventCanceled` to
 true.

 20. If `fetchController`
 [state](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-state) is \"`terminated`\" or \"`aborted`\", then:

 1. Let `deserializedError` be the result of
 [deserialize a serialized abort
 reason](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#deserialize-a-serialized-abort-reason) given `fetchController`'s
 [serialized abort
 reason](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-serialized-abort-reason) and `workerRealm`.

 2. [Queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) to [signal
 abort](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#abortcontroller-signal-abort) on `abortController` with
 `deserializedError`.

 If `task` is discarded, set
 `handleFetchFailed` to true.

 The `task` *must* use `activeWorker`'s
 [event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop) and the [handle fetch task
 source](#dfn-handle-fetch-task-source).

18. Wait for `task` to have executed or for
 `handleFetchFailed` to be true.

19. If `shouldSoftUpdate` is true, then [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel) run the [Soft
 Update](#soft-update)
 algorithm with `registration`.

20. If `activeWorker`'s [global
 object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object)'s [race response
 map](#serviceworkerglobalscope-race-response-map)\[`request`\]
 [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists),
 [remove](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-remove) `activeWorker`'s [global
 object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object)'s [race response
 map](#serviceworkerglobalscope-race-response-map)\[`request`\].

21. If `respondWithEntered` is false, then:

 1. If `eventCanceled` is true, then:

 1. If `eventHandled` is not null, then
 [reject](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#reject) `eventHandled` with a
 \"[`NetworkError`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#networkerror)\"
 [`DOMException`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException) in `workerRealm`.

 2. Return `networkError`.

 2. If `eventHandled` is not null, then
 [resolve](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#resolve) `eventHandled`.

 3. If `raceResponse` is not null, and
 `raceResponse`'s
 [value](#race-response-value) is not null, then:

 1. Wait until `raceResponse`'s
 [value](#race-response-value) is not \"`pending`\".

 2. If `raceResponse`'s
 [value](#race-response-value) is a
 [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response), return `raceResponse`'s
 [value](#race-response-value).

 4. Return null.

22. If `handleFetchFailed` is true, then:

 1. If `eventHandled` is not null, then
 [reject](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#reject) `eventHandled` with a
 \"[`NetworkError`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#networkerror)\"
 [`DOMException`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException) in `workerRealm`.

 2. Return `networkError`.

23. If `eventHandled` is not null, then
 [resolve](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#resolve) `eventHandled`.

24. Return `response`.

### [[Parse URL Pattern]]
Input

: `rawPattern`, a
 [`URLPatternCompatible`](https://urlpattern.spec.whatwg.org/#typedefdef-urlpatterncompatible)

: `serviceWorker`, a [service
 worker](#dfn-service-worker)

Output

: A [URL
 pattern](https://urlpattern.spec.whatwg.org/#url-pattern)

1. Let `baseURL` be `serviceWorker`'s [script
 url](#dfn-script-url).

2. Return the result of [building a URL pattern from a Web IDL
 value](https://urlpattern.spec.whatwg.org/#build-a-url-pattern-from-a-web-idl-value) `rawPattern` given `baseURL`.

### [[Verify Router Condition]]
Input

: `condition`, a
 [`RouterCondition`](#dictdef-routercondition)

: `serviceWorker`, a [service
 worker](#dfn-service-worker)

Output

: a boolean

1. Let `hasCondition` be false.

2. If
 `condition`\[\"[`urlPattern`](#dom-routercondition-urlpattern)\"\]
 [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), then:

 1. Let `rawPattern` be
 `condition`\[\"[`urlPattern`](#dom-routercondition-urlpattern)\"\].

 2. Let `pattern` be the result of running the [Parse URL
 Pattern](#parse-url-pattern) algorithm passing `rawPattern` and
 `serviceWorker`. If this throws an exception, catch
 it and return false.

 3. If `pattern` [has regexp
 groups](https://urlpattern.spec.whatwg.org/#url-pattern-has-regexp-groups), then return false.

 Since running a user-defined regular expression
 has a security concern, it is prohibited.

 4. Set `hasCondition` to true.

3. If
 `condition`\[\"[`requestMethod`](#dom-routercondition-requestmethod)\"\]
 [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists):

 1. Let `method` be
 `condition`\[\"[`requestMethod`](#dom-routercondition-requestmethod)\"\].

 2. If `method` is not a
 [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-method), then return false.

 3. If `method` is a [forbidden
 method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#forbidden-method), then return false.

 4. Set `hasCondition` to true.

4. If
 `condition`\[\"[`requestMode`](#dom-routercondition-requestmode)\"\]
 [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), set `hasCondition` to true.

5. If
 `condition`\[\"[`requestDestination`](#dom-routercondition-requestdestination)\"\]
 [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), set `hasCondition` to true.

6. If
 `condition`\[\"[`runningStatus`](#dom-routercondition-runningstatus)\"\]
 [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), set `hasCondition` to true.

7. If
 `condition`\[\"[`_or`](#dom-routercondition-or)\"\]
 [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), then:

 1. If `hasCondition` is true, return false.

 For ease of understanding the router rule, the
 \"or\" condition is mutually exclusive with other conditions.

 2. Let `orConditions` be
 `condition`\[\"[`_or`](#dom-routercondition-or)\"\].

 3. For each `orCondition` of `orConditions`:

 1. If running the [Verify Router
 Condition](#verify-router-condition) algorithm with `orCondition` and
 `serviceWorker` returns false, return false.

 4. Set `hasCondition` to true.

8. If
 `condition`\[\"[`not`](#dom-routercondition-not)\"\]
 [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), then:

 1. If `hasCondition` is true, return false.

 For ease of understanding the router rule, the
 \"not\" condition is mutually exclusive with other conditions.

 2. If running the [Verify Router
 Condition](#verify-router-condition) algorithm with
 `condition`\[\"[`not`](#dom-routercondition-not)\"\] and `serviceWorker` returns
 false, return false.

 3. Set `hasCondition` to true.

9. Return `hasCondition`.

### [[Match Router Condition]]
Input

: `condition`, a
 [`RouterCondition`](#dictdef-routercondition)

: `serviceWorker`, a [service
 worker](#dfn-service-worker)

: `request`, a
 [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request)

Output

: a boolean

 if there are multiple conditions (e.g. `urlPattern`,
`runningStatus`, and `requestMethod` are set), all conditions need to
match for true to be returned.

1. If
 `condition`\[\"[`or`](#dom-routercondition-or)\"\]
 [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), then:

 1. Let `orConditions` be
 `condition`\[\"[`or`](#dom-routercondition-or)\"\].

 2. For each `orCondition` of `orConditions`:

 1. If running the [Match Router
 Condition](#match-router-condition) algorithm with `orCondition`,
 `serviceWorker` and `request` returns
 true, then return true.

 3. Return false.

2. If
 `condition`\[\"[`not`](#dom-routercondition-not)\"\]
 [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), then:

 1. If running the [Match Router
 Condition](#match-router-condition) algorithm with
 `condition`\[\"[`not`](#dom-routercondition-not)\"\], `serviceWorker` and
 `request` returns true, then return false.

 2. Return true.

3. Else:

 The [Verify Router
 Condition](#verify-router-condition) algorithm guarantees that
 [`or`](#dom-routercondition-or),
 [`not`](#dom-routercondition-not), and other conditions are mutually exclusive.

 1. If
 `condition`\[\"[`urlPattern`](#dom-routercondition-urlpattern)\"\]
 [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), then:

 1. Let `rawPattern` be
 `condition`\[\"[`urlPattern`](#dom-routercondition-urlpattern)\"\].

 2. Let `pattern` be the result of running the [Parse
 URL Pattern](#parse-url-pattern) algorithm passing `rawPattern`
 and `serviceWorker`.

 3. If running
 [match](https://urlpattern.spec.whatwg.org/#url-pattern-match) with `pattern` and
 `request`'s
 [URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url) returns null, return false.

 2. If
 `condition`\[\"[`requestMethod`](#dom-routercondition-requestmethod)\"\]
 [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), then:

 1. Let `method` be
 `condition`\[\"[`requestMethod`](#dom-routercondition-requestmethod)\"\].

 2. [Normalize](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-method-normalize) `method`.

 3. If `request`'s
 [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method) is not `method`, return false.

 3. If
 `condition`\[\"[`requestMode`](#dom-routercondition-requestmode)\"\]
 [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), then:

 1. Let `mode` be
 `condition`\[\"[`requestMode`](#dom-routercondition-requestmode)\"\].

 2. If `request`'s
 [mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-mode) is not `mode`, return false.

 4. If
 `condition`\[\"[`requestDestination`](#dom-routercondition-requestdestination)\"\]
 [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), then:

 1. Let `destination` be
 `condition`\[\"[`requestDestination`](#dom-routercondition-requestdestination)\"\].

 2. If `request`'s
 [destination](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-destination) is not `destination`, return
 false.

 5. If
 `condition`\[\"[`runningStatus`](#dom-routercondition-runningstatus)\"\]
 [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), then:

 1. Let `runningStatus` be
 `condition`\[\"[`runningStatus`](#dom-routercondition-runningstatus)\"\].

 2. If `runningStatus` is
 [`"running"`](#dom-runningstatus-running), and `serviceWorker` is not
 [running](#service-worker-running), return false.

 3. If `runningStatus` is
 [`"not-running"`](#dom-runningstatus-not-running), and `serviceWorker` is
 [running](#service-worker-running), return false.

 6. Return true.

### [[Check Router Registration Limit]]
Input

: `routerRules`, a [list of router
 rules](#service-worker-list-of-router-rules)

Output

: a boolean

 Router conditions can be complex and nested using
[`_or`](#dom-routercondition-or) and
[`not`](#dom-routercondition-not). To prevent excessive processing, this algorithm
introduces two limits. First, the total number of conditions, counting
all nested conditions, cannot exceed 1024. Second, the nesting depth is
limited to 10 levels to avoid exponential computation.

1. Let `result` be a [count router condition
 result](#dfn-count-router-condition-result).

2. Set `result`'s [condition
 count](#dfn-count-router-condition-result-condition-count) to 1024.

3. Set `result`'s [quota
 exceeded](#dfn-dfn-count-router-condition-result-quota-exceeded) to false.

4. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) `rule` of `routerRules`:

 1. Set `result` to be the result of running [Count
 Router Inner
 Conditions](#count-router-inner-conditions) with
 `rule`\[\"[`condition`](#dom-routerrule-condition)\"\], `result`, and 10.

 2. If `result`'s [quota
 exceeded](#dfn-dfn-count-router-condition-result-quota-exceeded) is true, return false.

5. Return true.

### [[Count Router Inner Conditions]]
Input

: `condition`, a
 [`RouterCondition`](#dictdef-routercondition)

: `result`, a [count router condition
 result](#dfn-count-router-condition-result)

: `depth`, a number

Output

: `result`, a [count router condition
 result](#dfn-count-router-condition-result)

1. Decrement `result`'s [condition
 count](#dfn-count-router-condition-result-condition-count) by one.

2. If `result`'s [condition
 count](#dfn-count-router-condition-result-condition-count) is zero, or `depth` is zero, then:

 1. Set `result`'s [quota
 exceeded](#dfn-dfn-count-router-condition-result-quota-exceeded) to be true.

 2. Return `result`.

3. If
 `condition`\[\"[`_or`](#dom-routercondition-or)\"\]
 [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), then:

 1. Decrement `depth` by one.

 2. For each `orCondition` of
 `condition`\[\"[`_or`](#dom-routercondition-or)\"\]:

 1. Set `result` to be the result of running [Count
 Router Inner
 Conditions](#count-router-inner-conditions) with `orCondition`,
 `result`, and `depth`.

 2. If `result`'s [quota
 exceeded](#dfn-dfn-count-router-condition-result-quota-exceeded) is true, return `result`.

4. Else if
 `condition`\[\"[`not`](#dom-routercondition-not)\"\]
 [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), then:

 1. Decrement `depth` by one.

 2. Set `result` to be the result of running [Count
 Router Inner
 Conditions](#count-router-inner-conditions) with
 `condition`\[\"[`not`](#dom-routercondition-not)\"\], `result`, and
 `depth`.

 3. If `result`'s [quota
 exceeded](#dfn-dfn-count-router-condition-result-quota-exceeded) is true, return `result`.

5. Return `result`.

### [[Get Router Source]]
Input

: `serviceWorker`, a [service
 worker](#dfn-service-worker)

: `request`, a
 [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request)

Output

: [`RouterSource`](#typedefdef-routersource) or null

1. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) `rule` of `serviceWorker`'s
 [list of router
 rules](#service-worker-list-of-router-rules):

 1. If running the [Match Router
 Condition](#match-router-condition) algorithm with
 `rule`\[\"[`condition`](#dom-routerrule-condition)\"\], `serviceWorker` and
 `request` returns true, then return
 `rule`\[\"[`source`](#dom-routerrule-source)\"\].

2. Return null.

### [[Should Skip Event]]
Input

: `eventName`, a string

: `serviceWorker`, a [service
 worker](#dfn-service-worker)

Output

: a boolean

 To avoid unnecessary delays, this specification permits
skipping event dispatch when no event listeners for the event have been
deterministically added in the service worker's global during the very
first script execution.

1. If `serviceWorker`'s [set of event types to
 handle](#dfn-set-of-event-types-to-handle) does not
 [contain](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-contain) `eventName`, then the user agent *may*
 return true.

2. Return false.

### [[Fire Functional Event]]
Input

: `eventName`, a string

: `eventConstructor`, an event constructor that extends
 [`ExtendableEvent`](#extendableevent)

: `registration`, a [service worker
 registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration)

: `initialization`, optional property initialization for
 `event`, constructed from `eventConstructor`

: `postDispatchSteps`, optional steps to run on the [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker)'s event loop, with `dispatchedEvent` set
 to the instance of `eventConstructor` that was
 [dispatched](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-dispatch).

Output

: None

1. Assert: `registration`'s [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker) is not null.

2. Let `activeWorker` be `registration`'s [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker).

3. If the result of running [Should Skip
 Event](#should-skip-event) with `eventName` and
 `activeWorker` is true, then:

 1. If `registration` is
 [stale](#service-worker-registration-stale), then [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel) run the [Soft
 Update](#soft-update)
 algorithm with `registration`.

 2. Return.

4. If `activeWorker`'s
 [state](#dfn-state) is
 \"`activating`\", wait for `activeWorker`'s
 [state](#dfn-state) to become
 \"`activated`\".

5. If the result of running the [Run Service
 Worker](#run-service-worker) algorithm with `activeWorker` is
 *failure*, then:

 1. If `registration` is
 [stale](#service-worker-registration-stale), then [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel) run the [Soft
 Update](#soft-update)
 algorithm with `registration`.

 2. Return.

6. [Queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) `task` to run these substeps:

 1. Let `event` be the result of [creating an
 event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-create) with `eventConstructor` and the
 [relevant
 realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm) of `activeWorker`'s [global
 object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object).

 2. If `initialization` is not null, then initialize
 `event` with `initialization`.

 3. [Dispatch](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-dispatch) `event` on
 `activeWorker`'s [global
 object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object).

 4. Invoke [Update Service Worker Extended Events
 Set](#update-service-worker-extended-events-set) with `activeWorker` and
 `event`.

 5. If `postDispatchSteps` is not null, then run
 `postDispatchSteps` passing `event` as
 `dispatchedEvent`.

 The `task` *must* use `activeWorker`'s [event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop) and the [handle functional event task
 source](#dfn-handle-functional-event-task-source).

7. Wait for `task` to have executed or been discarded.

8. If `registration` is
 [stale](#service-worker-registration-stale), then [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel) run the [Soft
 Update](#soft-update)
 algorithm with `registration`.

To fire an \"`amazingthing`\" event
(which is of type `AmazingThingEvent`) on a particular
`serviceWorkerRegistration`, and initialize the event
object's properties, the prose would be:

1. [Fire Functional
 Event](#fire-functional-event) \"`amazingthing`\" using `AmazingThingEvent` on
 `serviceWorkerRegistration` with the following
 properties:

 propertyName

 : value

 anotherPropertyName

 : anotherValue

 Then run the following steps with `dispatchedEvent`:

 1. Do whatever you need to with `dispatchedEvent` on the
 service worker's event loop.

Note that the initialization steps and post-dispatch steps are optional.
If they aren't needed, the prose would be:

1. [Fire Functional
 Event](#fire-functional-event) \"`whatever`\" using
 [`ExtendableEvent`](#extendableevent) on `serviceWorkerRegistration`.

### [[Handle Service Worker Client Unload]]
The user agent *must* run these steps as part of when a [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client) unloads via [unloading document cleanup
steps](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-lifecycle.html#unloading-document-cleanup-steps) or
[termination](#terminate-service-worker).

Input

: `client`, a [service worker
 client](#dfn-service-worker-client)

Output

: None

1. Run the following steps atomically.

2. Let `registration` be the [service worker
 registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration) [used](#dfn-use)
 by `client`.

3. If `registration` is null, abort these steps.

4. If any other [service worker
 client](#dfn-service-worker-client) is [using](#dfn-use) `registration`, abort these steps.

5. If `registration` is
 [unregistered](#dfn-service-worker-registration-unregistered), invoke [Try Clear
 Registration](#try-clear-registration) with `registration`.

6. Invoke [Try Activate](#try-activate) with `registration`.

### [[Handle User Agent Shutdown]]
Input

: None

Output

: None

1. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) `registration` of [registration
 map](#dfn-scope-to-registration-map)'s
 [values](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-getting-the-values):

 1. If `registration`'s [installing
 worker](#dfn-installing-worker) is not null, then:

 1. If `registration`'s [waiting
 worker](#dfn-waiting-worker) is null and `registration`'s
 [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker) is null, invoke [Clear
 Registration](#clear-registration) with `registration` and continue
 to the next iteration of the loop.

 2. Else, set `registration`'s [installing
 worker](#dfn-installing-worker) to null.

 2. If `registration`'s [waiting
 worker](#dfn-waiting-worker) is not null, then [in
 parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel):

 1. Invoke [Activate](#activate) with `registration`.

### [[Update Service Worker Extended Events Set]]
Input

: `worker`, a [service
 worker](#dfn-service-worker)

: `event`, an
 [event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event)

Output

: None

1. Assert: `event`'s [dispatch
 flag](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#dispatch-flag) is unset.

2. For each `item` of `worker`'s [set of extended
 events](#dfn-set-of-extended-events):

 1. If `item` is not
 [active](#extendableevent-active),
 [remove](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-remove) `item` from `worker`'s
 [set of extended
 events](#dfn-set-of-extended-events).

3. If `event` is
 [active](#extendableevent-active),
 [append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#set-append) `event` to `worker`'s [set of
 extended
 events](#dfn-set-of-extended-events).

### [[Unregister]]
Input

: `job`, a [job](#dfn-job)

Output

: none

1. If the
 [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin) of `job`'s [scope
 url](#dfn-job-scope-url) is not `job`'s
 [client](#dfn-job-client)'s
 [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin), then:

 1. Invoke [Reject Job
 Promise](#reject-job-promise) with `job` and
 \"[`SecurityError`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#securityerror)\"
 [`DOMException`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException).

 2. Invoke [Finish Job](#finish-job) with `job` and abort these steps.

2. Let `registration` be the result of running [Get
 Registration](#get-registration) given `job`'s [storage
 key](#job-storage-key)
 and `job`'s [scope
 url](#dfn-job-scope-url).

3. If `registration` is null, then:

 1. Invoke [Resolve Job
 Promise](#resolve-job-promise) with `job` and false.

 2. Invoke [Finish Job](#finish-job) with `job` and abort these steps.

4. [Remove](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-remove) [registration
 map](#dfn-scope-to-registration-map)\[(`registration`'s [storage
 key](#service-worker-registration-storage-key), `job`'s [scope
 url](#dfn-job-scope-url))\].

5. Invoke [Resolve Job
 Promise](#resolve-job-promise) with `job` and true.

6. Invoke [Try Clear
 Registration](#try-clear-registration) with `registration`.

 If [Try Clear
 Registration](#try-clear-registration) does not trigger [Clear
 Registration](#clear-registration) here, [Clear
 Registration](#clear-registration) is tried again when the last client
 [using](#dfn-use) the
 registration is
 [unloaded](#handle-service-worker-client-unload) or the [extend lifetime
 promises](#extendableevent-extend-lifetime-promises) for the registration's service workers settle.

7. Invoke [Finish Job](#finish-job) with `job`.

### [[Set Registration]]
Input

: `storage key`, a [storage
 key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#storage-key)

: `scope`, a
 [URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url)

: `updateViaCache`, an [update via cache
 mode](#dfn-update-via-cache)

Output

: `registration`, a [service worker
 registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration)

1. Run the following steps atomically.

2. Let `scopeString` be
 [serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer) `scope` with the *exclude fragment flag*
 set.

3. Let `registration` be a new [service worker
 registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration) whose [storage
 key](#service-worker-registration-storage-key) is set to `storage key`, [scope
 url](#dfn-scope-url) is
 set to `scope`, and [update via cache
 mode](#dfn-update-via-cache) is set to `updateViaCache`.

4. [Set](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-set) [registration
 map](#dfn-scope-to-registration-map)\[(`storage key`,
 `scopeString`)\] to `registration`.

5. Return `registration`.

### [[Clear Registration]]
Input

: `registration`, a [service worker
 registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration)

Output

: None

1. Run the following steps atomically.

2. If `registration`'s [installing
 worker](#dfn-installing-worker) is not null, then:

 1. [Terminate](#terminate-service-worker) `registration`'s [installing
 worker](#dfn-installing-worker).

 2. Run the [Update Worker
 State](#update-worker-state) algorithm passing `registration`'s
 [installing
 worker](#dfn-installing-worker) and \"`redundant`\" as the arguments.

 3. Run the [Update Registration
 State](#update-registration-state) algorithm passing `registration`,
 \"`installing`\" and null as the arguments.

3. If `registration`'s [waiting
 worker](#dfn-waiting-worker) is not null, then:

 1. [Terminate](#terminate-service-worker) `registration`'s [waiting
 worker](#dfn-waiting-worker).

 2. Run the [Update Worker
 State](#update-worker-state) algorithm passing `registration`'s
 [waiting
 worker](#dfn-waiting-worker) and \"`redundant`\" as the arguments.

 3. Run the [Update Registration
 State](#update-registration-state) algorithm passing `registration`,
 \"`waiting`\" and null as the arguments.

4. If `registration`'s [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker) is not null, then:

 1. [Terminate](#terminate-service-worker) `registration`'s [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker).

 2. Run the [Update Worker
 State](#update-worker-state) algorithm passing `registration`'s
 [active worker](#dfn-active-worker) and \"`redundant`\" as the arguments.

 3. Run the [Update Registration
 State](#update-registration-state) algorithm passing `registration`,
 \"`active`\" and null as the arguments.

### [[Try Clear Registration]]
Input

: `registration`, a [service worker
 registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration)

Output

: None

1. Invoke [Clear
 Registration](#clear-registration) with `registration` if no [service
 worker
 client](#dfn-service-worker-client) is [using](#dfn-use) `registration` and all of the following
 conditions are true:

 - `registration`'s [installing
 worker](#dfn-installing-worker) is null or the result of running [Service Worker
 Has No Pending
 Events](#service-worker-has-no-pending-events) with `registration`'s [installing
 worker](#dfn-installing-worker) is true.

 - `registration`'s [waiting
 worker](#dfn-waiting-worker) is null or the result of running [Service Worker
 Has No Pending
 Events](#service-worker-has-no-pending-events) with `registration`'s [waiting
 worker](#dfn-waiting-worker) is true.

 - `registration`'s [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker) is null or the result of running [Service Worker
 Has No Pending
 Events](#service-worker-has-no-pending-events) with `registration`'s [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker) is true.

### [[Update Registration State]]
Input

: `registration`, a [service worker
 registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration)

: `target`, a string (one of \"`installing`\",
 \"`waiting`\", and \"`active`\")

: `source`, a [service
 worker](#dfn-service-worker) or null

Output

: None

1. Let `registrationObjects` be an array containing all the
 [`ServiceWorkerRegistration`](#serviceworkerregistration) objects associated with `registration`.

2. If `target` is \"`installing`\", then:

 1. Set `registration`'s [installing
 worker](#dfn-installing-worker) to `source`.

 2. For each `registrationObject` in
 `registrationObjects`:

 1. [Queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) to set the
 [`installing`](#dom-serviceworkerregistration-installing) attribute of
 `registrationObject` to null if
 `registration`'s [installing
 worker](#dfn-installing-worker) is null, or the result of [getting the
 service worker
 object](#get-the-service-worker-object) that represents `registration`'s
 [installing
 worker](#dfn-installing-worker) in `registrationObject`'s
 [relevant settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object).

3. Else if `target` is \"`waiting`\", then:

 1. Set `registration`'s [waiting
 worker](#dfn-waiting-worker) to `source`.

 2. For each `registrationObject` in
 `registrationObjects`:

 1. [Queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) to set the
 [`waiting`](#dom-serviceworkerregistration-waiting) attribute of
 `registrationObject` to null if
 `registration`'s [waiting
 worker](#dfn-waiting-worker) is null, or the result of [getting the
 service worker
 object](#get-the-service-worker-object) that represents `registration`'s
 [waiting
 worker](#dfn-waiting-worker) in `registrationObject`'s
 [relevant settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object).

4. Else if `target` is \"`active`\", then:

 1. Set `registration`'s [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker) to `source`.

 2. For each `registrationObject` in
 `registrationObjects`:

 1. [Queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) to set the
 [`active`](#dom-serviceworkerregistration-active) attribute of
 `registrationObject` to null if
 `registration`'s [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker) is null, or the result of [getting the
 service worker
 object](#get-the-service-worker-object) that represents `registration`'s
 [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker) in `registrationObject`'s
 [relevant settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object).

 The
 [task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-task) *must* use `registrationObject`'s
 [relevant settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object)'s [responsible event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop) and the [DOM manipulation task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source).

### [[Update Worker State]]
Input

: `worker`, a [service
 worker](#dfn-service-worker)

: `state`, a [service
 worker](#dfn-service-worker) [state](#dfn-state)

Output

: None

1. Assert: `state` is not \"`parsed`\".

 \"`parsed`\" is the initial state. A [service
