 worker](#dfn-service-worker) is never updated to this state.

2. Set `worker`'s [state](#dfn-state) to `state`.

3. Let `settingsObjects` be all [environment settings
 objects](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object) whose
 [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin) is `worker`'s [script
 url](#dfn-script-url)'s
 [origin](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-origin).

4. For each `settingsObject` of
 `settingsObjects`, [queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) on `settingsObject`'s [responsible event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop) in the [DOM manipulation task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source) to run the following steps:

 1. Let `objectMap` be `settingsObject`'s
 [service worker object
 map](#environment-settings-object-service-worker-object-map).

 2. If `objectMap`\[`worker`\] does not
 [exist](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), then abort these steps.

 3. Let `workerObj` be
 `objectMap`\[`worker`\].

 4. Set `workerObj`'s
 [`state`](#dom-serviceworker-state) to `state`.

 5. [Fire an
 event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-fire) named
 [`statechange`](#eventdef-serviceworker-statechange) at `workerObj`.

### [[Notify Controller Change]]
Input

: `client`, a [service worker
 client](#dfn-service-worker-client)

Output

: None

1. Assert: `client` is not null.

2. If `client` is an [environment settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object), [queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) to [fire an
 event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-fire) named `controllerchange` at the
 [`ServiceWorkerContainer`](#serviceworkercontainer) object that `client` is
 [associated](#serviceworkercontainer-service-worker-client) with.

The
[task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-task) *must* use `client`'s [responsible event
loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop) and the [DOM manipulation task
source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source).

### [[Match Service Worker Registration]]
Input

: `storage key`, a [storage
 key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#storage-key)

: `clientURL`, a
 [URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url)

Output

: A [service worker
 registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration)

1. Run the following steps atomically.

2. Let `clientURLString` be
 [serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer) `clientURL`.

3. Let `matchingScopeString` be the empty string.

4. Let `scopeStringSet` be an empty list.

5. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) (`entry storage key`,
 `entry scope`) of [registration
 map](#dfn-scope-to-registration-map)'s
 [keys](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-getting-the-keys):

 1. If `storage key`
 [equals](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#storage-key-equal) `entry storage key`, then
 [append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append) `entry scope` to the end of
 `scopeStringSet`.

6. Set `matchingScopeString` to the longest value in
 `scopeStringSet` which the value of
 `clientURLString` starts with, if it exists.

 The URL string matching in this step is
 prefix-based rather than path-structural. E.g. a client URL string
 with \"https://example.com/prefix-of/resource.html\" will match a
 registration for a scope with \"https://example.com/prefix\". The
 URL string comparison is safe for the same-origin security as
 HTTP(S) URLs are always
 [serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer) with a trailing slash at the end of the origin part
 of the URLs.

7. Let `matchingScope` be null.

8. If `matchingScopeString` is not the empty string, then:

 1. Set `matchingScope` to the result of
 [parsing](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-parser) `matchingScopeString`.

 2. Assert: `matchingScope`'s
 [origin](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-origin) and `clientURL`'s
 [origin](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-origin) are [same
 origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#same-origin).

9. Return the result of running [Get
 Registration](#get-registration) given `storage key` and
 `matchingScope`.

### [[Get Registration]]
Input

: `storage key`, a [storage
 key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#storage-key)

: `scope`, a
 [URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url)

Output

: A [service worker
 registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration)

1. Run the following steps atomically.

2. Let `scopeString` be the empty string.

3. If `scope` is not null, set `scopeString` to
 [serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer) `scope` with the *exclude fragment flag*
 set.

4. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-iterate) (`entry storage key`,
 `entry scope`) → `registration` of
 [registration
 map](#dfn-scope-to-registration-map):

 1. If `storage key`
 [equals](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#storage-key-equal) `entry storage key` and
 `scopeString` matches `entry scope`, then
 return `registration`.

5. Return null.

### [[Get Newest Worker]]
Input

: `registration`, a [service worker
 registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration)

Output

: `newestWorker`, a [service
 worker](#dfn-service-worker)

1. Run the following steps atomically.

2. Let `newestWorker` be null.

3. If `registration`'s [installing
 worker](#dfn-installing-worker) is not null, set `newestWorker` to
 `registration`'s [installing
 worker](#dfn-installing-worker).

4. Else if `registration`'s [waiting
 worker](#dfn-waiting-worker) is not null, set `newestWorker` to
 `registration`'s [waiting
 worker](#dfn-waiting-worker).

5. Else if `registration`'s [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker) is not null, set `newestWorker` to
 `registration`'s [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker).

6. Return `newestWorker`.

### [[Service Worker Has No Pending Events]]
Input

: `worker`, a [service
 worker](#dfn-service-worker)

Output

: True or false, a boolean

1. For each `event` of `worker`'s [set of
 extended
 events](#dfn-set-of-extended-events):

 1. If `event` is
 [active](#extendableevent-active), return false.

2. Return true.

### [[Create Client]]
Input

: `client`, a [service worker
 client](#dfn-service-worker-client)

Output

: `clientObject`, a
 [`Client`](#client) object

1. Let `clientObject` be a new
 [`Client`](#client) object.

2. Set `clientObject`'s [service worker
 client](#dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client) to `client`.

3. Return `clientObject`.

### [[Create Window Client]]
Input

: `client`, a [service worker
 client](#dfn-service-worker-client)

: `frameType`, a string

: `visibilityState`, a string

: `focusState`, a boolean

: `ancestorOriginsList`, a list

Output

: `windowClient`, a
 [`WindowClient`](#windowclient) object

1. Let `windowClient` be a new
 [`WindowClient`](#windowclient) object.

2. Set `windowClient`'s [service worker
 client](#dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client) to `client`.

3. Set `windowClient`'s [frame
 type](#dfn-service-worker-client-frame-type) to `frameType`.

4. Set `windowClient`'s [visibility
 state](#dfn-service-worker-client-visibilitystate) to `visibilityState`.

5. Set `windowClient`'s [focus
 state](#dfn-service-worker-client-focusstate) to `focusState`.

6. Set `windowClient`'s [ancestor origins
 array](#windowclient-ancestor-origins-array) to a [frozen
 array](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-frozen-array-type) created from `ancestorOriginsList`.

7. Return `windowClient`.

### [[Get Frame Type]]
Input

: `navigable`, a
 [navigable](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#navigable)

Output

: `frameType`, a string

1. If `navigable`'s
 [parent](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-parent) is not null, then return \"`nested`\".

2. If `navigable`'s [active browsing
 context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-bc) is an [auxiliary browsing
 context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#auxiliary-browsing-context), then return \"`auxiliary`\".

3. Return \"`top-level`\".

### [[Resolve Get Client Promise]]
Input

: `client`, a [service worker
 client](#dfn-service-worker-client)

: `promise`, a
 [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects)

Output

: none

1. If `client` is an [environment settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object), then:

 1. If `client` is not a [secure
 context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#secure-context), [queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) to reject `promise` with a
 \"[`SecurityError`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#securityerror)\"
 [`DOMException`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException), on `promise`'s [relevant settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object)'s [responsible event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop) using the [DOM manipulation task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source), and abort these steps.

2. Else:

 1. If `client`'s [creation
 URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-creation-url) is not a [potentially trustworthy
 URL](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-secure-contexts/#potentially-trustworthy-url), [queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) to reject `promise` with a
 \"[`SecurityError`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#securityerror)\"
 [`DOMException`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException), on `promise`'s [relevant settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object)'s [responsible event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop) using the [DOM manipulation task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source), and abort these steps.

3. If `client` is an [environment settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object) and is not a [window
 client](#dfn-window-client), then:

 1. Let `clientObject` be the result of running [Create
 Client](#create-client)
 algorithm with `client` as the argument.

 2. [Queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) to resolve `promise` with
 `clientObject`, on `promise`'s [relevant
 settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object)'s [responsible event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop) using the [DOM manipulation task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source), and abort these steps.

4. Else:

 1. Let `browsingContext` be null.

 2. If `client` is an [environment settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object), set `browsingContext` to
 `client`'s [global
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-global)'s [browsing
 context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#browsing-context).

 3. Else, set `browsingContext` to `client`'s
 [target browsing
 context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-target-browsing-context).

 4. Let `navigable` be the
 [navigable](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#navigable) with a [active browsing
 context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-bc) of `browsingContext`.

 5. [Queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) to run the following steps on
 `browsingContext`'s [event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop) using the [user interaction task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#user-interaction-task-source):

 1. Let `frameType` be the result of running [Get
 Frame Type](#get-frame-type) with `navigable`.

 2. Let `visibilityState` be
 `browsingContext`'s [active
 document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document)'s
 [`visibilityState`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#page-visibility) attribute value.

 3. Let `focusState` be the result of running the
 [has focus
 steps](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#has-focus-steps) with `browsingContext`'s [active
 document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document) as the argument.

 4. Let `ancestorOriginsList` be the empty list.

 5. If `client` is a [window
 client](#dfn-window-client), set `ancestorOriginsList` to
 `browsingContext`'s [active
 document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document)'s [relevant global
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-global)'s
 [`Location`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#location) object's [ancestor origins
 list](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#concept-location-ancestor-origins-list)'s associated list.

 6. [Queue a
 task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task) to run the following steps on
 `promise`'s [relevant settings
 object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object)'s [responsible event
 loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop) using the [DOM manipulation task
 source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source):

 1. If `client`'s [discarded
 flag](#service-worker-client-discarded-flag) is set, resolve `promise`
 with undefined and abort these steps.

 2. Let `windowClient` be the result of running
 [Create Window
 Client](#create-window-client) with `client`,
 `frameType`, `visibilityState`,
 `focusState`, and
 `ancestorOriginsList`.

 3. Resolve `promise` with
 `windowClient`.

### [[Query Cache]]
Input

: `requestQuery`, a
 [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request)

: `options`, a
 [`CacheQueryOptions`](#dictdef-cachequeryoptions) object, optional

: `targetStorage`, a [request response
 list](#dfn-request-response-list), optional

Output

: `resultList`, a [request response
 list](#dfn-request-response-list)

1. Let `resultList` be an empty
 [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list).

2. Let `storage` be null.

3. If the optional argument `targetStorage` is omitted, set
 `storage` to the [relevant request response
 list](#dfn-relevant-request-response-list).

4. Else, set `storage` to `targetStorage`.

5. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) `requestResponse` of
 `storage`:

 1. Let `cachedRequest` be `requestResponse`'s
 request.

 2. Let `cachedResponse` be
 `requestResponse`'s response.

 3. If [Request Matches Cached
 Item](#request-matches-cached-item) with `requestQuery`,
 `cachedRequest`, `cachedResponse`, and
 `options` returns true, then:

 1. Let `requestCopy` be a copy of
 `cachedRequest`.

 2. Let `responseCopy` be a copy of
 `cachedResponse`.

 3. Add `requestCopy`/`responseCopy` to
 `resultList`.

6. Return `resultList`.

### [[Request Matches Cached Item]]
Input

: `requestQuery`, a
 [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request)

: `request`, a
 [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request)

: `response`, a
 [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response) or null, optional, defaulting to null

: `options`, a
 [`CacheQueryOptions`](#dictdef-cachequeryoptions) object, optional

Output

: a boolean

1. If
 `options`\[\"[`ignoreMethod`](#dom-cachequeryoptions-ignoremethod)\"\] is false and `request`'s
 [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method) is not \``GET`\`, return false.

2. Let `queryURL` be `requestQuery`'s
 [url](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url).

3. Let `cachedURL` be `request`'s
 [url](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url).

4. If
 `options`\[\"[`ignoreSearch`](#dom-cachequeryoptions-ignoresearch)\"\] is true, then:

 1. Set `cachedURL`'s
 [query](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-query) to the empty string.

 2. Set `queryURL`'s
 [query](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-query) to the empty string.

5. If `queryURL` does not
 [equal](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-equals) `cachedURL` with the *exclude fragment
 flag* set, then return false.

6. If `response` is null,
 `options`\[\"[`ignoreVary`](#dom-cachequeryoptions-ignorevary)\"\] is true, or `response`'s [header
 list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list) does not
 [contain](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#header-list-contains) \``Vary`\`, then return true.

7. Let `fieldValues` be the
 [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list) containing the elements corresponding to the
 [field-values](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7230#section-3.2) of the
 [Vary](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7231#section-7.1.4) header for the
 [value](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-value) of the
 [header](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header) with
 [name](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-name) \``Vary`\`.

8. For each `fieldValue` in `fieldValues`:

 1. If `fieldValue` matches \"`*`\", or the [combined
 value](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-combine) given `fieldValue` and
 `request`'s [header
 list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list) does not match the [combined
 value](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-combine) given `fieldValue` and
 `requestQuery`'s [header
 list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list), then return false.

9. Return true.

### [[Batch Cache Operations]]
Input

: `operations`, a
 [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list) of [cache batch
 operation](#dfn-cache-batch-operation) objects

Output

: `resultList`, a [request response
 list](#dfn-request-response-list)

1. Let `cache` be the [relevant request response
 list](#dfn-relevant-request-response-list).

2. Let `backupCache` be a new [request response
 list](#dfn-request-response-list) that is a copy of `cache`.

3. Let `addedItems` be an empty
 [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list).

4. Try running the following substeps atomically:

 1. Let `resultList` be an empty
 [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list).

 2. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) `operation` in
 `operations`:

 1. If `operation`'s
 [type](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-type) matches neither \"`delete`\" nor \"`put`\",
 [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) a `TypeError`.

 2. If `operation`'s
 [type](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-type) matches \"`delete`\" and
 `operation`'s
 [response](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-response) is not null,
 [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) a `TypeError`.

 3. If the result of running [Query
 Cache](#query-cache)
 with `operation`'s
 [request](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-request), `operation`'s
 [options](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-options), and `addedItems` [is not
 empty](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-is-empty),
 [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) an
 \"[`InvalidStateError`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#invalidstateerror)\"
 [`DOMException`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException).

 4. Let `requestResponses` be an empty
 [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list).

 5. If `operation`'s
 [type](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-type) matches \"`delete`\", then:

 1. Set `requestResponses` to the result of
 running [Query
 Cache](#query-cache) with `operation`'s
 [request](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-request) and `operation`'s
 [options](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-options).

 2. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) `requestResponse` in
 `requestResponses`:

 1. [Remove](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-remove) the
 [item](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-item) whose value matches
 `requestResponse` from
 `cache`.

 6. Else if `operation`'s
 [type](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-type) matches \"`put`\", then:

 1. If `operation`'s
 [response](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-response) is null,
 [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) a `TypeError`.

 2. Let `r` be `operation`'s
 [request](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-request)'s associated
 [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request).

 3. If `r`'s
 [url](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url)'s
 [scheme](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-scheme) is not one of \"`http`\" and
 \"`https`\",
 [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) a `TypeError`.

 4. If `r`'s
 [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method) is not \``GET`\`,
 [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) a `TypeError`.

 5. If `operation`'s
 [options](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-options) is not null,
 [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) a `TypeError`.

 6. Set `requestResponses` to the result of
 running [Query
 Cache](#query-cache) with `operation`'s
 [request](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-request).

 7. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) `requestResponse` of
 `requestResponses`:

 1. [Remove](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-remove) the
 [item](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-item) whose value matches
 `requestResponse` from
 `cache`.

 8. [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append) `operation`'s
 [request](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-request)/`operation`'s
 [response](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-response) to `cache`.

 9. If the cache write operation in the previous two steps
 failed due to exceeding the granted quota limit,
 [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) a
 [`QuotaExceededError`](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#quotaexceedederror).

 10. [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append) `operation`'s
 [request](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-request)/`operation`'s
 [response](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-response) to `addedItems`.

 7. [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append) `operation`'s
 [request](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-request)/`operation`'s
 [response](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-response) to `resultList`.

 3. Return `resultList`.

5. And then, if an exception was
 [thrown](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw), then:

 1. [Remove](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-remove) all the
 [items](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-item) from the [relevant request response
 list](#dfn-relevant-request-response-list).

 2. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) `requestResponse` of
 `backupCache`:

 1. [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append) `requestResponse` to the
 [relevant request response
 list](#dfn-relevant-request-response-list).

 3. [Throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) the exception.

 When an exception is
 [thrown](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw), the implementation does undo (roll back) any
 changes made to the cache storage during the batch operation job.

### [[Is Async Module]]
Input

: `record`, a [Module
 Record](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-abstract-module-records)

: `moduleMap`, a [module
 map](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#module-map)

: `base`, a
 [URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url)

: `seen`, a
 [set](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-set) of
 [URLs](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url)

Output

: a boolean

1. If `record` is not a [Cyclic Module
 Record](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-cyclic-module-records), then:

 1. Return false.

2. If `record`.\[\[Async\]\] is true, then:

 1. Return true.

3. [For
 each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) string `requested` of
 `record`.\[\[RequestedModules\]\]:

 1. Let `url` be the result of [resolving a module
 specifier](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#resolve-a-module-specifier) given `base` and
 `requested`.

 2. Assert: `url` is never failure, because [resolving a
 module
 specifier](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#resolve-a-module-specifier) must have been previously successful with these
 same two arguments.

 3. If `seen` does not
 [contain](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-contain) `url`, then:

 1. [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#set-append) `url` to `seen`.

 2. If `moduleMap`\[`url`\] does not have
 a
 [record](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-script-record), then:

 1. Return false.

 3. If [Is Async
 Module](#is-async-module) for
 `moduleMap`\[`url`\]\'s
 [record](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-script-record), `moduleMap`, `base`,
 and `seen` is true, then:

 1. Return true.

4. Return false.

### [[Lookup Race Response]]
Input

: `request`, a
 [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request)

Output

: a
 [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response) or null

1. Let `registration` be null.

2. If `request` is a [non-subresource
 request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#non-subresource-request), then:

 1. If `request`'s [reserved
 client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-reserved-client) is null, return null.

 2. Let `storage key` be the result of running [obtain a
 storage
 key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#obtain-a-storage-key) given `request`'s [reserved
 client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-reserved-client).

 3. Set `registration` to the result of running [Match
 Service Worker
 Registration](#match-service-worker-registration) given `storage key` and
 `request`'s
 [url](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url).

3. Else if `request` is a [subresource
 request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#subresource-request), then:

 1. Let `client` be `request`'s
 [client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-client).

 2. If `client`'s [active service
 worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker) is null, return null.

 3. Set `registration` to `client`'s [active
 service
 worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker)'s [containing service worker
 registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration).

4. Otherwise, return null.

5. Let `activeWorker` be `registration`'s [active
 worker](#dfn-active-worker).

6. Let `map` be `activeWorker`'s [global
 object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object)'s [race response
 map](#serviceworkerglobalscope-race-response-map).

7. If `map`\[`request`\]
 [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), then:

 1. Let `entry` be
 `map`\[`request`\].

 2. Wait until `entry`'s
 [value](#race-response-value) is not \"`pending`\"

 3. If `entry`'s
 [value](#race-response-value) is
 [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response), return `entry`'s
 [value](#race-response-value).

8. Return null.

## [Appendix B: Extended HTTP headers]
### [Service Worker Script Request]
An HTTP request to
[fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch) a [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker)'s [script
resource](#dfn-script-resource) will include the following
[header](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header):

\`[`Service-Worker`]\`

: Indicates this request is a [service
 worker](#dfn-service-worker)'s [script
 resource](#dfn-script-resource) request.

 This header helps administrators log the requests
 and detect threats.

### [Service Worker Script Response]
An HTTP response to a [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker)'s [script
resource](#dfn-script-resource) request can include the following
[header](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header):

\`[`Service-Worker-Allowed`]\`

: Indicates the user agent will override the path restriction, which
 limits the maximum allowed [scope
 url](#dfn-scope-url) that
 the script can [control](#dfn-control), to the given value.

 The value is a URL. If a relative URL is given, it
 is parsed against the script's URL.

Default scope:

``` highlight
// Maximum allowed scope defaults to the path the script sits in
// "/js/" in this example
navigator.serviceWorker.register("/js/sw.js").then(() => {
 console.log("Install succeeded with the default scope '/js/'.");
});
```

Upper path without
Service-Worker-Allowed header:

``` highlight
// Set the scope to an upper path of the script location
// Response has no Service-Worker-Allowed header
navigator.serviceWorker.register("/js/sw.js", { scope: "/" }).catch(() => {
 console.error("Install failed due to the path restriction violation.");
});
```

Upper path with Service-Worker-Allowed
header:

``` highlight
// Set the scope to an upper path of the script location
// Response included "Service-Worker-Allowed : /"
navigator.serviceWorker.register("/js/sw.js", { scope: "/" }).then(() => {
 console.log("Install succeeded as the max allowed scope was overriden to '/'.");
});
```

A path restriction voliation even with
Service-Worker-Allowed header:

``` highlight
// Set the scope to an upper path of the script location
// Response included "Service-Worker-Allowed : /foo"
navigator.serviceWorker.register("/foo/bar/sw.js", { scope: "/" }).catch(() => {
 console.error("Install failed as the scope is still out of the overriden maximum allowed scope.");
});
```

### [Syntax]
[ABNF](#biblio-rfc5234 "Augmented BNF for Syntax Specifications: ABNF")
for the values of the headers used by the [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker)'s [script
resource](#dfn-script-resource) requests and responses:

 Service-Worker = %x73.63.72.69.70.74 ; "script", case-sensitive

 The validation of the Service-Worker-Allowed header's
values is done by URL parsing algorithm (in Update algorithm) instead of
using ABNF.
